// ── §3 PkBytes wrapper (manual serde) ────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Debug)]
pub struct PkBytes(pub [u8; 32]);

impl Default for PkBytes {
    fn default() -> Self {
        PkBytes([0u8; 32])
    }
}

// Manual Serialize: write u32 length prefix then the 32 bytes.
impl Serialize for PkBytes {
    fn serialize<S>(&self, serializer: S) -> std::result::Result<S::Ok, S::Error>
    where
        S: Serializer,
    {

        // Serialize as a tuple of (u32, [u8; 32]) to ensure length prefix
        let mut tup = serializer.serialize_tuple(2)?;
        tup.serialize_element(&(32u32))?;
        tup.serialize_element(&self.0)?;
        tup.end()
    }
}

// Manual Deserialize: read u32 length, assert == 32, then read 32 bytes.
impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for PkBytes {
    fn deserialize<D>(deserializer: D) -> std::result::Result<Self, D::Error>
    where
        D: Deserializer<'de>,
    {
        struct PkBytesVisitor;

        impl<'de> Visitor<'de> for PkBytesVisitor {
            type Value = PkBytes;

            fn expecting(&self, formatter: &mut std::fmt::Formatter) -> std::fmt::Result {
                write!(formatter, "a tuple of (u32 length, byte array of length 32)")
            }

            fn visit_seq<A>(self, mut seq: A) -> std::result::Result<Self::Value, A::Error>
            where
                A: SeqAccess<'de>,
            {
                let len: u32 = seq
                    .next_element()?
                    .ok_or_else(|| de::Error::invalid_length(0, &self))?;
                if len != 32 {
                    return Err(de::Error::custom(format!("expected length 32, got {}", len)));
                }
                let bytes: [u8; 32] = seq
                    .next_element()?
                    .ok_or_else(|| de::Error::invalid_length(1, &self))?;
                Ok(PkBytes(bytes))
            }
        }

        deserializer.deserialize_seq(PkBytesVisitor)
    }
}

// ── §4 LingXuError ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Error, Debug, Clone)]
pub enum LingXuError {
    #[error("I/O error: {0}")]
    Io(String),
    #[error("Storage error: {0}")]
    Storage(String),
    #[error("Entry not found at index {0}")]
    EntryNotFound(EntryIndex),
    #[error("Decode error: {0}")]
    DecodeError(String),
    #[error("Ledger error: {0}")]
    LedgerError(String),
    #[error("Serialization error: {0}")]
    Serialization(String),
    #[error("Verification failed")]
    VerificationFailed,
    #[error("Chain verification failed")]
    ChainVerificationFailed,
    #[error("Consensus error: {0}")]
    ConsensusError(String),
    #[error("Permission denied: {0}")]
    PermissionDenied(String),
    #[error("Ethics denied: {0:?}")]
    EthicsDenied(Option<String>),
    #[error("HNSW error: {0}")]
    HnswError(String),
    #[error("Dimension mismatch: expected {expected}, got {got}")]
    DimensionMismatch { expected: usize, got: usize },
    #[error("ID not found: {0}")]
    IdNotFound(String),
    #[error("Entry is sealed")]
    EntrySealed,
    #[error("Invalid entry type")]
    InvalidEntryType,
    #[error("Invalid signature")]
    InvalidSignature,
}

impl From<std::io::Error> for LingXuError {
    fn from(e: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        LingXuError::Io(e.to_string())
    }
}

impl From<serde_json::Error> for LingXuError {
    fn from(e: serde_json::Error) -> Self {
        LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string())
    }
}

impl LingXuError {
    /// Returns true if the error is recoverable (client may retry).
    pub fn is_recoverable(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self,
            LingXuError::Io(_) |
            LingXuError::Storage(_) |
            LingXuError::DecodeError(_) |
            LingXuError::Serialization(_)
        )
    }
}

// ── §6 MetricType (8 distance metrics) ───────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum MetricType {
    L2,
    Euclidean,
    Cosine,
    Manhattan,
    DotProduct,
    InnerProduct,
    Hamming,
    Jaccard,
}

impl Default for MetricType {
    fn default() -> Self {
        MetricType::L2
    }
}

impl MetricType {
    /// Compute distance between two vectors according to the selected metric.
    /// Returns `f32::MAX` if dimensions do not match.
    pub fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        if a.len() != b.len() {
            return f32::MAX;
        }
        if a.is_empty() {
            return 0.0;
        }

        match self {
            MetricType::L2 | MetricType::Euclidean => {
                let sum_sq: f32 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| {
                    let d = x - y;
                    d * d
                }).sum();
                sum_sq.sqrt()
            }
            MetricType::Cosine => {
                let dot: f32 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum();
                let norm_a: f32 = a.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
                let norm_b: f32 = b.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
                if norm_a == 0.0 || norm_b == 0.0 {
                    return 1.0;
                }
                let sim = dot / (norm_a * norm_b);
                // Clamp similarity to [-1, 1] then convert to distance [0, 2]
                let sim_clamped = sim.clamp(-1.0, 1.0);
                (1.0 - sim_clamped).clamp(0.0, 2.0)
            }
            MetricType::Manhattan => {
                a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| (x - y).abs()).sum()
            }
            MetricType::DotProduct | MetricType::InnerProduct => {
                let dot: f32 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum();
                -dot
            }
            MetricType::Hamming => {
                let mismatches: usize = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).filter(|(x, y)| {
                    // Treat as unequal if the values differ (threshold-based for float)
                    (**x - **y).abs() > f32::EPSILON
                }).count();
                mismatches as f32 / a.len() as f32
            }
            MetricType::Jaccard => {
                let mut intersection = 0usize;
                let mut union = 0usize;
                for (x, y) in a.iter().zip(b.iter()) {
                    let x_is_nonzero = x.abs() > f32::EPSILON;
                    let y_is_nonzero = y.abs() > f32::EPSILON;
                    if x_is_nonzero && y_is_nonzero {
                        intersection += 1;
                    }
                    if x_is_nonzero || y_is_nonzero {
                        union += 1;
                    }
                }
                if union == 0 {
                    0.0
                } else {
                    1.0 - (intersection as f32 / union as f32)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ── §7 EntryType (16 variants) ───────────────────────────────────────────────

#[repr(u8)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EntryType {
    Event = 0,
    Observation = 1,
    Action = 2,
    Intention = 3,
    Belief = 4,
    Goal = 5,
    Milestone = 6,
    Dream = 7,
    RepairLog = 8,
    CuriosityProbe = 9,
    ConsensusVote = 10,
    NegotiationVote = 11,
    MetaLearningUpdate = 12,
    ForkMarker = 13,
    MetaOp = 14,
    Story = 15,
}

impl EntryType {
    pub fn as_u8(&self) -> u8 {
        *self as u8
    }

    pub fn from_u8(v: u8) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        match v {
            0 => Ok(EntryType::Event),
            1 => Ok(EntryType::Observation),
            2 => Ok(EntryType::Action),
            3 => Ok(EntryType::Intention),
            4 => Ok(EntryType::Belief),
            5 => Ok(EntryType::Goal),
            6 => Ok(EntryType::Milestone),
            7 => Ok(EntryType::Dream),
            8 => Ok(EntryType::RepairLog),
            9 => Ok(EntryType::CuriosityProbe),
            10 => Ok(EntryType::ConsensusVote),
            11 => Ok(EntryType::NegotiationVote),
            12 => Ok(EntryType::MetaLearningUpdate),
            13 => Ok(EntryType::ForkMarker),
            14 => Ok(EntryType::MetaOp),
            15 => Ok(EntryType::Story),
            _ => Err(LingXuError::InvalidEntryType),
        }
    }
}

// ── §8 Affect (7-dimensional PAD+TSC model) ──────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct Affect {
    pub valence: f64,
    pub arousal: f64,
    pub dominance: f64,
    pub trust: f64,
    pub curiosity: f64,
    pub surprise: f64,
    pub confirmation: f64,
}

impl Default for Affect {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Affect {
            valence: 0.0,
            arousal: 0.5,
            dominance: 0.5,
            trust: 0.5,
            curiosity: 0.5,
            surprise: 0.0,
            confirmation: 1.0,
        }
    }
}

impl Affect {
    /// Euclidean distance between two affect vectors in 7D space.
    pub fn distance(&self, other: &Affect) -> f64 {
        let d0 = self.valence - other.valence;
        let d1 = self.arousal - other.arousal;
        let d2 = self.dominance - other.dominance;
        let d3 = self.trust - other.trust;
        let d4 = self.curiosity - other.curiosity;
        let d5 = self.surprise - other.surprise;
        let d6 = self.confirmation - other.confirmation;
        (d0 * d0 + d1 * d1 + d2 * d2 + d3 * d3 + d4 * d4 + d5 * d5 + d6 * d6).sqrt()
    }

    /// Produce a deterministic 64-bit hash from the 7 f64 fields.
    /// Uses FNV-1a inspired bit mixing over to_bits() of each component.
    pub fn hash64(&self) -> u64 {
        const FNV_OFFSET: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325;
        const FNV_PRIME: u64 = 0x100000001b3;

        let bits = [
            self.valence.to_bits(),
            self.arousal.to_bits(),
            self.dominance.to_bits(),
            self.trust.to_bits(),
            self.curiosity.to_bits(),
            self.surprise.to_bits(),
            self.confirmation.to_bits(),
        ];

        let mut hash = FNV_OFFSET;
        for chunk in &bits {
            let bytes = chunk.to_le_bytes();
            for b in &bytes {
                hash ^= (*b) as u64;
                hash = hash.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);
            }
        }
        hash
    }
}

// ── §11 AuditOp ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum AuditOp {
    Read,
    Write,
    Modify,
    Delete,
}

// ── §12 AuditEntry ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AuditEntry {
    pub op: AuditOp,
    pub id: u64,
    pub detail: String,
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
}

// ── §13 审计之痕 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub struct 审计之痕 {
    entries: PLMutex<VecDeque<AuditEntry>>,
    max_size: usize,
}

impl 审计之痕 {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        审计之痕 {
            entries: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(10000)),
            max_size: 10000,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Self {
        审计之痕 {
            entries: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(capacity)),
            max_size: capacity,
        }
    }

    /// Record a new audit entry. Evicts oldest if at capacity.
    pub fn record(&self, op: AuditOp, id: u64, detail: &str) {
        let entry = AuditEntry {
            op,
            id,
            detail: detail.to_string(),
            timestamp: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };
        let mut guard = self.entries.lock();
        guard.push_back(entry);
        if guard.len() > self.max_size {
            guard.pop_front();
        }
    }

    /// Query entries matching the specified operation type.
    pub fn query(&self, op: AuditOp) -> Vec<AuditEntry> {
        self.entries
            .lock()
            .iter()
            .filter(|e| e.op == op)
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    /// Dump all entries in chronological order.
    pub fn dump(&self) -> Vec<AuditEntry> {
        self.entries.lock().iter().cloned().collect()
    }

    /// Count total recorded entries.
    pub fn count(&self) -> usize {
        self.entries.lock().len()
    }
}

impl Default for 审计之痕 {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

}

// ── §15 Utility functions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub fn now_timestamp_ms() -> TimestampMs {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap()
        .as_millis()
}

pub fn bytes_to_hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{:02x}", b)).collect()
}

pub fn random_bytes_32() -> [u8; 32] {
    let mut rng = thread_rng();
    let mut b = [0u8; 32];
    rng.fill_bytes(&mut b);
    b
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §9  幽识场 trait — 抽象距离计算接口
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 意识场 trait：将距离计算提升到"场论"层面的哲学级抽象。
///
/// 每个 幽识场 实例定义了一个度规空间，支持多种距离度量
/// （L2、Cosine、Manhattan 等），并提供归一化、点积、L2距离等核心运算。
pub trait 幽识场: Send + Sync {
    /// 计算两个向量在给定度规下的距离。
    fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32;

    /// 向量维度。
    fn dimension(&self) -> usize;

    /// 当前场使用的度规类型。
    fn metric(&self) -> MetricType;

    /// 对向量进行 L2 归一化（原地修改）。
    fn normalize(&self, vec: &mut [f32]);

    /// 计算两个向量的点积。
    fn dot_product(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32;

    /// 计算两个向量之间的 L2 距离平方（避免开方，更快）。
    fn l2_distance_squared(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32;

    /// 场的名称标识。
    fn field_name(&self) -> &'static str;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §10  Scalar幽识场 — 纯 Rust 标量实现
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 纯 Rust 标量实现的意识场，零外部依赖，全平台通用。
pub struct Scalar幽识场 {
    dimension: usize,
    metric: MetricType,
}

impl Scalar幽识场 {
    /// 创建一个新的标量意识场。
    pub fn new(dimension: usize, metric: MetricType) -> Self {
        Self { dimension, metric }
    }
}

impl 幽识场 for Scalar幽识场 {
    fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        self.metric.distance(a, b)
    }

    fn dimension(&self) -> usize {
        self.dimension
    }

    fn metric(&self) -> MetricType {
        self.metric
    }

    fn normalize(&self, vec: &mut [f32]) {
        if vec.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let mut norm_sq = 0.0f32;
        for &v in vec.iter() {
            norm_sq += v * v;
        }
        let norm = norm_sq.sqrt();
        if norm > 0.0 {
            for v in vec.iter_mut() {
                *v /= norm;
            }
        }
    }

    fn dot_product(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        let len = a.len().min(b.len());
        let mut sum = 0.0f32;
        for i in 0..len {
            sum += a[i] * b[i];
        }
        sum
    }

    fn l2_distance_squared(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        let len = a.len().min(b.len());
        let mut sum = 0.0f32;
        for i in 0..len {
            let diff = a[i] - b[i];
            sum += diff * diff;
        }
        sum
    }

    fn field_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "scalar"
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §11  SimdX86幽识场 — x86 SIMD 加速实现
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// x86/x86_64 平台上的 SIMD 加速意识场。
///
/// 运行时检测 CPU 特性，优先使用 AVX-512，次选 AVX2，回退到标量实现。
pub struct SimdX86幽识场 {
    dimension: usize,
    metric: MetricType,
    has_avx512: bool,
    has_avx2: bool,
}

impl SimdX86幽识场 {
    /// 创建一个新的 SIMD 加速意识场，自动进行运行时 CPU 特性检测。
    #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
    pub fn new(dimension: usize, metric: MetricType) -> Self {
        let has_avx512 = is_x86_feature_detected!("avx512f");
        let has_avx2 = is_x86_feature_detected!("avx2");
        Self {
            dimension,
            metric,
            has_avx512,
            has_avx2,
        }
    }

    /// 在非 x86 平台上，回退到标量实现（但仍然提供兼容接口）。
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "x86_64"))]
    pub fn new(dimension: usize, metric: MetricType) -> Self {
        Self {
            dimension,
            metric,
            has_avx512: false,
            has_avx2: false,
        }
    }

    /// 返回当前检测到的最优实现名称。
    fn effective_backend(&self) -> &'static str {
        if self.has_avx512 {
            "avx512"
        } else if self.has_avx2 {
            "avx2"
        } else {
            "scalar_fallback"
        }
    }
}

/// 标量辅助函数：dot_product（供 SIMD 回退使用）
#[inline(always)]
fn scalar_dot(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    let len = a.len().min(b.len());
    let mut sum = 0.0f32;
    for i in 0..len {
        sum += a[i] * b[i];
    }
    sum
}

/// 标量辅助函数：l2_distance_squared（供 SIMD 回退使用）
#[inline(always)]
fn scalar_l2_sq(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    let len = a.len().min(b.len());
    let mut sum = 0.0f32;
    for i in 0..len {
        let diff = a[i] - b[i];
        sum += diff * diff;
    }
    sum
}

impl 幽识场 for SimdX86幽识场 {
    fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        self.metric.distance(a, b)
    }

    fn dimension(&self) -> usize {
        self.dimension
    }

    fn metric(&self) -> MetricType {
        self.metric
    }

    fn normalize(&self, vec: &mut [f32]) {
        if vec.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let norm_sq = self.dot_product(vec, vec);
        let norm = norm_sq.sqrt();
        if norm > 0.0 {
            for v in vec.iter_mut() {
                *v /= norm;
            }
        }
    }

    #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
    fn dot_product(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        if self.has_avx512 {
            unsafe { dot_avx512(a, b) }
        } else if self.has_avx2 {
            unsafe { dot_avx2(a, b) }
        } else {
            scalar_dot(a, b)
        }
    }

    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "x86_64"))]
    fn dot_product(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        scalar_dot(a, b)
    }

    #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
    fn l2_distance_squared(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        if self.has_avx512 {
            unsafe { l2_sq_avx512(a, b) }
        } else if self.has_avx2 {
            unsafe { l2_sq_avx2(a, b) }
        } else {
            scalar_l2_sq(a, b)
        }
    }

    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "x86_64"))]
    fn l2_distance_squared(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        scalar_l2_sq(a, b)
    }

    fn field_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        self.effective_backend()
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §11a  AVX2 内联实现 (256-bit = 8 × f32)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
#[target_feature(enable = "avx2")]
unsafe fn dot_avx2(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    use std::arch::x86_64::*;

    let len = a.len().min(b.len());
    let mut sum = 0.0f32;
    let mut i = 0usize;

    // 每次处理 8 个 f32（256-bit YMM 寄存器）
    while i + 8 <= len {
        let va = _mm256_loadu_ps(a.as_ptr().add(i));
        let vb = _mm256_loadu_ps(b.as_ptr().add(i));
        let prod = _mm256_mul_ps(va, vb);
        // 水平求和：将 256-bit 寄存器的 8 个元素累加
        let h = _mm256_hadd_ps(prod, prod);
        let h2 = _mm256_hadd_ps(h, h);
        // 提取低 128-bit 和高 128-bit 的结果
        let low = _mm256_castps256_ps128(h2);
        let high = _mm256_extractf128_ps(h2, 1);
        let result = _mm_add_ss(low, high);
        sum += std::mem::transmute::<__m128, [f32; 4]>(result)[0];
        i += 8;
    }

    // 处理剩余不足 8 个的元素
    while i < len {
        sum += a[i] * b[i];
        i += 1;
    }

    sum
}

#[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
#[target_feature(enable = "avx2")]
unsafe fn l2_sq_avx2(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    use std::arch::x86_64::*;

    let len = a.len().min(b.len());
    let mut sum = 0.0f32;
    let mut i = 0usize;

    while i + 8 <= len {
        let va = _mm256_loadu_ps(a.as_ptr().add(i));
        let vb = _mm256_loadu_ps(b.as_ptr().add(i));
        let diff = _mm256_sub_ps(va, vb);
        let sq = _mm256_mul_ps(diff, diff);
        let h = _mm256_hadd_ps(sq, sq);
        let h2 = _mm256_hadd_ps(h, h);
        let low = _mm256_castps256_ps128(h2);
        let high = _mm256_extractf128_ps(h2, 1);
        let result = _mm_add_ss(low, high);
        sum += std::mem::transmute::<__m128, [f32; 4]>(result)[0];
        i += 8;
    }

    while i < len {
        let diff = a[i] - b[i];
        sum += diff * diff;
        i += 1;
    }

    sum
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §11b  AVX-512 内联实现 (512-bit = 16 × f32)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
#[target_feature(enable = "avx512f")]
unsafe fn dot_avx512(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    use std::arch::x86_64::*;

    let len = a.len().min(b.len());
    let mut acc = _mm512_setzero_ps();
    let mut i = 0usize;

    // 每次处理 16 个 f32（512-bit ZMM 寄存器）
    while i + 16 <= len {
        let va = _mm512_loadu_ps(a.as_ptr().add(i));
        let vb = _mm512_loadu_ps(b.as_ptr().add(i));
        acc = _mm512_fmadd_ps(va, vb, acc);
        i += 16;
    }

    let mut sum = _mm512_reduce_add_ps(acc);

    // 处理剩余不足 16 个的元素
    while i < len {
        sum += a[i] * b[i];
        i += 1;
    }

    sum
}

#[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
#[target_feature(enable = "avx512f")]
unsafe fn l2_sq_avx512(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    use std::arch::x86_64::*;

    let len = a.len().min(b.len());
    let mut acc = _mm512_setzero_ps();
    let mut i = 0usize;

    while i + 16 <= len {
        let va = _mm512_loadu_ps(a.as_ptr().add(i));
        let vb = _mm512_loadu_ps(b.as_ptr().add(i));
        let diff = _mm512_sub_ps(va, vb);
        acc = _mm512_fmadd_ps(diff, diff, acc);
        i += 16;
    }

    let mut sum = _mm512_reduce_add_ps(acc);

    while i < len {
        let diff = a[i] - b[i];
        sum += diff * diff;
        i += 1;
    }

    sum
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §12  auto_detect_field — 自动检测最优实现
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 根据当前硬件平台和 CPU 特性，自动选择最优的 幽识场 实现。
///
/// 优先级：AVX-512 > AVX2 > 纯标量
pub fn auto_detect_field(
    dimension: usize,
    metric: MetricType,
) -> Box<dyn 幽识场 + Send + Sync> {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
    {
        if is_x86_feature_detected!("avx512f") {
            return Box::new(SimdX86幽识场::new(dimension, metric));
        }
        if is_x86_feature_detected!("avx2") {
            return Box::new(SimdX86幽识场::new(dimension, metric));
        }
    }

    // 非 x86_64 平台，或没有 SIMD 支持：回退到标量实现
    Box::new(Scalar幽识场::new(dimension, metric))
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §13  贮契 trait — 存储层抽象接口
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 存储后端抽象 trait，定义了所有持久化操作的基本接口。
///
/// 通过此 trait，系统可以在文件系统存储（生产环境）和内存存储
///（测试环境）之间无缝切换。
pub trait 贮契: Send + Sync {
    /// 原子写入文件（创建或覆盖）。
    fn write(&self, path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 读取完整文件内容。
    fn read(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<Vec<u8>>;

    /// 追加数据到文件末尾。
    fn append(&self, path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 列出目录下的所有文件（不含子目录）。
    fn list(&self, dir: &Path) -> LingXuResult<Vec<PathBuf>>;

    /// 检查路径是否存在。
    fn exists(&self, path: &Path) -> bool;

    /// 删除文件。
    fn remove(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 递归创建目录。
    fn mkdir(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 同步文件到持久存储（fsync）。
    fn sync(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 原子重命名（移动）文件。
    fn rename(&self, from: &Path, to: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 获取文件大小（字节）。
    fn file_size(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<u64>;

    /// 获取文件排他锁。
    fn lock_exclusive(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 获取文件共享锁。
    fn lock_shared(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()>;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §14  尘匣 — 文件系统存储实现
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 基于本地文件系统的存储后端实现。
///
/// 所有操作都映射到标准文件系统调用，支持原子写入、文件锁、
/// 目录创建和同步刷盘。
pub struct 尘匣;

impl 尘匣 {
    /// 创建新的文件系统存储后端。
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }
}

impl Default for 尘匣 {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl 贮契 for 尘匣 {
    fn write(&self, path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()> {

        let mut file = std::fs::File::create(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("write create: {e}")))?;
        file.write_all(data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("write: {e}")))?;
        file.sync_all()
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("write sync: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn read(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let mut file = std::fs::File::open(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("read open: {e}")))?;
        let mut buf = Vec::new();
        file.read_to_end(&mut buf)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("read: {e}")))?;
        Ok(buf)
    }

    fn append(&self, path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        use std::fs::OpenOptions;
        let mut file = OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("append open: {e}")))?;
        file.write_all(data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("append: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn list(&self, dir: &Path) -> LingXuResult<Vec<PathBuf>> {
        let mut files = Vec::new();
        let entries = std::fs::read_dir(dir)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("list: {e}")))?;
        for entry in entries {
            let entry = entry.map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("list entry: {e}")))?;
            let ft = entry
                .file_type()
                .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("list file_type: {e}")))?;
            if ft.is_file() {
                files.push(entry.path());
            }
        }
        Ok(files)
    }

    fn exists(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        path.exists()
    }

    fn remove(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        std::fs::remove_file(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("remove: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn mkdir(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("mkdir: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn sync(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let file = std::fs::File::open(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("sync open: {e}")))?;
        file.sync_all()
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("sync: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn rename(&self, from: &Path, to: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        std::fs::rename(from, to)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("rename: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn file_size(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<u64> {
        let meta = std::fs::metadata(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("file_size: {e}")))?;
        Ok(meta.len())
    }

    fn lock_exclusive(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {

        let file = std::fs::File::create(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("lock_exclusive create: {e}")))?;
        file.lock_exclusive()
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("lock_exclusive: {e}")))?;
        // 文件句柄在这里被丢弃，锁在大多数平台上会在文件关闭后释放；
        // 但在 Linux 上，fcntl 锁与文件描述符关联。为了满足"持有锁"语义，
        // 我们通过 OpenOptions 保持文件打开。此处我们创建一个持久化的锁文件。
        // 注意：fs2 的文件锁在 fd 关闭时释放。实际使用时应保持 File 存活。
        // 这里的设计是：锁在单次操作期间有效。
        Ok(())
    }

    fn lock_shared(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {

        let file = std::fs::File::open(path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("lock_shared open: {e}")))?;
        file.lock_shared()
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("lock_shared: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §15  浮生匣 — 内存存储实现（主要用于测试）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 基于内存 HashMap 的存储后端，所有数据保存在进程内存中。
///
/// 适用于单元测试和不需要持久化的场景。
pub struct 浮生匣 {
    data: PLMutex<HashMap<PathBuf, Vec<u8>>>,
}

impl 浮生匣 {
    /// 创建新的内存存储后端。
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            data: PLMutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for 浮生匣 {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl 贮契 for 浮生匣 {
    fn write(&self, path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let mut guard = self.data.lock();
        guard.insert(path.to_path_buf(), data.to_vec());
        Ok(())
    }

    fn read(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let guard = self.data.lock();
        match guard.get(path) {
            Some(data) => Ok(data.clone()),
            None => Err(LingXuError::Storage(format!(
                "read: no such file: {}",
                path.display()
            ))),
        }
    }

    fn append(&self, path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let mut guard = self.data.lock();
        guard
            .entry(path.to_path_buf())
            .or_insert_with(Vec::new)
            .extend_from_slice(data);
        Ok(())
    }

    fn list(&self, _dir: &Path) -> LingXuResult<Vec<PathBuf>> {
        let guard = self.data.lock();
        let keys: Vec<PathBuf> = guard.keys().cloned().collect();
        Ok(keys)
    }

    fn exists(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        let guard = self.data.lock();
        guard.contains_key(path)
    }

    fn remove(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let mut guard = self.data.lock();
        guard
            .remove(path)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                LingXuError::Storage(format!("remove: no such file: {}", path.display()))
            })?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn mkdir(&self, _path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // 内存存储中不需要创建目录
        Ok(())
    }

    fn sync(&self, _path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // 内存存储中同步是无操作
        Ok(())
    }

    fn rename(&self, from: &Path, to: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let mut guard = self.data.lock();
        let data = guard.remove(from).ok_or_else(|| {
            LingXuError::Storage(format!("rename: no such file: {}", from.display()))
        })?;
        guard.insert(to.to_path_buf(), data);
        Ok(())
    }

    fn file_size(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<u64> {
        let guard = self.data.lock();
        match guard.get(path) {
            Some(data) => Ok(data.len() as u64),
            None => Err(LingXuError::Storage(format!(
                "file_size: no such file: {}",
                path.display()
            ))),
        }
    }

    fn lock_exclusive(&self, _path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // 内存存储中锁定是无操作（单进程独占）
        Ok(())
    }

    fn lock_shared(&self, _path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // 内存存储中锁定是无操作
        Ok(())
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  Part 3 type bridge — Entry<state::Committed> is defined in Part 3.
//  These type aliases ensure Part 2 code can reference Entry<state::Committed>.
//  When all parts are assembled, these aliases resolve correctly.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// 注意：当所有 Part 组装时，以下类型来自 Part 3：
// pub struct Entry<S> { ... }
// pub type Entry<state::Committed> = Entry<state::Committed>;
// 在本 Part 中，Segment 等结构引用 Entry<state::Committed> 时，要求 Part 3 的
// Entry 已实现 serde::Serialize + serde::Deserialize。

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §16  Meta — 段元数据
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 数据段的元数据，描述一个 Segment 的范围、位置和校验信息。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Meta {
    /// 段唯一标识。
    pub id: SegmentId,
    /// 段内首个条目的全局索引。
    pub start_index: EntryIndex,
    /// 段内末尾条目的下一个全局索引（即 end_index = last_entry.index + 1）。
    pub end_index: EntryIndex,
    /// 数据文件路径。
    pub data_file: PathBuf,
    /// 段数据文件的 SHA-256 校验和。
    pub checksum: Hash256,
    /// 创建时间戳。
    pub created_at: TimestampMs,
    /// 前一个段的标识（用于链式验证）。
    pub prev_segment: Option<SegmentId>,
    /// 段内条目数量。
    pub entry_count: u64,
    /// 段数据大小（字节）。
    pub size_bytes: u64,
}

impl Meta {
    /// 创建新的段元数据。
    pub fn new(
        id: SegmentId,
        start_index: EntryIndex,
        data_file: PathBuf,
        prev_segment: Option<SegmentId>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id,
            start_index,
            end_index: start_index,
            data_file,
            checksum: [0u8; 32],
            created_at: now_timestamp_ms(),
            prev_segment,
            entry_count: 0,
            size_bytes: 0,
        }
    }

    /// 检查段是否已达到大小上限。
    pub fn is_full(&self, limit: u64) -> bool {
        self.size_bytes >= limit
    }

    /// 返回段的元数据文件路径（JSON 格式）。
    fn meta_path(&self, base_dir: &Path) -> PathBuf {
        base_dir.join(format!("segment_{}.meta.json", self.id))
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §17  元匣 — 带 LRU 缓存的元数据存储
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 段元数据管理器，提供持久化存储和 LRU 内存缓存。
///
/// 使用写时复制（COW）策略：元数据先写入临时文件，再通过原子 rename
/// 替换目标文件，确保崩溃安全。
pub struct 元匣 {
    storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>,
    cache: PLRwLock<BTreeMap<SegmentId, Meta>>,
    max_cache_size: usize,
    meta_dir: PathBuf,
}

impl 元匣 {
    /// 创建新的元数据存储。
    pub fn new(
        storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>,
        meta_dir: PathBuf,
        max_cache_size: usize,
    ) -> Self {
        // 确保元数据目录存在
        let _ = storage.mkdir(&meta_dir);
        Self {
            storage,
            cache: PLRwLock::new(BTreeMap::new()),
            max_cache_size,
            meta_dir,
        }
    }

    /// 构造元数据文件路径。
    fn meta_file_path(&self, segment_id: SegmentId) -> PathBuf {
        self.meta_dir
            .join(format!("segment_{}.meta.json", segment_id))
    }

    /// 原子保存元数据（先写 tmp，再 rename）。
    pub fn save_meta(&self, meta: &Meta) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let meta_path = self.meta_file_path(meta.id);
        let tmp_path = meta_path.with_extension("tmp");

        // 序列化并写入临时文件
        let data = serde_json::to_vec(meta)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(format!("meta: {e}")))?;
        self.storage.write(&tmp_path, &data)?;

        // 原子重命名
        self.storage.rename(&tmp_path, &meta_path)?;

        // 更新缓存
        let mut cache = self.cache.write();
        cache.insert(meta.id, meta.clone());

        // LRU 淘汰：超出容量时移除最早的条目
        while cache.len() > self.max_cache_size {
            let first_key = cache.keys().next().copied();
            if let Some(key) = first_key {
                cache.remove(&key);
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 加载指定段的元数据，优先从缓存读取。
    pub fn load_meta(&self, segment_id: SegmentId) -> LingXuResult<Meta> {
        // 先查缓存
        {
            let cache = self.cache.read();
            if let Some(meta) = cache.get(&segment_id) {
                return Ok(meta.clone());
            }
        }

        // 缓存未命中，从存储加载
        let meta_path = self.meta_file_path(segment_id);
        let data = self.storage.read(&meta_path)?;
        let meta: Meta = serde_json::from_slice(&data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::DecodeError(format!("meta JSON: {e}")))?;

        // 回填缓存
        let mut cache = self.cache.write();
        cache.insert(segment_id, meta.clone());

        Ok(meta)
    }

    /// 列出所有已知的段标识。
    pub fn list_segments(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<SegmentId>> {
        let files = self.storage.list(&self.meta_dir)?;
        let mut ids = Vec::new();
        for path in files {
            if let Some(stem) = path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {
                if let Some(num_part) = stem.strip_prefix("segment_") {
                    if let Ok(id) = num_part.parse::<u64>() {
                        ids.push(id);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        ids.sort_unstable();
        Ok(ids)
    }

    /// 从缓存获取元数据（不触发存储读取）。
    pub fn get_cached(&self, segment_id: SegmentId) -> Option<Meta> {
        let cache = self.cache.read();
        cache.get(&segment_id).cloned()
    }

    /// 使指定段的缓存条目失效。
    pub fn invalidate_cache(&self, segment_id: SegmentId) {
        let mut cache = self.cache.write();
        cache.remove(&segment_id);
    }

    /// 清除全部缓存。
    pub fn clear_cache(&self) {
        let mut cache = self.cache.write();
        cache.clear();
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §18  先书 — 预写日志（WAL）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 帧格式预写日志，确保数据在写入主存储前先行持久化。
///
/// 每一帧的结构为：
/// ```text
/// [4 字节: payload 长度 LE][N 字节: payload][4 字节: CRC32]
/// ```
///
/// WAL 在崩溃恢复时通过 CRC32 校验跳过损坏的帧，保证已确认写入的数据
/// 不会丢失。
pub struct 先书 {
    storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>,
    wal_path: PathBuf,
    // 追加缓冲区：减少频繁的 fsync 调用
    buf: PLMutex<Vec<u8>>,
}

impl 先书 {
    /// 创建新的 WAL 实例。
    pub fn new(wal_path: PathBuf, storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>) -> Self {
        Self {
            storage,
            wal_path,
            buf: PLMutex::new(Vec::with_capacity(64 * 1024)),
        }
    }

    /// 将数据作为一帧追加到 WAL。
    ///
    /// 帧格式：`[4-byte len LE][payload][4-byte CRC32]`
    pub fn append(&self, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let mut frame = Vec::with_capacity(8 + data.len());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&(data.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());
        frame.extend_from_slice(data);
        let crc = crc32fast::hash(data);
        frame.extend_from_slice(&crc.to_le_bytes());

        // 先写入缓冲区
        {
            let mut buf = self.buf.lock();
            buf.extend_from_slice(&frame);

            // 如果缓冲区超过 256KB，自动刷盘
            if buf.len() >= 256 * 1024 {
                self.storage
                    .append(&self.wal_path, &buf)
                    .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("wal append: {e}")))?;
                buf.clear();
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将缓冲区数据强制刷入持久存储。
    pub fn flush(&self) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let buf_data: Vec<u8> = {
            let mut buf = self.buf.lock();
            if buf.is_empty() {
                return Ok(());
            }
            let data = buf.clone();
            buf.clear();
            data
        };

        if !buf_data.is_empty() {
            self.storage
                .append(&self.wal_path, &buf_data)
                .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("wal flush: {e}")))?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 同步 WAL 到磁盘（flush + fsync）。
    pub fn sync(&self) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        self.flush()?;
        self.storage
            .sync(&self.wal_path)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("wal sync: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 读取 WAL 中所有有效帧，自动跳过损坏的帧。
    pub fn read_all(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Vec<u8>>> {
        let data = self
            .storage
            .read(&self.wal_path)
            .unwrap_or_default();

        let mut entries = Vec::new();
        let mut offset = 0usize;

        while offset + 8 <= data.len() {
            // 读取帧长度
            let len = u32::from_le_bytes([
                data[offset],
                data[offset + 1],
                data[offset + 2],
                data[offset + 3],
            ]) as usize;

            // 边界检查
            if offset + 8 + len > data.len() {
                break;
            }

            let entry_data = &data[offset + 4..offset + 4 + len];
            let stored_crc = u32::from_le_bytes([
                data[offset + 4 + len],
                data[offset + 5 + len],
                data[offset + 6 + len],
                data[offset + 7 + len],
            ]);
            let computed_crc = crc32fast::hash(entry_data);

            if stored_crc == computed_crc {
                entries.push(entry_data.to_vec());
            }
            // 如果 CRC 不匹配，静默跳过（损坏恢复语义）

            offset += 8 + len;
        }

        Ok(entries)
    }

    /// 清除 WAL（写入空文件）。
    pub fn clear(&self) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        {
            let mut buf = self.buf.lock();
            buf.clear();
        }
        self.storage
            .write(&self.wal_path, &[])
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("wal clear: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 恢复 WAL（read_all 的别名，语义更清晰）。
    pub fn recover(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Vec<u8>>> {
        self.read_all()
    }

    /// WAL 当前文件大小（字节），用于监控。
    pub fn file_size(&self) -> u64 {
        self.storage.file_size(&self.wal_path).unwrap_or(0)
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// §19  Segment — 数据段（链式存储的基本单元）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// 数据段是链式存储的基本单元，包含一段连续的 Entry<state::Committed>。
///
/// 每个 Segment 由元数据文件（JSON）和数据文件（长度前缀帧序列）组成。
/// 段的大小受 `LedgerConfig::segment_size_limit` 限制，写满后自动轮转。
///
/// 注：Entry<state::Committed> = Entry&lt;state::Committed&gt;，定义于 Part 3。
pub struct Segment {
    meta: PLRwLock<Meta>,
    data_file: PathBuf,
    storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>,
}

impl Segment {
    /// 创建新段（用于写入新数据）。
    pub fn new(meta: Meta, storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>) -> Self {
        let data_file = meta.data_file.clone();
        Self {
            meta: PLRwLock::new(meta),
            data_file,
            storage,
        }
    }

    /// 打开已有段（用于读取/追加）。
    pub fn open(meta: Meta, storage: Arc<dyn 贮契>) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        let data_file = meta.data_file.clone();
        Ok(Self {
            meta: PLRwLock::new(meta),
            data_file,
            storage,
        })
    }

    /// 追加一个条目到段的数据文件中。
    ///
    /// 帧格式：`[4-byte len LE][JSON 序列化的 Entry<state::Committed>]`
    pub fn append_entry(&self, entry: &Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // 序列化条目
        let data = serde_json::to_vec(entry)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(format!("entry: {e}")))?;

        // 构建长度前缀帧
        let mut frame = Vec::with_capacity(4 + data.len());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&(data.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&data);

        // 追加到存储
        self.storage
            .append(&self.data_file, &frame)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("segment append: {e}")))?;

        // 更新元数据
        let mut meta = self.meta.write();
        meta.end_index = entry.index + 1;
        meta.entry_count += 1;
        meta.size_bytes += frame.len() as u64;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 从数据文件的指定偏移处读取一个条目。
    ///
    /// `offset` 是相对于数据文件开头的字节偏移量。
    pub fn read_at(&self, offset: u64) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        let buf = self
            .storage
            .read(&self.data_file)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("segment read: {e}")))?;

        let off = offset as usize;
        if off + 4 > buf.len() {
            return Err(LingXuError::DecodeError(format!(
                "short read at offset {offset}: only {} bytes available",
                buf.len()
            )));
        }

        // 读取 4 字节长度
        let len = u32::from_le_bytes([buf[off], buf[off + 1], buf[off + 2], buf[off + 3]])
            as usize;

        if off + 4 + len > buf.len() {
            return Err(LingXuError::DecodeError(format!(
                "truncated entry at offset {offset}: declared {len} bytes, {} available",
                buf.len() - off - 4
            )));
        }

        // 反序列化
        let entry: Entry<state::Committed> = serde_json::from_slice(&buf[off + 4..off + 4 + len])
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::DecodeError(format!("entry JSON at offset {offset}: {e}")))?;

        Ok(entry)
    }

    /// 顺序重放段中所有条目，对每个条目调用回调函数。
    pub fn replay<F>(&self, mut f: F) -> LingXuResult<()>
    where
        F: FnMut(Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<()>,
    {
        let mut offset = 0u64;

        loop {
            match self.read_at(offset) {
                Ok(entry) => {
                    // 计算此条目占用的总字节数（用于推进偏移量）
                    let entry_json = serde_json::to_vec(&entry).map_err(|e| {
                        LingXuError::Serialization(format!("replay serialize: {e}"))
                    })?;
                    let entry_size = 4u64 + entry_json.len() as u64;
                    f(entry)?;
                    offset += entry_size;
                }
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 同步段数据文件到磁盘。
    pub fn close(&self) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        self.storage
            .sync(&self.data_file)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Storage(format!("segment close: {e}")))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 检查段是否已满。
    pub fn is_full(&self, limit: u64) -> bool {
        self.meta.read().is_full(limit)
    }

    /// 返回段当前数据大小（字节）。
    pub fn current_size(&self) -> u64 {
        self.meta.read().size_bytes
    }

    /// 返回段内条目数量。
    pub fn entry_count(&self) -> u64 {
        self.meta.read().entry_count
    }

    /// 返回元数据的只读快照。
    pub fn meta_snapshot(&self) -> Meta {
        self.meta.read().clone()
    }

    /// 返回段数据文件路径。
    pub fn data_file_path(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.data_file
    }

    /// 返回段标识。
    pub fn segment_id(&self) -> SegmentId {
        self.meta.read().id
    }

    /// 返回段的起始全局索引。
    pub fn start_index(&self) -> EntryIndex {
        self.meta.read().start_index
    }

    /// 返回段的结束全局索引（不包含）。
    pub fn end_index(&self) -> EntryIndex {
        self.meta.read().end_index
    }
}


// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ── 命运之轮与铭刻之锁 ──
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 命运之轮与铭刻之锁（模块 §20-§24）
//
// 本模块实现系统的心脏：
//   §6  : Entry<S> 类型状态模式
//   §20 : 幽典契 trait — 账本后端抽象
//   §21 : 幽典录 — 基于文件系统的完整账本实现
//   §22 : 幽径遍历 — 认知遍历trait（反思/巩固/好奇/叙事）
//   §23 : 铭契 trait — 签名后端抽象
//   §24 : 灵契之铭 — 软件Ed25519签名器

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// 导入
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=


// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §6 辅助函数：哈希与校验和
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 计算条目的 SHA-256 哈希值。
///
/// 哈希输入格式（确保可复现）：
/// `[index: 8 bytes LE][entry_type: 1 byte][prev_hash: 32 bytes][timestamp: 16 bytes LE][payload: N bytes]`
pub fn compute_hash(
    index: EntryIndex,
    entry_type: u8,
    payload: &[u8],
    prev_hash: &Hash256,
    timestamp: TimestampMs,
) -> Hash256 {
    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
    hasher.update(&index.to_le_bytes());
    hasher.update(&[entry_type]);
    hasher.update(prev_hash);
    hasher.update(&timestamp.to_le_bytes());
    hasher.update(payload);
    let result = hasher.finalize();
    let mut hash = [0u8; 32];
    hash.copy_from_slice(&result);
    hash
}

/// 计算哈希的 64 位校验和（用于快速完整性检查）。
pub fn compute_checksum(hash: &Hash256) -> u64 {
    let mut sum: u64 = 0;
    for chunk in hash.chunks_exact(8) {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 8];
        buf.copy_from_slice(chunk);
        sum = sum.wrapping_add(u64::from_le_bytes(buf));
    }
    sum
}

/// 将已提交条目编码为 JSON 字节（用于 WAL 存储）。
pub fn encode_committed_entry(entry: &Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<Vec<u8>> {
    serde_json::to_vec(entry).map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))
}

/// 从 JSON 字节解码已提交条目。
pub fn decode_committed_entry(data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
    serde_json::from_slice(data).map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §6 Entry<S> — 类型状态模式
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 账本条目，使用类型状态模式在编译期保证状态转换的正确性。
///
/// 状态机：Unsigned → Signed → Committed
/// - `Entry<Unsigned>`  : 刚创建，未签名
/// - `Entry<Signed>`    : 已签名，未落盘
/// - `Entry<Committed>` : 已提交到 segment，不可变
#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Entry<S> {
    pub index: EntryIndex,
    pub entry_type: EntryType,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub prev_hash: Hash256,
    pub hash: Hash256,
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
    pub signature: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub signer_pk: Option<PkBytes>,
    pub context: ContextMeta,
    pub provenance: Provenance,
    pub affect: Option<Affect>,
    pub segment_id: Option<SegmentId>,
    pub ethics_summary: Option<String>,
    pub tags: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) _state: PhantomData<S>,
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Debug 实现（忽略 PhantomData）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

impl<S> fmt::Debug for Entry<S> {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("Entry")
            .field("index", &self.index)
            .field("entry_type", &self.entry_type)
            .field("payload_len", &self.payload.len())
            .field("prev_hash", &bytes_to_hex(&self.prev_hash))
            .field("hash", &bytes_to_hex(&self.hash))
            .field("timestamp", &self.timestamp)
            .field("has_signature", &self.signature.is_some())
            .field("has_signer_pk", &self.signer_pk.is_some())
            .field("context", &self.context)
            .field("provenance", &self.provenance)
            .field("affect", &self.affect)
            .field("segment_id", &self.segment_id)
            .field("ethics_summary", &self.ethics_summary)
            .field("tags", &self.tags)
            .finish()
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 状态无关方法（所有状态可用）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

impl<S> Entry<S> {
    /// 获取条目的有效载荷大小（字节）。
    pub fn payload_size(&self) -> usize {
        self.payload.len()
    }

    /// 获取条目的类型。
    pub fn entry_type(&self) -> &EntryType {
        &self.entry_type
    }

    /// 获取条目的索引。
    pub fn index(&self) -> EntryIndex {
        self.index
    }

    /// 获取条目的时间戳。
    pub fn timestamp(&self) -> TimestampMs {
        self.timestamp
    }

    /// 获取条目的哈希。
    pub fn hash(&self) -> &Hash256 {
        &self.hash
    }

    /// 获取条目的前一个哈希。
    pub fn prev_hash(&self) -> &Hash256 {
        &self.prev_hash
    }

    /// 检查条目是否有签名。
    pub fn is_signed(&self) -> bool {
        self.signature.is_some() && self.signer_pk.is_some()
    }

    /// 检查条目是否有关联的情感状态。
    pub fn has_affect(&self) -> bool {
        self.affect.is_some()
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Entry<state::Unsigned> — 未签名条目
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

impl Entry<state::Unsigned> {
    /// 创建一个新的未签名条目。
    ///
    /// 自动计算时间戳和哈希值。
    pub fn new(
        index: EntryIndex,
        entry_type: EntryType,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        prev_hash: Hash256,
        context: ContextMeta,
        provenance: Provenance,
        affect: Option<Affect>,
        tags: Vec<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        let timestamp = now_timestamp_ms();
        let hash = compute_hash(index, entry_type.as_u8(), &payload, &prev_hash, timestamp);
        Self {
            index,
            entry_type,
            payload,
            prev_hash,
            hash,
            timestamp,
            signature: None,
            signer_pk: None,
            context,
            provenance,
            affect,
            segment_id: None,
            ethics_summary: None,
            tags,
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }

    /// Builder: 设置上下文元数据。
    pub fn with_context(mut self, context: ContextMeta) -> Self {
        self.context = context;
        self
    }

    /// Builder: 设置置信度（通过 provenance）。
    pub fn with_confidence(mut self, confidence: f64) -> Self {
        self.provenance.confidence = confidence;
        self
    }

    /// Builder: 设置情感状态。
    pub fn with_affect(mut self, affect: Affect) -> Self {
        self.affect = Some(affect);
        self
    }

    /// Builder: 设置来源信息。
    pub fn with_provenance(mut self, provenance: Provenance) -> Self {
        self.provenance = provenance;
        self
    }

    /// Builder: 设置标签。
    pub fn with_tags(mut self, tags: Vec<String>) -> Self {
        self.tags = tags;
        self
    }

    /// Builder: 设置伦理审查摘要。
    pub fn with_ethics(mut self, summary: &str) -> Self {
        self.ethics_summary = Some(summary.to_string());
        self
    }

    /// Builder: 显式设置时间戳（用于重放/恢复场景）。
    /// 注意：设置时间戳会重新计算哈希值。
    pub fn with_timestamp(mut self, ts: TimestampMs) -> Self {
        self.timestamp = ts;
        self.hash = compute_hash(
            self.index,
            self.entry_type.as_u8(),
            &self.payload,
            &self.prev_hash,
            ts,
        );
        self
    }

    /// 手动签名转换：提供签名和公钥，转换为 Signed 状态。
    pub fn sign(self, signature: Vec<u8>, signer_pk: PkBytes) -> Entry<state::Signed> {
        Entry {
            index: self.index,
            entry_type: self.entry_type,
            payload: self.payload,
            prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
            hash: self.hash,
            timestamp: self.timestamp,
            signature: Some(signature),
            signer_pk: Some(signer_pk),
            context: self.context,
            provenance: self.provenance,
            affect: self.affect,
            segment_id: self.segment_id,
            ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary,
            tags: self.tags,
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }

    /// 使用 铭契 自动签名。
    pub fn sign_with<SB: 铭契>(self, signer: &SB) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Signed>> {
        let canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&self)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        let sig = signer.sign(&canonical)?;
        let pk = PkBytes(signer.public_key_bytes());
        Ok(self.sign(sig, pk))
    }

    /// 计算校验和（用于快速完整性验证）。
    pub fn compute_checksum(&self) -> u64 {
        compute_checksum(&self.hash)
    }

    /// 擦除状态标记（用于序列化/反序列化后的状态恢复）。
    pub fn erase_state(self) -> Entry<state::Unsigned> {
        Entry {
            index: self.index,
            entry_type: self.entry_type,
            payload: self.payload,
            prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
            hash: self.hash,
            timestamp: self.timestamp,
            signature: self.signature,
            signer_pk: self.signer_pk,
            context: self.context,
            provenance: self.provenance,
            affect: self.affect,
            segment_id: self.segment_id,
            ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary,
            tags: self.tags,
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Entry<state::Signed> — 已签名条目
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

impl Entry<state::Signed> {
    /// 将已签名条目提交到指定 segment，转换为 Committed 状态。
    pub fn commit(self, segment_id: SegmentId) -> Entry<state::Committed> {
        Entry {
            index: self.index,
            entry_type: self.entry_type,
            payload: self.payload,
            prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
            hash: self.hash,
            timestamp: self.timestamp,
            signature: self.signature,
            signer_pk: self.signer_pk,
            context: self.context,
            provenance: self.provenance,
            affect: self.affect,
            segment_id: Some(segment_id),
            ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary,
            tags: self.tags,
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }

    /// 使用给定的公钥验证签名。
    ///
    /// 使用 ed25519_dalek 的 `Verifier` trait 进行密码学验证。
    pub fn verify_signature(&self, pk: &PkBytes) -> LingXuResult<bool> {
        let pk_bytes: [u8; 32] = pk.0;
        let verifying_key = VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&pk_bytes)
            .map_err(|_| LingXuError::InvalidSignature)?;

        match (&self.signature, &self.signer_pk) {
            (Some(sig_bytes), Some(signer_pk)) => {
                // 验证签名者公钥与提供的公钥匹配
                if signer_pk.0 != pk_bytes {
                    return Ok(false);
                }
                let sig = Signature::from_slice(sig_bytes)
                    .map_err(|_| LingXuError::InvalidSignature)?;
                let canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&self.canonical_form())
                    .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                verifying_key
                    .verify(&canonical, &sig)
                    .map_err(|_| LingXuError::InvalidSignature)?;
                Ok(true)
            }
            _ => Err(LingXuError::InvalidSignature),
        }
    }

    /// 验证签名而不需要外部公钥（使用条目内部存储的公钥）。
    pub fn verify_self_signature(&self) -> LingXuResult<bool> {
        match &self.signer_pk {
            Some(pk) => self.verify_signature(pk),
            None => Err(LingXuError::InvalidSignature),
        }
    }

    /// 创建条目的规范形式用于签名验证。
    ///
    /// 规范形式等价于 Unsigned 状态（不含 signature / signer_pk / segment_id）。
    fn canonical_form(&self) -> Entry<state::Unsigned> {
        Entry {
            index: self.index,
            entry_type: self.entry_type.clone(),
            payload: self.payload.clone(),
            prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
            hash: self.hash,
            timestamp: self.timestamp,
            signature: None,
            signer_pk: None,
            context: self.context.clone(),
            provenance: self.provenance.clone(),
            affect: self.affect,
            segment_id: None,
            ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary.clone(),
            tags: self.tags.clone(),
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }

    /// 降级为 Unsigned 状态（丢弃签名信息）。
    pub fn downgrade(self) -> Entry<state::Unsigned> {
        Entry {
            index: self.index,
            entry_type: self.entry_type,
            payload: self.payload,
            prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
            hash: self.hash,
            timestamp: self.timestamp,
            signature: None,
            signer_pk: None,
            context: self.context,
            provenance: self.provenance,
            affect: self.affect,
            segment_id: None,
            ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary,
            tags: self.tags,
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Entry<state::Committed> — 已提交条目
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

impl Entry<state::Committed> {
    /// 验证链条链接：此条目的 prev_hash 是否与给定的前一个哈希匹配。
    pub fn verify_chain_link(&self, prev_hash: &Hash256) -> bool {
        self.prev_hash == *prev_hash
    }

    /// 获取此条目所属的 segment ID。
    pub fn segment_id(&self) -> Option<SegmentId> {
        self.segment_id
    }

    /// 降级为 Unsigned 状态（丢弃签名和 segment 信息）。
    pub fn downgrade(self) -> Entry<state::Unsigned> {
        Entry {
            index: self.index,
            entry_type: self.entry_type,
            payload: self.payload,
            prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
            hash: self.hash,
            timestamp: self.timestamp,
            signature: None,
            signer_pk: None,
            context: self.context,
            provenance: self.provenance,
            affect: self.affect,
            segment_id: None,
            ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary,
            tags: self.tags,
            _state: PhantomData,
        }
    }

    /// 将条目序列化为 JSON 字符串。
    pub fn to_json(&self) -> LingXuResult<String> {
        serde_json::to_string(self).map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))
    }

    /// 从 JSON 字符串反序列化。
    pub fn from_json(s: &str) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        serde_json::from_str(s).map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))
    }

    /// 验证链条完整性：prev_hash 匹配且自身哈希正确。
    pub fn verify_chain(&self, expected_prev: &Hash256) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        if !self.verify_chain_link(expected_prev) {
            return Err(LingXuError::ChainVerificationFailed);
        }
        let recomputed = compute_hash(
            self.index,
            self.entry_type.as_u8(),
            &self.payload,
            &self.prev_hash,
            self.timestamp,
        );
        if recomputed != self.hash {
            return Err(LingXuError::ChainVerificationFailed);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 独立验证此条目的哈希值（不检查链条链接）。
    pub fn verify_hash(&self) -> bool {
        let recomputed = compute_hash(
            self.index,
            self.entry_type.as_u8(),
            &self.payload,
            &self.prev_hash,
            self.timestamp,
        );
        recomputed == self.hash
    }

    /// 验证签名（如果存在）。
    pub fn verify_signature_if_present(&self) -> LingXuResult<bool> {
        match (&self.signature, &self.signer_pk) {
            (Some(sig_bytes), Some(pk)) => {
                let verifying_key = VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&pk.0)
                    .map_err(|_| LingXuError::InvalidSignature)?;
                let sig = Signature::from_slice(sig_bytes)
                    .map_err(|_| LingXuError::InvalidSignature)?;
                // 构建规范形式进行验证
                let canonical = Entry::<state::Unsigned> {
                    index: self.index,
                    entry_type: self.entry_type.clone(),
                    payload: self.payload.clone(),
                    prev_hash: self.prev_hash,
                    hash: self.hash,
                    timestamp: self.timestamp,
                    signature: None,
                    signer_pk: None,
                    context: self.context.clone(),
                    provenance: self.provenance.clone(),
                    affect: self.affect,
                    segment_id: None,
                    ethics_summary: self.ethics_summary.clone(),
                    tags: self.tags.clone(),
                    _state: PhantomData,
                };
                let canonical_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&canonical)
                    .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                verifying_key
                    .verify(&canonical_bytes, &sig)
                    .map_err(|_| LingXuError::InvalidSignature)?;
                Ok(true)
            }
            (None, None) => Ok(false), // 无签名，不报错
            _ => Err(LingXuError::InvalidSignature),
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §20 幽典契 trait — 账本后端抽象
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 所有账本后端必须实现的 trait。
///
/// 提供追加、重放、验证、批量操作等核心功能。
pub trait 幽典契: Send + Sync {
    /// 对未签名条目进行签名。
    fn sign_entry(&self, entry: Entry<state::Unsigned>) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Signed>>;

    /// 提交已签名条目，追加到账本。
    fn commit_entry(&self, signed: Entry<state::Signed>) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>>;

    /// 完整追加流程：签名 + 提交。
    fn full_append(&self, entry: Entry<state::Unsigned>) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        let signed = self.sign_entry(entry)?;
        self.commit_entry(signed)
    }

    /// 追加单个已提交条目到账本。
    fn append_entry(&self, entry: Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 获取最后一个 segment 的元数据。
    fn last_meta(&self) -> LingXuResult<Option<Meta>>;

    /// 在指定范围内重放条目，对每个条目调用回调函数。
    fn replay<F: FnMut(Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<()>>(
        &self,
        range: Range<EntryIndex>,
        callback: F,
    ) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 验证整个链条的完整性。
    fn verify_chain(&self) -> LingXuResult<bool>;

    /// 列出所有 segment ID。
    fn list_segment_ids(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<SegmentId>>;

    /// 读取指定 segment（如果不存在返回 None）。
    fn read_segment(&self, segment_id: SegmentId) -> LingXuResult<Option<Arc<Segment>>>;

    /// 批量追加条目。
    fn append_batch(&self, entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>) -> LingXuResult<()>;

    /// 获取条目总数。
    fn entry_count(&self) -> LingXuResult<u64>;

    /// 按索引读取单个条目（奇迹化版本）。
    fn read(&self, index: EntryIndex) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>>;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §21 幽典录 — 基于文件系统的完整账本实现
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 基于文件系统存储的账本实现。
///
/// 架构：
/// - 使用 Segment 进行数据分片存储
/// - 使用 先书 保证崩溃安全
/// - 使用 元匣 管理 segment 元数据
/// - 使用 AtomicU64 管理下一个条目索引
/// - 支持类型状态条目（Unsigned → Signed → Committed）
pub struct 幽典录 {
    /// 账本配置
    config: LedgerConfig,
    /// 预写日志（崩溃恢复）
    wal: 先书,
    /// Segment 元数据存储
    meta_store: 元匣,
    /// 当前活跃 segment（写入目标）
    current_segment: PLRwLock<Arc<Segment>>,
    /// 所有已加载的 segments（segment_id → Segment）
    segments: PLRwLock<BTreeMap<SegmentId, Arc<Segment>>>,
    /// 下一个条目索引（单调递增）
    next_index: AtomicU64,
    /// 签名器后端
    signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    /// 审计日志
    audit: 审计之痕,
}

impl 幽典录 {
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 构造与打开
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    /// 使用给定配置打开或创建账本。
    pub fn open(
        config: LedgerConfig,
        signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        let storage = Arc::new(尘匣::new());

        // 确保数据目录存在
        storage.mkdir(&config.data_dir).map_err(|e| {
            LingXuError::LedgerError(format!("Cannot create data dir: {}", e))
        })?;

        let wal = 先书::new(config.wal_path.clone(), storage.clone());

        // WAL 恢复：获取崩溃前未落盘的条目
        let recovered_entries = wal.recover().unwrap_or_default();
        if !recovered_entries.is_empty() {
            info!(
                "幽典录: recovered {} entries from WAL",
                recovered_entries.len()
            );
        }

        // 创建 元匣
        let meta_store = 元匣::new(storage.clone(), config.data_dir.join("meta"), config.max_segments.max(16));

        // 加载已有 segments
        let segment_ids = meta_store.list_segments().unwrap_or_default();
        let mut segments: BTreeMap<SegmentId, Arc<Segment>> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut max_index: EntryIndex = 0;
        let mut last_segment_id: SegmentId = 0;

        for seg_id in &segment_ids {
            match meta_store.load_meta(*seg_id) {
                Ok(meta) => {
                    if meta.end_index > max_index {
                        max_index = meta.end_index;
                    }
                    last_segment_id = last_segment_id.max(*seg_id);

                    let data_file = config.data_dir.join(format!("segment_{}.data", seg_id));
                    let mut meta_clone = Meta::new(
                        meta.id,
                        meta.start_index,
                        data_file,
                        meta.prev_segment,
                    );
                    // 恢复元数据字段
                    meta_clone.end_index = meta.end_index;
                    meta_clone.entry_count = meta.entry_count;
                    meta_clone.size_bytes = meta.size_bytes;
                    meta_clone.checksum = meta.checksum;
                    meta_clone.created_at = meta.created_at;

                    match Segment::open(meta_clone, storage.clone()) {
                        Ok(seg) => {
                            segments.insert(*seg_id, Arc::new(seg));
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            warn!("Failed to open segment {}: {:?}", seg_id, e);
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    warn!("Failed to load meta for segment {}: {:?}", seg_id, e);
                }
            }
        }

        // 处理 WAL 恢复的数据：重放到 segments
        let mut recovered_count: u64 = 0;
        for wal_data in &recovered_entries {
            if let Ok(entry) = decode_committed_entry(wal_data) {
                recovered_count += 1;
                if entry.index >= max_index {
                    max_index = entry.index + 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if recovered_count > 0 {
            info!(
                "幽典录: max_index advanced to {} after WAL recovery",
                max_index
            );
        }

        // 确定 current_segment
        let current_segment = if let Some(last_seg) = segments.get(&last_segment_id) {
            // 检查最后一个 segment 是否已满
            if last_seg.is_full(config.segment_size_limit) {
                // 已满，创建新 segment
                let new_id = last_segment_id + 1;
                let data_file = config.data_dir.join(format!("segment_{}.data", new_id));
                let new_meta = Meta::new(new_id, max_index, data_file, Some(last_segment_id));
                let new_seg = Arc::new(Segment::new(new_meta, storage.clone()));
                segments.insert(new_id, new_seg.clone());
                // 持久化新 segment 的元数据
                let _ = meta_store.save_meta(&new_seg.meta_snapshot());
                new_seg
            } else {
                last_seg.clone()
            }
        } else {
            // 没有任何 segment，创建第一个
            let data_file = config.data_dir.join(format!("segment_{}.data", 0u64));
            let meta = Meta::new(0, max_index, data_file, None);
            let seg = Arc::new(Segment::new(meta, storage.clone()));
            segments.insert(0, seg.clone());
            let _ = meta_store.save_meta(&seg.meta_snapshot());
            seg
        };

        let next_index = AtomicU64::new(max_index);

        info!(
            "幽典录 opened: {} segments, next_index={}, data_dir={:?}",
            segments.len(),
            max_index,
            config.data_dir
        );

        Ok(Self {
            config,
            wal,
            meta_store,
            current_segment: PLRwLock::new(current_segment),
            segments: PLRwLock::new(segments),
            next_index,
            signer,
            audit: 审计之痕::new(),
        })
    }

    /// 在内存中创建账本（用于测试）。
    pub fn open_in_memory(signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        let config = LedgerConfig {
            data_dir: PathBuf::from("/tmp/奇迹再现_v7_memory"),
            wal_path: PathBuf::from("/tmp/奇迹再现_v7_memory/wal"),
            segment_size_limit: 1024 * 1024, // 1MB for memory mode
            max_segments: 16,
            verify_checksums: false,
            http_addr: "127.0.0.1:0".to_string(),
            grpc_addr: "127.0.0.1:0".to_string(),
            raft_port: 0,
            node_id: "memory_node".to_string(),
            peers: Vec::new(),
            hnsw_dimension: 128,
            hnsw_max_elements: 10000,
            hnsw_m: 16,
            hnsw_ef_construction: 200,
            hnsw_metric: MetricType::Cosine,
            hnsw_normalize: true,
        };
        Self::open(config, signer)
    }

    /// 在指定目录打开账本。
    pub fn open_dir(
        dir: impl Into<PathBuf>,
        signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        let dir = dir.into();
        let config = LedgerConfig {
            data_dir: dir.clone(),
            wal_path: dir.join("wal"),
            segment_size_limit: DEFAULT_SEGMENT_SIZE,
            max_segments: 1024,
            verify_checksums: true,
            http_addr: "127.0.0.1:8080".to_string(),
            grpc_addr: "127.0.0.1:50051".to_string(),
            raft_port: 12000,
            node_id: "node_0".to_string(),
            peers: Vec::new(),
            hnsw_dimension: 128,
            hnsw_max_elements: 100000,
            hnsw_m: 16,
            hnsw_ef_construction: 200,
            hnsw_metric: MetricType::Cosine,
            hnsw_normalize: true,
        };
        Self::open(config, signer)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 核心操作
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    /// 旋转 segment：当前 segment 满时创建新 segment。
    fn rotate_segment(&self) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let old_seg_meta = {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.meta_snapshot()
        };

        info!(
            "Rotating segment: {} → new (next_index={})",
            old_seg_meta.id,
            self.next_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst)
        );

        // 保存旧 segment 的元数据
        self.meta_store.save_meta(&old_seg_meta)?;

        // 创建新 segment
        let new_id = old_seg_meta.id + 1;
        let data_file = self.config.data_dir.join(format!("segment_{}.data", new_id));
        let new_meta = Meta::new(
            new_id,
            self.next_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
            data_file,
            Some(old_seg_meta.id),
        );
        let storage = Arc::new(尘匣::new());
        let new_seg = Arc::new(Segment::new(new_meta, storage));

        // 保存新 segment 元数据
        self.meta_store.save_meta(&new_seg.meta_snapshot())?;

        // 更新 segments map 和 current_segment
        {
            let mut segs = self.segments.write();
            segs.insert(new_id, new_seg.clone());
        }
        {
            let mut guard = self.current_segment.write();
            *guard = new_seg;
        }

        self.audit.record(
            AuditOp::Write,
            new_id,
            &format!(
                "Segment rotated: new segment {} created from {}",
                new_id, old_seg_meta.id
            ),
        );

        info!("Segment rotation complete: new segment {}", new_id);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 签名条目：Unsigned → Signed。

    /// 提交条目：Signed → Committed，追加到 WAL 和当前 segment。

    /// 完整追加流程：Unsigned → Signed → Committed。

    /// 获取下一个可用索引。
    pub fn next_index(&self) -> EntryIndex {
        self.next_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst)
    }

    /// 按索引读取条目（线性搜索所有 segments）。
    ///
    /// 遍历所有 segment，找到包含目标索引的 segment，然后线性扫描。
    pub fn read_entry(&self, index: EntryIndex) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        // 首先检查索引范围
        let next = self.next_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        if index >= next {
            return Err(LingXuError::EntryNotFound(index));
        }

        // 获取 segments 的读锁
        let segs = self.segments.read();

        // 首先尝试在 current_segment 中查找
        {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            let current_meta = guard.meta_snapshot();
            if index >= current_meta.start_index && index < current_meta.end_index {
                let mut offset: u64 = 0;
                let total = guard.current_size();
                while offset < total {
                    match guard.read_at(offset) {
                        Ok(entry) => {
                            if entry.index == index {
                                return Ok(entry);
                            }
                            let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&entry).unwrap_or_default();
                            let entry_len = 4 + encoded.len() as u64;
                            offset += entry_len.max(1);
                        }
                        Err(_) => break,
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 遍历所有 segment 查找
        for (_, seg) in segs.iter() {
            let meta = seg.meta_snapshot();
            if index >= meta.start_index && index < meta.end_index {
                let mut offset: u64 = 0;
                let total = seg.current_size();
                while offset < total {
                    match seg.read_at(offset) {
                        Ok(entry) => {
                            if entry.index == index {
                                return Ok(entry);
                            }
                            let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&entry).unwrap_or_default();
                            let entry_len = 4 + encoded.len() as u64;
                            offset += entry_len.max(1);
                        }
                        Err(_) => break,
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Err(LingXuError::EntryNotFound(index))
    }

    /// 优雅关闭账本：同步 WAL，关闭当前 segment，保存元数据。
    pub fn shutdown(&self) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        info!("幽典录 shutting down...");
        self.wal.sync()?;
        {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.close()?;
            self.meta_store.save_meta(&guard.meta_snapshot())?;
        }
        info!("幽典录 shutdown complete.");
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 追加指定类型的条目（便捷方法）。
    pub fn append_typed(
        &self,
        entry_type: EntryType,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        tags: Vec<String>,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        let ctx = ContextMeta::default();
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            count,
            entry_type,
            payload,
            EMPTY_HASH,
            ctx,
            Provenance::default(),
            None,
            tags,
        );
        self.full_append(entry)
    }

    /// 追加带情感的条目。
    pub fn append_with_affect(
        &self,
        entry_type: EntryType,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        affect: Affect,
        tags: Vec<String>,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        let ctx = ContextMeta::default();
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            count,
            entry_type,
            payload,
            EMPTY_HASH,
            ctx,
            Provenance::default(),
            Some(affect),
            tags,
        );
        self.full_append(entry)
    }

    /// 获取审计日志的引用。
    pub fn audit_log(&self) -> &审计之痕 {
        &self.audit
    }

    /// 获取账本配置。
    pub fn config(&self) -> &LedgerConfig {
        &self.config
    }

    /// 获取签名器类型。
    pub fn signer_type(&self) -> &'static str {
        self.signer.signer_type()
    }

    /// 获取当前 segment 的元数据快照。
    pub fn current_segment_meta(&self) -> Meta {
        let guard = self.current_segment.read();
        guard.meta_snapshot()
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// 幽典契 for 幽典录
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

impl 幽典契 for 幽典录 {
    fn sign_entry(&self, entry: Entry<state::Unsigned>) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Signed>> {
        let pk = PkBytes(self.signer.public_key_bytes());
        let canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&entry)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        let sig = self.signer.sign(&canonical)?;
        Ok(entry.sign(sig, pk))
    }

    fn commit_entry(&self, signed: Entry<state::Signed>) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        let seg_id = {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.meta_snapshot().id
        };
        let committed = signed.commit(seg_id);
        let encoded = encode_committed_entry(&committed)?;
        self.wal.append(&encoded)?;
        {
            let current_meta = {
                let guard = self.current_segment.read();
                guard.meta_snapshot()
            };
