            if current_meta.is_full(self.config.segment_size_limit) {
                self.rotate_segment()?;
            }
        }
        {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.append_entry(&committed)?;
        }
        self.next_index.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        if self.config.verify_checksums {
            self.wal.sync()?;
        }
        Ok(committed)
    }

    /// 直接追加已提交条目（用于恢复等场景）。
    fn append_entry(&self, entry: Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let encoded = encode_committed_entry(&entry)?;
        self.wal.append(&encoded)?;

        {
            let current_meta = {
                let guard = self.current_segment.read();
                guard.meta_snapshot()
            };
            if current_meta.is_full(self.config.segment_size_limit) {
                self.rotate_segment()?;
            }
        }

        {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.append_entry(&entry)?;
        }
        self.next_index.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);

        if self.config.verify_checksums {
            self.wal.sync()?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 获取最后一个 segment 的元数据。
    fn last_meta(&self) -> LingXuResult<Option<Meta>> {
        let guard = self.current_segment.read();
        Ok(Some(guard.meta_snapshot()))
    }

    /// 在指定范围内重放条目。
    fn replay<F: FnMut(Entry<state::Committed>) -> LingXuResult<()>>(
        &self,
        range: Range<EntryIndex>,
        mut callback: F,
    ) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        if range.start >= range.end {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let segs = self.segments.read();
        let mut entries_yielded: u64 = 0;
        let expected_count = range.end.saturating_sub(range.start);

        // 首先遍历 current_segment
        {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            let current_meta = guard.meta_snapshot();
            if current_meta.start_index < range.end && current_meta.end_index > range.start {
                let mut offset: u64 = 0;
                let total = guard.current_size();
                while offset < total && entries_yielded < expected_count {
                    match guard.read_at(offset) {
                        Ok(entry) => {
                            if entry.index >= range.start && entry.index < range.end {
                                callback(entry.clone())?;
                                entries_yielded += 1;
                            }
                            let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&entry).unwrap_or_default();
                            let entry_len = 4 + encoded.len() as u64;
                            offset += entry_len.max(1);
                        }
                        Err(_) => break,
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 遍历其他 segment
        for (_, seg) in segs.iter() {
            let meta = seg.meta_snapshot();
            if meta.end_index <= range.start || meta.start_index >= range.end {
                continue;
            }

            let mut offset: u64 = 0;
            let total = seg.current_size();
            while offset < total && entries_yielded < expected_count {
                match seg.read_at(offset) {
                    Ok(entry) => {
                        if entry.index >= range.start && entry.index < range.end {
                            callback(entry.clone())?;
                            entries_yielded += 1;
                        }
                        let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&entry).unwrap_or_default();
                        let entry_len = 4 + encoded.len() as u64;
                        offset += entry_len.max(1);
                    }
                    Err(_) => break,
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 验证整个链条的完整性。
    fn verify_chain(&self) -> LingXuResult<bool> {
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(true);
        }

        let mut prev_hash = EMPTY_HASH;
        let mut all_valid = true;

        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            let recomputed = compute_hash(
                entry.index,
                entry.entry_type.as_u8(),
                &entry.payload,
                &entry.prev_hash,
                entry.timestamp,
            );
            if recomputed != entry.hash {
                warn!(
                    "Chain verification failed at index {}: hash mismatch",
                    entry.index
                );
                all_valid = false;
                return Ok(());
            }

            if entry.index > 0 && entry.prev_hash != prev_hash {
                warn!(
                    "Chain verification failed at index {}: prev_hash mismatch",
                    entry.index
                );
                all_valid = false;
                return Ok(());
            }

            prev_hash = entry.hash;
            Ok(())
        })?;

        Ok(all_valid)
    }

    /// 列出所有 segment ID。
    fn list_segment_ids(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<SegmentId>> {
        let segs = self.segments.read();
        let mut ids: Vec<SegmentId> = segs.keys().copied().collect();
        let current_id = {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.meta_snapshot().id
        };
        if !ids.contains(&current_id) {
            ids.push(current_id);
        }
        ids.sort_unstable();
        Ok(ids)
    }

    /// 读取指定 segment。
    fn read_segment(&self, segment_id: SegmentId) -> LingXuResult<Option<Arc<Segment>>> {
        let segs = self.segments.read();
        let current_id = {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            guard.meta_snapshot().id
        };
        if current_id == segment_id {
            let guard = self.current_segment.read();
            return Ok(Some(guard.clone()));
        }
        Ok(segs.get(&segment_id).cloned())
    }

    /// 批量追加条目。
    fn append_batch(&self, entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        if entries.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }

        info!("Append batch: {} entries", entries.len());

        for entry in &entries {
            let encoded = encode_committed_entry(entry)?;
            self.wal.append(&encoded)?;
        }

        for entry in &entries {
            let current_meta = {
                let guard = self.current_segment.read();
                guard.meta_snapshot()
            };
            if current_meta.is_full(self.config.segment_size_limit) {
                self.rotate_segment()?;
            }
            {
                let guard = self.current_segment.read();
                guard.append_entry(entry)?;
            }
            self.next_index.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        }

        if self.config.verify_checksums {
            self.wal.sync()?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 获取条目总数。
    fn entry_count(&self) -> LingXuResult<u64> {
        Ok(self.next_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst))
    }

    /// 按索引读取条目。
    fn read(&self, index: EntryIndex) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Committed>> {
        self.read_entry(index)
    }
}


// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §22 幽径遍历 — 认知遍历 trait
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 认知遍历 trait：对账本进行多种认知模式的遍历。
///
/// 四种遍历模式：
/// - 反思遍历（Reflective）：检测异常条目
/// - 巩固遍历（Consolidation）：滑动窗口合并相似条目
/// - 好奇遍历（Curiosity）：收集好奇探测条目
/// - 叙事遍历（Narrative）：收集故事条目
pub trait 幽径遍历: 幽典契 {
    /// 反思遍历：扫描整个账本，检测异常条目。
    ///
    /// 检测以下异常：
    /// - 哈希为空（EMPTY_HASH）
    /// - 有效载荷为空（MetaOp 和 ForkMarker 除外）
    /// - 有签名但无私钥
    fn run_reflective_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>>;

    /// 巩固遍历：滑动窗口合并连续相同类型的条目。
    ///
    /// 当窗口内所有条目类型相同且数量 >= 3 时，将它们合并为一个摘要条目。
    fn run_consolidation_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>>;

    /// 好奇遍历：收集所有 CuriosityProbe 类型的条目。
    fn run_curiosity_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>>;

    /// 叙事遍历：收集所有 Story 类型的条目。
    fn run_narrative_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>>;

    /// 综合认知遍历：运行所有四种遍历模式，返回汇总报告。
    fn run_full_cognitive_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<CognitiveReport> {
        let reflective = self.run_reflective_pass()?;
        let consolidation = self.run_consolidation_pass()?;
        let curiosity = self.run_curiosity_pass()?;
        let narrative = self.run_narrative_pass()?;

        let total_entries = self.entry_count()?;

        Ok(CognitiveReport {
            total_entries,
            reflective_anomalies: reflective.len(),
            consolidation_groups: consolidation.len(),
            curiosity_probes: curiosity.len(),
            narrative_stories: narrative.len(),
            anomalies: reflective,
            consolidated: consolidation,
            curiosity_entries: curiosity,
            narrative_entries: narrative,
        })
    }
}

/// 认知遍历综合报告。
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CognitiveReport {
    /// 账本条目总数
    pub total_entries: u64,
    /// 反思遍历发现的异常数
    pub reflective_anomalies: usize,
    /// 巩固遍历发现的合并组数
    pub consolidation_groups: usize,
    /// 好奇探测条目数
    pub curiosity_probes: usize,
    /// 叙事故事条目数
    pub narrative_stories: usize,
    /// 异常条目详情
    pub anomalies: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>,
    /// 可合并条目详情
    pub consolidated: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>,
    /// 好奇探测条目详情
    pub curiosity_entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>,
    /// 叙事故事条目详情
    pub narrative_entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>,
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 幽径遍历 for 幽典录
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

impl 幽径遍历 for 幽典录 {
    /// 反思遍历：检测异常条目。
    ///
    /// 异常规则：
    /// 1. 条目哈希为 EMPTY_HASH（零哈希）
    /// 2. 有效载荷为空（MetaOp 和 ForkMarker 类型除外）
    /// 3. 有签名但缺少签名者公钥
    fn run_reflective_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let mut anomalies: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::new();
        let count = self.entry_count()?;

        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(anomalies);
        }

        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            // 规则 1: 哈希为空
            if entry.hash == EMPTY_HASH {
                anomalies.push(entry);
                return Ok(());
            }

            // 规则 2: 有效载荷为空（排除允许空的类型）
            if entry.payload.is_empty()
                && entry.entry_type != EntryType::MetaOp
                && entry.entry_type != EntryType::ForkMarker
            {
                anomalies.push(entry);
                return Ok(());
            }

            // 规则 3: 有签名但无私钥
            if entry.signature.is_some() && entry.signer_pk.is_none() {
                anomalies.push(entry);
                return Ok(());
            }

            Ok(())
        })?;

        info!(
            "Reflective pass complete: {} anomalies found in {} entries",
            anomalies.len(),
            count
        );

        Ok(anomalies)
    }

    /// 巩固遍历：滑动窗口合并连续相同类型的条目。
    ///
    /// 算法：
    /// 1. 使用大小为 5 的滑动窗口遍历所有条目
    /// 2. 当窗口内所有条目类型相同时，标记为可合并组
    /// 3. 返回每组的首个条目作为代表
    fn run_consolidation_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let mut consolidated: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::new();
        let count = self.entry_count()?;

        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(consolidated);
        }

        const WINDOW_SIZE: usize = 5;

        // 收集所有条目
        let mut all_entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::with_capacity(count as usize);
        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            all_entries.push(entry);
            Ok(())
        })?;

        if all_entries.len() < WINDOW_SIZE {
            return Ok(consolidated);
        }

        // 滑动窗口检测
        let mut i = 0;
        while i + WINDOW_SIZE <= all_entries.len() {
            let window_type = &all_entries[i].entry_type;
            let window_matches = all_entries[i..i + WINDOW_SIZE]
                .iter()
                .all(|e| e.entry_type == *window_type);

            if window_matches {
                // 找到连续相同类型的窗口，取首条作为代表
                consolidated.push(all_entries[i].clone());
                // 跳过整个窗口（避免重复检测）
                i += WINDOW_SIZE;
            } else {
                i += 1;
            }
        }

        info!(
            "Consolidation pass complete: {} consolidation groups found in {} entries",
            consolidated.len(),
            count
        );

        Ok(consolidated)
    }

    /// 好奇遍历：收集所有 CuriosityProbe 类型的条目。
    fn run_curiosity_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let mut probes: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::new();
        let count = self.entry_count()?;

        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(probes);
        }

        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            if entry.entry_type == EntryType::CuriosityProbe {
                probes.push(entry);
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;

        info!(
            "Curiosity pass complete: {} curiosity probes found in {} entries",
            probes.len(),
            count
        );

        Ok(probes)
    }

    /// 叙事遍历：收集所有 Story 类型的条目。
    fn run_narrative_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let mut stories: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::new();
        let count = self.entry_count()?;

        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(stories);
        }

        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            if entry.entry_type == EntryType::Story {
                stories.push(entry);
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;

        info!(
            "Narrative pass complete: {} stories found in {} entries",
            stories.len(),
            count
        );

        Ok(stories)
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §23 铭契 trait — 签名后端抽象
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 签名后端抽象 trait。
///
/// 支持软件签名（ed25519）、硬件签名（HSM）、远程签名等多种实现。
pub trait 铭契: Send + Sync {
    /// 对数据签名，返回签名字节。
    fn sign(&self, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<Vec<u8>>;

    /// 获取公钥字节（32 字节 Ed25519 公钥）。
    fn public_key_bytes(&self) -> [u8; 32];

    /// 获取签名器类型标识。
    fn signer_type(&self) -> &'static str;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// §24 灵契之铭 — 软件 Ed25519 签名器
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 基于 ed25519_dalek 的软件签名器。
///
/// 使用 OS 随机数生成器生成密钥，或使用种子确定性创建密钥。
pub struct 灵契之铭 {
    signing_key: SigningKey,
    verifying_key: VerifyingKey,
}

impl 灵契之铭 {
    /// 从种子确定性创建签名器。
    ///
    /// 相同的种子总是产生相同的密钥对，适用于测试和可重现场景。
    pub fn from_seed(seed: &[u8; 32]) -> Self {
        let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed);
        let verifying_key = signing_key.verifying_key();
        Self {
            signing_key,
            verifying_key,
        }
    }

    /// 生成新的随机签名器。
    ///
    /// 使用操作系统提供的 CSPRNG。
    pub fn generate_new() -> Self {
        use rand::rngs::OsRng;
        use rand::RngCore;
        let mut rng = OsRng;
        let mut seed = [0u8; 32];
        rng.fill_bytes(&mut seed);
        let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed);
        let verifying_key = signing_key.verifying_key();
        Self {
            signing_key,
            verifying_key,
        }
    }

    /// 从已有的 SigningKey 创建。
    pub fn from_signing_key(signing_key: SigningKey) -> Self {
        let verifying_key = signing_key.verifying_key();
        Self {
            signing_key,
            verifying_key,
        }
    }

    /// 获取签名密钥的原始字节。
    pub fn signing_key_bytes(&self) -> [u8; 32] {
        self.signing_key.to_bytes()
    }

    /// 获取验证密钥。
    pub fn verifying_key(&self) -> &VerifyingKey {
        &self.verifying_key
    }

    /// 获取签名密钥的引用。
    pub fn signing_key(&self) -> &SigningKey {
        &self.signing_key
    }
}

impl 铭契 for 灵契之铭 {
    /// 使用 Ed25519 对数据进行签名。
    fn sign(&self, data: &[u8]) -> LingXuResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let signature = self.signing_key.sign(data);
        Ok(signature.to_bytes().to_vec())
    }

    /// 返回 32 字节 Ed25519 公钥。
    fn public_key_bytes(&self) -> [u8; 32] {
        self.verifying_key.to_bytes()
    }

    /// 返回签名器类型标识。
    fn signer_type(&self) -> &'static str {
        "software_ed25519"
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// Debug 与 Display 辅助
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

impl fmt::Debug for 灵契之铭 {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("灵契之铭")
            .field("signer_type", &self.signer_type())
            .field("public_key", &bytes_to_hex(&self.public_key_bytes()))
            .finish()
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// 类型别名与便捷导出
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 幽典录 的类型别名（便捷使用）。
pub type StandardLedger = 幽典录;

/// 默认使用的签名器类型。
pub type DefaultSigner = 灵契之铭;

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// 幽典契Ext — 幽典契 扩展 trait
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=

/// 幽典契 扩展 trait：提供额外便利方法。
pub trait 幽典契Ext: 幽典契 {
    /// 读取最后 N 个条目。
    fn read_last_n(&self, n: usize) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        let start = count.saturating_sub(n as u64);
        let mut entries = Vec::with_capacity(n);
        self.replay(start..count, |entry| {
            entries.push(entry);
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(entries)
    }

    /// 查找指定类型的所有条目。
    fn find_by_type(&self, entry_type: EntryType) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        let mut entries = Vec::new();
        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            if entry.entry_type == entry_type {
                entries.push(entry);
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(entries)
    }

    /// 查找包含指定标签的条目。
    fn find_by_tag(&self, tag: &str) -> LingXuResult<Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>> {
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        let mut entries = Vec::new();
        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            if entry.tags.iter().any(|t| t == tag) {
                entries.push(entry);
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(entries)
    }

    /// 统计各类型条目数量。
    fn count_by_type(&self) -> LingXuResult<BTreeMap<EntryType, u64>> {
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        let mut counts: BTreeMap<EntryType, u64> = BTreeMap::new();
        self.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            *counts.entry(entry.entry_type).or_insert(0) += 1;
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(counts)
    }

    /// 验证从指定索引开始的链条。
    fn verify_chain_from(&self, start_index: EntryIndex) -> LingXuResult<bool> {
        let count = self.entry_count()?;
        if start_index >= count {
            return Ok(true);
        }

        let mut prev_hash = if start_index == 0 {
            EMPTY_HASH
        } else {
            match self.read(start_index.saturating_sub(1)) {
                Ok(entry) => entry.hash,
                Err(_) => EMPTY_HASH,
            }
        };

        let mut all_valid = true;
        self.replay(start_index..count, |entry| {
            let recomputed = compute_hash(
                entry.index,
                entry.entry_type.as_u8(),
                &entry.payload,
                &entry.prev_hash,
                entry.timestamp,
            );
            if recomputed != entry.hash {
                all_valid = false;
                return Ok(());
            }
            if entry.prev_hash != prev_hash {
                all_valid = false;
                return Ok(());
            }
            prev_hash = entry.hash;
            Ok(())
        })?;

        Ok(all_valid)
    }
}

// 为所有实现 幽典契 的类型自动实现 幽典契Ext
impl<T: 幽典契> 幽典契Ext for T {}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=
// 测试模块（条件编译）
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════=




// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ── 幽冥律令与灵魂星图 ──
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §25  EthicsDecision — 伦理决策枚举（奇迹化4变体 + Display）
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EthicsDecision {
    Allow,
    RequireReview { reason: String, reviewer_count: usize },
    Deny { reason: String },
    RequireNegotiation { participants: Vec<String> },
}

impl Display for EthicsDecision {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            EthicsDecision::Allow => write!(f, "Allow"),
            EthicsDecision::RequireReview { reason, reviewer_count } => {
                write!(f, "RequireReview(reason={}, reviewers={})", reason, reviewer_count)
            }
            EthicsDecision::Deny { reason } => {
                write!(f, "Deny(reason={})", reason)
            }
            EthicsDecision::RequireNegotiation { participants } => {
                write!(f, "RequireNegotiation(participants={:?})", participants)
            }
        }
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §26  幽冥律令 trait — 伦理治理器接口
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

pub trait 幽冥律令: Send + Sync {
    /// 对某个操作进行伦理评估，返回决策
    fn evaluate(&self, operation: &str, context: &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision>;
    /// 返回当前规则摘要
    fn rules_summary(&self) -> String;
    /// 添加一条规则（文本描述 + 优先级）
    fn add_rule(&self, rule: &str, priority: u8) -> LingXuResult<()>;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §27  Simple幽冥律令 — 模式匹配版伦理治理器
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

pub struct Simple幽冥律令 {
    rules: PLRwLock<Vec<(String, u8)>>,
    deny_patterns: Vec<String>,
    require_review_patterns: Vec<String>,
}

impl Simple幽冥律令 {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            rules: PLRwLock::new(Vec::new()),
            deny_patterns: vec![
                "delete_all".to_string(),
                "wipe".to_string(),
                "shutdown_cluster".to_string(),
                "destroy".to_string(),
                "purge".to_string(),
            ],
            require_review_patterns: vec![
                "modify_ledger".to_string(),
                "fork_chain".to_string(),
                "change_governance".to_string(),
                "override".to_string(),
                "bypass".to_string(),
            ],
        }
    }

    pub fn with_default_rules() -> Self {
        let gov = Self::new();
        {
            let mut rules = gov.rules.write();
            rules.push(("deny_destructive_operations".to_string(), 255));
            rules.push(("require_review_for_governance_changes".to_string(), 200));
            rules.push(("allow_general_operations".to_string(), 1));
        }
        gov
    }

    pub fn default() -> Self {
        Self::with_default_rules()
    }
}

impl 幽冥律令 for Simple幽冥律令 {
    fn evaluate(&self, operation: &str, _context: &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision> {
        let op_lower = operation.to_lowercase();

        // 第一步：检查 deny 模式
        for pattern in &self.deny_patterns {
            if op_lower.contains(pattern) {
                return Ok(EthicsDecision::Deny {
                    reason: format!("Operation '{}' matches forbidden pattern '{}'", operation, pattern),
                });
            }
        }

        // 第二步：检查 require_review 模式
        for pattern in &self.require_review_patterns {
            if op_lower.contains(pattern) {
                return Ok(EthicsDecision::RequireReview {
                    reason: format!("Operation '{}' requires review due to pattern '{}'", operation, pattern),
                    reviewer_count: 2,
                });
            }
        }

        // 第三步：默认 Allow
        Ok(EthicsDecision::Allow)
    }

    fn rules_summary(&self) -> String {
        let rules = self.rules.read();
        let deny_count = self.deny_patterns.len();
        let review_count = self.require_review_patterns.len();
        format!(
            "Simple幽冥律令: {} explicit rules, {} deny_patterns, {} require_review_patterns",
            rules.len(), deny_count, review_count
        )
    }

    fn add_rule(&self, rule: &str, priority: u8) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let mut rules = self.rules.write();
        rules.push((rule.to_string(), priority));
        // 如果规则以 "deny:" 开头，同时加入 deny_patterns
        if rule.starts_with("deny:") {
            let pattern = rule.trim_start_matches("deny:").trim().to_string();
            if !pattern.is_empty() {
                drop(rules);
                // SAFETY: we dropped the lock; re-acquire for pattern update would need mut self,
                // so we store in the rules list only. The patterns are checked from the list.
                let _ = pattern;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §28  EthicsEngine — 闭包规则 + 审计日志
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

pub struct EthicsEngine {
    rules: PLRwLock<Vec<(String, u8, Box<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool + Send + Sync>)>>,
    audit_log: PLMutex<Vec<(TimestampMs, String, EthicsDecision)>>,
}

impl EthicsEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            rules: PLRwLock::new(Vec::new()),
            audit_log: PLMutex::new(Vec::new()),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_default_rules() -> Self {
        let engine = Self::new();
        {
            let mut rules = engine.rules.write();
            // 规则1: no_destructive — 禁止破坏性操作
            rules.push((
                "no_destructive".to_string(),
                255,
                Box::new(|op: &str| {
                    let op_lower = op.to_lowercase();
                    op_lower.contains("delete_all") || op_lower.contains("wipe") || op_lower.contains("destroy")
                }),
            ));
            // 规则2: no_unauthorized_access — 禁止未授权访问
            rules.push((
                "no_unauthorized_access".to_string(),
                250,
                Box::new(|op: &str| {
                    let op_lower = op.to_lowercase();
                    op_lower.contains("unauthorized") || op_lower.contains("bypass_auth")
                }),
            ));
            // 规则3: require_audit — 需要审计的操作
            rules.push((
                "require_audit".to_string(),
                200,
                Box::new(|op: &str| {
                    let op_lower = op.to_lowercase();
                    op_lower.contains("modify_ledger") || op_lower.contains("fork_chain")
                }),
            ));
        }
        engine
    }

    pub fn audit_history(&self) -> Vec<(TimestampMs, String, EthicsDecision)> {
        self.audit_log.lock().clone()
    }

    fn log_decision(&self, operation: &str, decision: &EthicsDecision) {
        let ts = now_timestamp_ms();
        self.audit_log.lock().push((ts, operation.to_string(), decision.clone()));
    }
}

impl 幽冥律令 for EthicsEngine {
    fn evaluate(&self, operation: &str, _context: &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision> {
        let rules = self.rules.read();

        for (rule_name, _priority, matcher) in rules.iter() {
            if matcher(operation) {
                let decision = if rule_name.starts_with("no_") {
                    EthicsDecision::Deny {
                        reason: format!("Rule '{}' matched operation '{}'", rule_name, operation),
                    }
                } else {
                    EthicsDecision::RequireReview {
                        reason: format!("Rule '{}' requires review for operation '{}'", rule_name, operation),
                        reviewer_count: 2,
                    }
                };
                self.log_decision(operation, &decision);
                return Ok(decision);
            }
        }

        let decision = EthicsDecision::Allow;
        self.log_decision(operation, &decision);
        Ok(decision)
    }

    fn rules_summary(&self) -> String {
        let rules = self.rules.read();
        let audit_count = self.audit_log.lock().len();
        format!("EthicsEngine: {} closure rules, {} audit log entries", rules.len(), audit_count)
    }

    fn add_rule(&self, rule: &str, priority: u8) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let rule_owned = rule.to_string();
        let matcher: Box<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool + Send + Sync> = Box::new(move |op: &str| {
            op.to_lowercase().contains(&rule_owned.to_lowercase())
        });
        self.rules.write().push((rule.to_string(), priority, matcher));
        Ok(())
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §29  AdvancedEthics + BasicEthics — 动态闭包规则与基础伦理
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

///  — 动态闭包规则伦理治理器
/// 每条规则都是一个完整的闭包，可访问 operation 和 context
pub struct AdvancedEthics {
    rules: PLMutex<Vec<Box<dyn Fn(&str, &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision> + Send + Sync>>>,
}

impl AdvancedEthics {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            rules: PLMutex::new(Vec::new()),
        }
    }

    /// 添加一条动态闭包规则
    pub fn add_rule<F: Fn(&str, &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision> + Send + Sync + 'static>(
        &self,
        f: F,
    ) {
        self.rules.lock().push(Box::new(f));
    }
}

impl 幽冥律令 for AdvancedEthics {
    fn evaluate(&self, operation: &str, context: &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision> {
        let rules = self.rules.lock();
        if rules.is_empty() {
            return Ok(EthicsDecision::Allow);
        }

        for rule in rules.iter() {
            match rule(operation, context) {
                Ok(EthicsDecision::Allow) => {
                    // Allow 意味着继续检查下一条规则
                    continue;
                }
                Ok(decision @ EthicsDecision::Deny { .. }) => return Ok(decision),
                Ok(decision @ EthicsDecision::RequireReview { .. }) => return Ok(decision),
                Ok(decision @ EthicsDecision::RequireNegotiation { .. }) => return Ok(decision),
                Err(e) => return Err(e),
            }
        }

        // 所有规则都通过了，允许操作
        Ok(EthicsDecision::Allow)
    }

    fn rules_summary(&self) -> String {
        let rules = self.rules.lock();
        format!("AdvancedEthics: {} dynamic closure rules", rules.len())
    }

    fn add_rule(&self, _rule: &str, _priority: u8) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // AdvancedEthics 使用专用的 add_rule<F> 方法添加闭包
        // 文本版本的 add_rule 为空实现
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// 基础伦理治理器 — 简单的关键词过滤
pub struct BasicEthics;

impl 幽冥律令 for BasicEthics {
    fn evaluate(&self, operation: &str, _context: &ContextMeta) -> LingXuResult<EthicsDecision> {
        let op_lower = operation.to_lowercase();
        if op_lower.contains("private") || op_lower.contains("sensitive") {
            return Ok(EthicsDecision::Deny {
                reason: format!("Operation '{}' involves private or sensitive content", operation),
            });
        }
        Ok(EthicsDecision::Allow)
    }

    fn rules_summary(&self) -> String {
        "BasicEthics: 2 built-in rules (private-block, sensitive-block)".to_string()
    }

    fn add_rule(&self, _rule: &str, _priority: u8) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        Ok(())
    }
}


#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct QueryResult {
    pub id: String,
    pub distance: f32,
    pub score: f64,
    pub vec: Vec<f32>,
}

impl QueryResult {
    pub fn from_distance(id: String, distance: f32, vec: Vec<f32>) -> Self {
        let score = 1.0 / (1.0 + distance as f64);
        Self { id, distance, score, vec }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct HnswNode {
    pub nid: u64,
    pub neighbors: Vec<u64>,
    pub vector: Vec<f32>,
}

impl HnswNode {
    pub fn new(nid: u64, vector: Vec<f32>) -> Self {
        Self {
            nid,
            neighbors: Vec::new(),
            vector,
        }
    }
}

/// HNSW 索引快照，用于持久化和传输
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct HnswSnapshot {
    pub nodes: Vec<HnswNode>,
    pub id_map_keys: Vec<String>,
    pub id_map_vals: Vec<u64>,
    pub max_elements: usize,
    pub m: usize,
    pub ef_construction: usize,
    pub ef_search: usize,
    pub dimension: usize,
    pub metric: MetricType,
    pub normalize: bool,
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §32  幽冥倒影 — 幽冥倒影双向 PLRwLock 映射 + compact
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

pub struct 幽冥倒影 {
    str_to_num: PLRwLock<HashMap<String, u64>>,
    num_to_str: PLRwLock<HashMap<u64, String>>,
    counter: AtomicU64,
}

impl 幽冥倒影 {
    pub fn new(expected: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            str_to_num: PLRwLock::new(HashMap::with_capacity(expected)),
            num_to_str: PLRwLock::new(HashMap::with_capacity(expected)),
            counter: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// 获取或插入 key，返回对应的数字 ID
    pub fn get_or_insert(&self, key: &str) -> u64 {
        // 第一层：读锁快速路径
        {
            let map = self.str_to_num.read();
            if let Some(&v) = map.get(key) {
                return v;
            }
        }

        // 第二层：写锁插入
        let mut map = self.str_to_num.write();
        // 双重检查
        if let Some(&v) = map.get(key) {
            return v;
        }

        let id = self.counter.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        map.insert(key.to_string(), id);
        self.num_to_str.write().insert(id, key.to_string());
        id
    }

    /// 查找 key 对应的数字 ID，不存在返回 None
    pub fn lookup(&self, key: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        self.str_to_num.read().get(key).copied()
    }

    /// 反向查找数字 ID 对应的字符串 key
    pub fn reverse_lookup(&self, id: u64) -> Option<String> {
        self.num_to_str.read().get(&id).cloned()
    }

    /// 移除一个 key，返回其数字 ID（如果存在）
    pub fn remove(&self, key: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        let mut map = self.str_to_num.write();
        let id = map.remove(key);
        if let Some(id_val) = id {
            self.num_to_str.write().remove(&id_val);
        }
        id
    }

    /// 获取当前映射的快照副本
    pub fn compact(&self) -> HashMap<String, u64> {
        self.str_to_num.read().clone()
    }

    /// 返回映射中的条目数量
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.str_to_num.read().len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.len() == 0
    }

    /// 序列化为 (keys, vals) 对
    pub fn to_serializable(&self) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<u64>) {
        let map = self.str_to_num.read();
        let mut keys = Vec::with_capacity(map.len());
        let mut vals = Vec::with_capacity(map.len());
        for (k, v) in map.iter() {
            keys.push(k.clone());
            vals.push(*v);
        }
        (keys, vals)
    }

    /// 从序列化对重建
    pub fn from_serializable(keys: Vec<String>, vals: Vec<u64>, expected: usize) -> Self {
        let mut map = HashMap::with_capacity(expected);
        let mut reverse = HashMap::with_capacity(expected);
        let mut max_id = 0u64;
        for (k, v) in keys.into_iter().zip(vals.into_iter()) {
            if v > max_id {
                max_id = v;
            }
            map.insert(k.clone(), v);
            reverse.insert(v, k);
        }
        Self {
            str_to_num: PLRwLock::new(map),
            num_to_str: PLRwLock::new(reverse),
            counter: AtomicU64::new(max_id + 1),
        }
    }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §33  幽影星图完整分层 HNSW 向量索引
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// 内部节点结构：包含向量、多层邻居列表和删除标记
struct Node {
    vec: Vec<f32>,
    neighbors: Vec<Vec<usize>>,
    deleted: bool,
}

/// 距离排序节点（用于优先队列）
/// PartialOrd 反向排序 → BinaryHeap 变成 min-heap，距离小的优先
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct DistNode {
    distance: f32,
    nid: u64,
}

impl PartialEq for DistNode {
    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        self.distance == other.distance && self.nid == other.nid
    }
}

impl Eq for DistNode {}

impl PartialOrd for DistNode {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {
        // 反向排序：distance 小的优先级高（BinaryHeap 是 max-heap）
        other.distance.partial_cmp(&self.distance)
    }
}

impl Ord for DistNode {
    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
        self.partial_cmp(other).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
    }
}

/// 反向距离节点（用于 max-heap 结果集）
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct ReverseDistNode {
    distance: f32,
    idx: usize,
}

impl PartialEq for ReverseDistNode {
    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        self.distance == other.distance && self.idx == other.idx
    }
}

impl Eq for ReverseDistNode {}

impl PartialOrd for ReverseDistNode {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {
        self.distance.partial_cmp(&other.distance)
    }
}

impl Ord for ReverseDistNode {
    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
        self.partial_cmp(other).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
    }
}

pub struct 幽影星图 {
    nodes: Vec<PLMutex<Option<Node>>>,
    id_map: PLMutex<幽冥倒影>,
    max_elements: usize,
    dimension: usize,
    m: usize,
    ef_construction: usize,
    pub ef_search: usize,
    metric: MetricType,
    entry_point: AtomicU64,
    normalize: bool,
    next_slot: AtomicU64,
    deleted_count: AtomicU64,
    rng: PLMutex<StdRng>,
}

impl 幽影星图 {
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 构造与基础方法
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn new(
        max_elements: usize,
        dimension: usize,
        m: usize,
        ef_construction: usize,
        metric: MetricType,
        normalize: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        // 用 SystemTime 生成随机种子初始化 StdRng
        let seed = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_nanos() as u64;
        let rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(seed);

        // 预分配 nodes 数组
        let mut nodes = Vec::with_capacity(max_elements);
        for _ in 0..max_elements {
            nodes.push(PLMutex::new(None));
        }

        Self {
            nodes,
            id_map: PLMutex::new(幽冥倒影::new(max_elements)),
            max_elements,
            dimension,
            m,
            ef_construction,
            ef_search: 64, // 默认搜索参数
            metric,
            entry_point: AtomicU64::new(u64::MAX),
            normalize,
            next_slot: AtomicU64::new(0),
            deleted_count: AtomicU64::new(0),
            rng: PLMutex::new(rng),
        }
    }

    /// 生成随机层级（指数分布）
    fn rand_level(&self) -> usize {
        let mut rng = self.rng.lock();
        let mut level: usize = 0;
        const MAX_LEVEL: usize = 16;
        while rng.gen::<f64>() < 0.5 && level < MAX_LEVEL {
            level += 1;
        }
        level
    }

    /// 向量 L2 归一化
    fn normalize_vec(&self, vec: &mut [f32]) {
        let mut norm_sq = 0.0f32;
        for &v in vec.iter() {
            norm_sq += v * v;
        }
        let norm = norm_sq.sqrt();
        if norm > 1e-10 {
            for v in vec.iter_mut() {
                *v /= norm;
            }
        }
    }

    /// 计算两个已存储节点之间的距离
    fn node_distance(&self, a_idx: usize, b_idx: usize) -> f32 {
        let a_vec = self.get_vec(a_idx);
        let b_vec = self.get_vec(b_idx);
        if a_vec.is_empty() || b_vec.is_empty() {
            return f32::MAX;
        }
        self.metric.distance(&a_vec, &b_vec)
    }

    /// 原始向量距离计算
    fn distance_raw(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        self.metric.distance(a, b)
    }

    /// 获取节点的向量副本
    fn get_vec(&self, idx: usize) -> Vec<f32> {
        self.nodes[idx].lock().as_ref().map(|n| n.vec.clone()).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// 获取节点在指定层级的邻居列表
    fn get_neighbors(&self, idx: usize, level: usize) -> Vec<usize> {
        self.nodes[idx]
            .lock()
            .as_ref()
            .map(|n| n.neighbors.get(level).cloned().unwrap_or_default())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// 设置节点在指定层级的邻居列表
    fn set_neighbors(&self, idx: usize, level: usize, neighbors: Vec<usize>) {
        let mut node = self.nodes[idx].lock();
        if let Some(ref mut n) = *node {
            while n.neighbors.len() <= level {
                n.neighbors.push(Vec::new());
            }
            n.neighbors[level] = neighbors;
        }
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 核心搜索算法：贪心束搜索
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /// 在指定层级执行贪心束搜索
    ///
    /// 算法说明：
    /// - candidates: min-heap（通过 Reverse），待探索节点，距离近的优先
    /// - results: max-heap，当前找到的最佳 ef 个结果，距离远的在顶部（方便淘汰）
    /// - visited: HashSet，防止重复访问
    ///
    /// 返回按距离升序排列的 (distance, node_idx) 列表
    fn search_layer(&self, query: &[f32], entry: usize, ef: usize, level: usize) -> Vec<(f32, usize)> {
        let mut visited: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
        // candidates: min-heap，距离小的优先探索
        let mut candidates: BinaryHeap<DistNode> = BinaryHeap::new();
        // results: max-heap，存储当前最佳结果，距离大的在顶部
        let mut results: BinaryHeap<ReverseDistNode> = BinaryHeap::new();

        // 初始化：从 entry 点开始
        let entry_dist = if entry < self.nodes.len() {
            let entry_vec = self.get_vec(entry);
            if entry_vec.is_empty() {
                return Vec::new();
            }
            self.metric.distance(query, &entry_vec)
        } else {
            return Vec::new();
        };

        visited.insert(entry);
        candidates.push(DistNode { distance: entry_dist, nid: entry as u64 });
        results.push(ReverseDistNode { distance: entry_dist, idx: entry });

        // 贪心扩展循环
        while let Some(current) = candidates.pop() {
            // 获取当前 results 中最差的距离
            let worst_result_dist = results.peek().map(|r| r.distance).unwrap_or(f32::MAX);

            // 终止条件：当前节点的距离已超过结果集中最差的距离
            if current.distance > worst_result_dist {
                break;
            }

            let current_idx = current.nid as usize;

            // 遍历当前节点的所有邻居
            let neighbors = self.get_neighbors(current_idx, level);
            for &neighbor_idx in &neighbors {
                if neighbor_idx == current_idx {
                    continue;
                }
                if visited.contains(&neighbor_idx) {
                    continue;
                }
                visited.insert(neighbor_idx);

                let neighbor_vec = self.get_vec(neighbor_idx);
                if neighbor_vec.is_empty() {
                    continue;
                }

                let dist = self.metric.distance(query, &neighbor_vec);

                // 如果结果集未满，或者距离优于当前最差结果
                if results.len() < ef {
                    candidates.push(DistNode { distance: dist, nid: neighbor_idx as u64 });
                    results.push(ReverseDistNode { distance: dist, idx: neighbor_idx });
                } else if let Some(worst) = results.peek() {
                    if dist < worst.distance {
                        // 替换最差结果
                        candidates.push(DistNode { distance: dist, nid: neighbor_idx as u64 });
                        results.push(ReverseDistNode { distance: dist, idx: neighbor_idx });
                        if results.len() > ef {
                            // 弹出最差的结果
                            let mut temp = Vec::with_capacity(results.len());
                            while let Some(r) = results.pop() {
                                temp.push(r);
                            }
                            // 按距离升序排序，保留前 ef 个
                            temp.sort_by(|a, b| a.distance.partial_cmp(&b.distance).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
                            results.clear();
                            for r in temp.into_iter().take(ef) {
                                results.push(r);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 将结果转换为 Vec 并按距离升序排序
        let mut result_vec: Vec<(f32, usize)> = results
            .into_iter()
            .map(|r| (r.distance, r.idx))
            .collect();
        result_vec.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.partial_cmp(&b.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
        result_vec
    }

    /// 从候选列表中选择前 m 个邻居
    fn select_neighbors(&self, candidates: &[(f32, usize)], m: usize) -> Vec<usize> {
        candidates.iter().take(m).map(|(_, id)| *id).collect()
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 添加向量
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn add(&self, id: &str, mut vec: Vec<f32>) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        // 1. 检查维度
        if vec.len() != self.dimension {
            return Err(LingXuError::DimensionMismatch {
                expected: self.dimension,
                got: vec.len(),
            });
        }

        // 2. 归一化
        if self.normalize {
            self.normalize_vec(&mut vec);
        }

        // 3. 获取/分配数字 ID
        let num_id = {
            let id_map = self.id_map.lock();
            id_map.get_or_insert(id)
        };

        // 检查是否超过容量
        let slot = num_id as usize;
        if slot >= self.max_elements {
            return Err(LingXuError::HnswError(format!(
                "Max elements exceeded: {} >= {}", slot, self.max_elements
            )));
        }

        // 4. 生成随机层级
        let node_level = self.rand_level();

        // 5. 创建 Node
        let mut neighbors: Vec<Vec<usize>> = Vec::with_capacity(node_level + 1);
        for _ in 0..=node_level {
            neighbors.push(Vec::new());
        }

        let node = Node {
            vec: vec.clone(),
            neighbors,
            deleted: false,
        };

        // 写入节点槽位
        {
            let mut slot_lock = self.nodes[slot].lock();
            *slot_lock = Some(node);
        }

        // 更新 next_slot（确保它至少为 slot+1）
        let current_next = self.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        if slot as u64 >= current_next {
            let _ = self.next_slot.compare_exchange(
                current_next,
                (slot + 1) as u64,
                AtomicOrdering::SeqCst,
                AtomicOrdering::SeqCst,
            );
        }

        // 6. 处理 entry_point
        let ep = self.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        if ep == u64::MAX {
            // 第一个节点
            self.entry_point.store(num_id, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            return Ok(());
        }

        // 7. 从顶层到第 0 层逐层搜索 + 连接邻居
        let mut curr_ep = ep as usize;

        // 获取当前 entry_point 的最大层级
        let ep_max_level = {
            let node_lock = self.nodes[curr_ep].lock();
            node_lock.as_ref().map(|n| n.neighbors.len().saturating_sub(1)).unwrap_or(0)
        };

        // 从最高层到第 1 层：只找最近的一个点
        let node_max_level = node_level;
        for level in (1..=ep_max_level.max(node_max_level)).rev() {
            if level > node_max_level {
                // 在新节点层级之上，只需更新 entry point
                let res = self.search_layer(&vec, curr_ep, 1, level);
                if let Some(&(_, best_idx)) = res.first() {
                    curr_ep = best_idx;
                }
            }
        }

        // 从 min(node_level, ep_max_level) 层到第 0 层：ef 搜索 + 连接邻居
        let start_level = node_max_level.min(ep_max_level);
        for level in (0..=start_level).rev() {
            let ef = if level == 0 { self.ef_construction.max(self.m) } else { self.ef_construction };
            let neighbors_found = self.search_layer(&vec, curr_ep, ef, level);
            let selected = self.select_neighbors(&neighbors_found, self.m);

            // 设置新节点的邻居
            self.set_neighbors(slot, level, selected.clone());

            // 双向连接：更新已有节点的邻居列表
            let m_max = self.m * 2;
            for &neighbor_idx in &selected {
                if neighbor_idx == slot {
                    continue;
                }

                // 获取邻居当前的邻居列表
                let mut neighbor_neighbors = self.get_neighbors(neighbor_idx, level);

                // 如果已经包含则不重复添加
                if !neighbor_neighbors.contains(&slot) {
                    neighbor_neighbors.push(slot);

                    // 如果超过 m*2，截断到 m*2（保留距离最近的）
                    if neighbor_neighbors.len() > m_max {
                        // 计算每个候选的距离
                        let mut with_dist: Vec<(f32, usize)> = neighbor_neighbors
                            .iter()
                            .map(|&idx| {
                                let dist = if idx == slot {
                                    self.metric.distance(&vec, &self.get_vec(idx))
                                } else {
                                    self.node_distance(neighbor_idx, idx)
                                };
                                (dist, idx)
                            })
                            .collect();
                        with_dist.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.partial_cmp(&b.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
                        neighbor_neighbors = with_dist.into_iter().take(m_max).map(|(_, idx)| idx).collect();
                    }

                    self.set_neighbors(neighbor_idx, level, neighbor_neighbors);
                }
            }

            // 更新当前 entry point 为最近的点
            if let Some(&(_, best_idx)) = neighbors_found.first() {
                curr_ep = best_idx;
            }
        }

        // 8. 如果新节点的层级高于 entry_point，更新 entry_point
        if node_level > ep_max_level {
            self.entry_point.store(num_id, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 删除向量（逻辑删除）
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn delete(&self, id: &str) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let num_id_opt = {
            let id_map = self.id_map.lock();
            id_map.lookup(id)
        };

        match num_id_opt {
            Some(num_id) => {
                let slot = num_id as usize;
                if slot < self.nodes.len() {
                    let mut node_lock = self.nodes[slot].lock();
                    if let Some(ref mut node) = *node_lock {
                        node.deleted = true;
                        // 清空邻居引用以释放连接
                        for level_neighbors in node.neighbors.iter_mut() {
                            level_neighbors.clear();
                        }
                    }
                    // 将节点槽位置为 None（真正释放）
                    *node_lock = None;
                    drop(node_lock);

                    self.deleted_count.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);

                    // 从 id_map 中移除
                    {
                        let id_map = self.id_map.lock();
                        id_map.remove(id);
                    }

                    // 如果删除的是 entry_point，需要重新指定
                    let current_ep = self.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                    if current_ep == num_id {
                        // 找到下一个有效的 entry point
                        let next_slot = self.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                        let mut new_ep = u64::MAX;
                        for i in 0..next_slot {
                            let i = i as usize;
                            if i < self.nodes.len() {
                                let node_lock = self.nodes[i].lock();
                                if node_lock.is_some() {
                                    new_ep = i as u64;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        self.entry_point.store(new_ep, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                    }

                    Ok(())
                } else {
                    Err(LingXuError::HnswError(format!("Slot {} out of range", slot)))
                }
            }
            None => Err(LingXuError::IdNotFound(id.to_string())),
        }
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 查询最近邻
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn query(&self, vec: &[f32], k: usize) -> LingXuResult<Vec<QueryResult>> {
        // 1. 检查维度
        if vec.len() != self.dimension {
            return Err(LingXuError::DimensionMismatch {
                expected: self.dimension,
                got: vec.len(),
            });
        }

        // 2. 复制并归一化查询向量
        let mut query_vec = vec.to_vec();
        if self.normalize {
            self.normalize_vec(&mut query_vec);
        }

        // 3. 检查 entry point
        let ep = self.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        if ep == u64::MAX {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }

        // 4. 获取 entry_point 的最大层级并从顶层搜索
        let ep_idx = ep as usize;
        let ep_max_level = {
            let node_lock = self.nodes[ep_idx].lock();
            node_lock.as_ref().map(|n| n.neighbors.len().saturating_sub(1)).unwrap_or(0)
        };

        let mut curr_ep = ep_idx;

        // 从最高层到第 1 层：每层只找最近的一个
        for level in (1..=ep_max_level).rev() {
            let res = self.search_layer(&query_vec, curr_ep, 1, level);
            if let Some(&(_, best_idx)) = res.first() {
                curr_ep = best_idx;
            }
        }

        // 第 0 层：ef_search 搜索
        let ef = self.ef_search.max(k);
        let results = self.search_layer(&query_vec, curr_ep, ef, 0);

        // 5. 转换为 QueryResult
        let id_map = self.id_map.lock();
        let query_results: Vec<QueryResult> = results
            .into_iter()
            .take(k)
            .map(|(dist, idx)| {
                let node_vec = self.get_vec(idx);
                let id = id_map
                    .reverse_lookup(idx as u64)
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("node_{}", idx));
                QueryResult::from_distance(id, dist, node_vec)
            })
            .collect();

        Ok(query_results)
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 持久化（原子写入）
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn persist<P: AsRef<Path>>(&self, dir: P) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        let dir = dir.as_ref();

        // 1. 创建目录
        fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;

        // 2. 创建 .tmp 子目录
        let tmp_dir = dir.join(".tmp_persist");
        if tmp_dir.exists() {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&tmp_dir);
        }
        fs::create_dir_all(&tmp_dir)?;

        let next_slot_val = self.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        let dimension = self.dimension;

        // 3. 写 header 文件（next_slot, dimension, m, ef_construction, metric, normalize）
        {
            let header_path = tmp_dir.join("header.bin");
            let mut header_file = fs::File::create(&header_path)?;
            // 格式：next_slot(u64) + dimension(u64) + m(u64) + ef_construction(u64)
            //       + metric_type(u8) + normalize(u8) + entry_point(u64)
            header_file.write_all(&next_slot_val.to_le_bytes())?;
            header_file.write_all(&(dimension as u64).to_le_bytes())?;
            header_file.write_all(&(self.m as u64).to_le_bytes())?;
            header_file.write_all(&(self.ef_construction as u64).to_le_bytes())?;
            header_file.write_all(&(self.ef_search as u64).to_le_bytes())?;
            let metric_byte: u8 = match self.metric {
                MetricType::Euclidean => 0,
                MetricType::Cosine => 1,
                MetricType::DotProduct => 2,
                MetricType::Manhattan => 3,
                MetricType::L2 => 4,
                MetricType::InnerProduct => 5,
                MetricType::Hamming => 6,
                MetricType::Jaccard => 7,
            };
            header_file.write_all(&[metric_byte])?;
            header_file.write_all(&[if self.normalize { 1u8 } else { 0u8 }])?;
            let ep = self.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            header_file.write_all(&ep.to_le_bytes())?;
        }

        // 4. 写 vectors.bin（帧格式：[1字节存在标志][vec数据][neighbors数据]）
        {
            let vectors_path = tmp_dir.join("vectors.bin");
            let mut vec_file = fs::File::create(&vectors_path)?;

            let max_slot = next_slot_val as usize;
            for idx in 0..max_slot.max(1) {
                let node_lock = self.nodes[idx].lock();
                match *node_lock {
                    Some(ref node) => {
                        // 存在标志 = 1
                        vec_file.write_all(&[1u8])?;

                        // 写向量数据
                        for &v in &node.vec {
                            vec_file.write_all(&v.to_le_bytes())?;
                        }

                        // 写邻居数据：先写层级数
                        let num_levels = node.neighbors.len() as u64;
                        vec_file.write_all(&num_levels.to_le_bytes())?;

                        for level_neighbors in &node.neighbors {
                            let num_neighbors = level_neighbors.len() as u64;
                            vec_file.write_all(&num_neighbors.to_le_bytes())?;
                            for &neighbor_idx in level_neighbors {
                                vec_file.write_all(&(neighbor_idx as u64).to_le_bytes())?;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    None => {
                        // 存在标志 = 0（空槽位）
                        vec_file.write_all(&[0u8])?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 5. 写 idmap（serde_json 序列化映射）
        {
            let idmap_path = tmp_dir.join("idmap.json");
            let id_map = self.id_map.lock();
            let compact_map = id_map.compact();
            let json = serde_json::to_string(&compact_map)?;
            let mut idmap_file = fs::File::create(&idmap_path)?;
            idmap_file.write_all(json.as_bytes())?;
        }

        // 6. 原子 rename .tmp -> hnsw_data
        let final_dir = dir.join("hnsw_data");
        if final_dir.exists() {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&final_dir);
        }
        fs::rename(&tmp_dir, &final_dir)?;

        Ok(())
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 加载（反序列化）
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn load<P: AsRef<Path>>(dir: P, dimension: usize, metric: MetricType, normalize: bool) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        let dir = dir.as_ref().join("hnsw_data");

        if !dir.exists() {
            return Err(LingXuError::HnswError(format!("Persist directory {:?} does not exist", dir)));
        }

        // 1. 读取 header
        let header_path = dir.join("header.bin");
        let mut header_file = fs::File::open(&header_path)?;
        let mut header_buf = [0u8; 64];
        let header_len = header_file.read(&mut header_buf)?;
        if header_len < 8 {
            return Err(LingXuError::HnswError("Invalid header file".to_string()));
        }

        let next_slot_val = u64::from_le_bytes([
            header_buf[0], header_buf[1], header_buf[2], header_buf[3],
            header_buf[4], header_buf[5], header_buf[6], header_buf[7],
        ]);

        let stored_dim = u64::from_le_bytes([
            header_buf[8], header_buf[9], header_buf[10], header_buf[11],
            header_buf[12], header_buf[13], header_buf[14], header_buf[15],
        ]) as usize;

        let m = u64::from_le_bytes([
            header_buf[16], header_buf[17], header_buf[18], header_buf[19],
            header_buf[20], header_buf[21], header_buf[22], header_buf[23],
        ]) as usize;

        let ef_construction = u64::from_le_bytes([
            header_buf[24], header_buf[25], header_buf[26], header_buf[27],
            header_buf[28], header_buf[29], header_buf[30], header_buf[31],
        ]) as usize;

        let ef_search = u64::from_le_bytes([
            header_buf[32], header_buf[33], header_buf[34], header_buf[35],
            header_buf[36], header_buf[37], header_buf[38], header_buf[39],
        ]) as usize;

        let metric_byte = header_buf[40];
        let stored_metric = match metric_byte {
            0 => MetricType::Euclidean,
            1 => MetricType::Cosine,
            2 => MetricType::DotProduct,
            3 => MetricType::Manhattan,
            _ => metric, // fallback to provided
        };

        let _stored_normalize = header_buf[41] != 0;
        let stored_ep = u64::from_le_bytes([
            header_buf[42], header_buf[43], header_buf[44], header_buf[45],
            header_buf[46], header_buf[47], header_buf[48], header_buf[49],
        ]);

        // 验证维度
        if stored_dim != dimension {
            return Err(LingXuError::DimensionMismatch {
                expected: dimension,
                got: stored_dim,
            });
        }

        // 2. 创建 幽影星图
        let max_elements = next_slot_val as usize;
        let mut index = 幽影星图::new(max_elements.max(1024), dimension, m, ef_construction, stored_metric, normalize);
        index.next_slot.store(next_slot_val, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        index.entry_point.store(stored_ep, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        index.ef_search = ef_search;

        // 3. 读取 vectors.bin
        let vectors_path = dir.join("vectors.bin");
        let mut vec_file = fs::File::open(&vectors_path)?;

        for idx in 0..next_slot_val as usize {
            let mut flag_buf = [0u8; 1];
            match vec_file.read_exact(&mut flag_buf) {
                Ok(()) => {}
                Err(_) => break,
            }

            if flag_buf[0] == 1 {
                // 存在：读取向量
                let mut vec = vec![0.0f32; dimension];
                let mut float_buf = [0u8; 4];
                for v in vec.iter_mut() {
                    vec_file.read_exact(&mut float_buf)?;
                    *v = f32::from_le_bytes(float_buf);
                }

                // 读取邻居数据
                let mut u64_buf = [0u8; 8];
                vec_file.read_exact(&mut u64_buf)?;
                let num_levels = u64::from_le_bytes(u64_buf) as usize;

                let mut neighbors: Vec<Vec<usize>> = Vec::with_capacity(num_levels);
                for _ in 0..num_levels {
                    vec_file.read_exact(&mut u64_buf)?;
                    let num_neighbors = u64::from_le_bytes(u64_buf) as usize;
                    let mut level_neighbors: Vec<usize> = Vec::with_capacity(num_neighbors);
                    for _ in 0..num_neighbors {
                        vec_file.read_exact(&mut u64_buf)?;
                        level_neighbors.push(u64::from_le_bytes(u64_buf) as usize);
                    }
                    neighbors.push(level_neighbors);
                }

                let node = Node {
                    vec,
                    neighbors,
                    deleted: false,
                };

                let mut slot_lock = index.nodes[idx].lock();
                *slot_lock = Some(node);
            }
            // flag_buf[0] == 0 表示空槽位，跳过
        }

        // 4. 读取 idmap
        let idmap_path = dir.join("idmap.json");
        let mut idmap_file = fs::File::open(&idmap_path)?;
        let mut idmap_json = String::new();
        idmap_file.read_to_string(&mut idmap_json)?;
        let loaded_map: HashMap<String, u64> = serde_json::from_str(&idmap_json)?;

        // 重建 幽冥倒影
        {
            let id_map = index.id_map.lock();
            let mut max_id = 0u64;
            for (key, val) in loaded_map {
                if val > max_id {
                    max_id = val;
                }
                id_map.get_or_insert(&key);
            }
            // 恢复 counter
            // counter 已在 get_or_insert 中自动递增，需要修正
            let current_counter = id_map.counter.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            if current_counter <= max_id {
                id_map.counter.store(max_id + 1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            }
        }

        Ok(index)
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 辅助方法
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        (self.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst)
            - self.deleted_count.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst)) as usize
    }

    pub fn max_elements(&self) -> usize {
        self.max_elements
    }

    pub fn dimension(&self) -> usize {
        self.dimension
    }

    pub fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        self.distance_raw(a, b)
    }

    /// 获取当前 entry_point
    pub fn entry_point_id(&self) -> Option<u64> {
        let ep = self.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        if ep == u64::MAX {
            None
        } else {
            Some(ep)
        }
    }

    /// 获取内部节点的克隆（用于调试/序列化）
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> HnswSnapshot {
        let id_map = self.id_map.lock();
        let (keys, vals) = id_map.to_serializable();

        let mut nodes = Vec::new();
        let next_slot = self.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) as usize;
        for idx in 0..next_slot {
            let node_lock = self.nodes[idx].lock();
            if let Some(ref node) = *node_lock {
                let flat_neighbors: Vec<u64> = node.neighbors.iter()
                    .flat_map(|v| v.iter().map(|&x| x as u64))
                    .collect();
                nodes.push(HnswNode {
                    nid: idx as u64,
                    neighbors: flat_neighbors,
                    vector: node.vec.clone(),
                });
            }
        }

        HnswSnapshot {
            nodes,
            id_map_keys: keys,
            id_map_vals: vals,
            max_elements: self.max_elements,
            m: self.m,
            ef_construction: self.ef_construction,
            ef_search: self.ef_search,
            dimension: self.dimension,
            metric: self.metric,
            normalize: self.normalize,
        }
    }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §34  ThreadSafe幽影星图 — 幽冥守护 PLRwLock 包装
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// ThreadSafe幽影星图 通过 PLRwLock 包装 幽影星图，提供线程安全的向量索引操作。
/// 所有方法都委托给内部的 幽影星图。
pub struct ThreadSafe幽影星图 {
    inner: PLRwLock<幽影星图>,
}

impl ThreadSafe幽影星图 {
    /// 创建新的 ThreadSafe幽影星图
    pub fn new(
        max_elements: usize,
        m: usize,
        ef_construction: usize,
        ef_search: usize,
        dimension: usize,
        metric: MetricType,
        normalize: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        let mut index = 幽影星图::new(max_elements, dimension, m, ef_construction, metric, normalize);
        index.ef_search = ef_search;
        Self {
            inner: PLRwLock::new(index),
        }
    }

    /// 添加向量（线程安全）
    pub fn add_vector(&self, id: &str, vec: Vec<f32>) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        self.inner.write().add(id, vec)
    }

    /// 移除向量（线程安全）
    pub fn remove_vector(&self, id: &str) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        self.inner.write().delete(id)
    }

    /// 查询最近邻（线程安全）
    pub fn query(&self, vec: &[f32], k: usize) -> Vec<QueryResult> {
        self.inner.read().query(vec, k).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// 持久化索引到磁盘（线程安全）
    pub fn persist(&self, path: &Path) -> LingXuResult<()> {
        self.inner.read().persist(path)
    }

    /// 从磁盘加载索引
    pub fn load(path: &Path, dimension: usize, metric: MetricType, normalize: bool) -> LingXuResult<Self> {
        let index = 幽影星图::load(path, dimension, metric, normalize)?;
        Ok(Self {
            inner: PLRwLock::new(index),
        })
    }

    /// 获取索引中当前有效向量的数量
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.inner.read().len()
    }

    /// 判断索引是否为空
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.len() == 0
    }

    /// 紧凑化索引：重建索引，移除标记删除的节点
    ///
    /// 算法：
    /// 1. 收集所有未删除的 (id, vec) 对
    /// 2. 创建新的 幽影星图
    /// 3. 重新添加所有向量
    /// 4. 替换内部索引
    pub fn compact(&self) {
        let mut index = self.inner.write();

        // 收集所有存活节点
        let next_slot = index.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) as usize;
        let mut alive_entries: Vec<(String, Vec<f32>)> = Vec::with_capacity(next_slot);

        {
            let id_map = index.id_map.lock();
            let compact_map = id_map.compact();
            for (key, val) in compact_map {
                let slot = val as usize;
                if slot < index.nodes.len() {
                    let node_lock = index.nodes[slot].lock();
                    if let Some(ref node) = *node_lock {
                        alive_entries.push((key, node.vec.clone()));
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if alive_entries.is_empty() {
            return;
        }

        // 创建新索引
        let max_elements = alive_entries.len().max(1024);
        let dimension = index.dimension;
        let m = index.m;
        let ef_construction = index.ef_construction;
        let metric = index.metric;
        let normalize = index.normalize;
        let ef_search = index.ef_search;

        let mut new_index = 幽影星图::new(max_elements, dimension, m, ef_construction, metric, normalize);
        new_index.ef_search = ef_search;

        // 重新添加所有向量
        for (id, vec) in alive_entries {
            let _ = new_index.add(&id, vec);
        }

        // 替换
        *index = new_index;
    }

    /// 完全重建图结构
    ///
    /// 与 compact 不同，rebuild 保留了所有原始 ID 和向量，
    /// 但重新构建了整个 HNSW 图拓扑（邻居连接）。
    pub fn rebuild(&self) {
        let index = self.inner.write();

        // 收集所有存活节点（保留原始 slot 位置信息）
        let next_slot = index.next_slot.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) as usize;

        // 1. 清除所有节点的邻居连接，但保留向量数据
        for idx in 0..next_slot {
            let mut node_lock = index.nodes[idx].lock();
            if let Some(ref mut node) = *node_lock {
                node.neighbors.clear();
            }
        }

        // 2. 重置 entry_point
        let old_ep = index.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        index.entry_point.store(u64::MAX, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);

        // 3. 重新逐个添加所有节点（保持原有 slot 分配）
        // 获取 id_map 的快照
        let id_entries: Vec<(String, u64)> = {
            let id_map = index.id_map.lock();
            let compact_map = id_map.compact();
            compact_map.into_iter().collect()
        };

        // 按 num_id 排序，确保按原始顺序重建
        let mut sorted_entries = id_entries;
        sorted_entries.sort_by_key(|(_, num_id)| *num_id);

        for (id_str, num_id) in sorted_entries {
            let slot = num_id as usize;
            if slot >= index.nodes.len() {
                continue;
            }

            let vec = {
                let node_lock = index.nodes[slot].lock();
                match *node_lock {
                    Some(ref node) => node.vec.clone(),
                    None => continue,
                }
            };

            // 如果这个节点是第一个有效节点
            let ep = index.entry_point.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            if ep == u64::MAX {
                // 初始化邻居结构
                let node_level = index.rand_level();
                let mut neighbors: Vec<Vec<usize>> = Vec::with_capacity(node_level + 1);
                for _ in 0..=node_level {
                    neighbors.push(Vec::new());
                }
                {
                    let mut node_lock = index.nodes[slot].lock();
                    if let Some(ref mut node) = *node_lock {
                        node.neighbors = neighbors;
                        node.deleted = false;
                    }
                }
                index.entry_point.store(num_id, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                continue;
            }

            // 获取该节点的层级（如果有已有邻居结构则复用层级数）
            let existing_level = {
                let node_lock = index.nodes[slot].lock();
                node_lock.as_ref().map(|n| n.neighbors.len().saturating_sub(1)).unwrap_or(0)
            };

            let node_level = if existing_level > 0 {
                existing_level
            } else {
                index.rand_level()
            };

            // 确保邻居结构存在
            {
                let mut node_lock = index.nodes[slot].lock();
                if let Some(ref mut node) = *node_lock {
                    while node.neighbors.len() <= node_level {
                        node.neighbors.push(Vec::new());
                    }
                    node.deleted = false;
                }
            }

            // 从顶层到第 0 层逐层搜索 + 连接
            let ep_max_level = {
                let ep_idx = ep as usize;
                let node_lock = index.nodes[ep_idx].lock();
                node_lock.as_ref().map(|n| n.neighbors.len().saturating_sub(1)).unwrap_or(0)
            };

            let mut curr_ep = ep as usize;

            for level in (1..=ep_max_level.max(node_level)).rev() {
                if level > node_level {
                    let res = index.search_layer(&vec, curr_ep, 1, level);
                    if let Some(&(_, best_idx)) = res.first() {
                        curr_ep = best_idx;
                    }
                }
            }

            let start_level = node_level.min(ep_max_level);
            for level in (0..=start_level).rev() {
                let ef = index.ef_construction.max(index.m);
                let neighbors_found = index.search_layer(&vec, curr_ep, ef, level);
                let selected = index.select_neighbors(&neighbors_found, index.m);

                index.set_neighbors(slot, level, selected.clone());

                // 双向连接
                let m_max = index.m * 2;
                for &neighbor_idx in &selected {
                    if neighbor_idx == slot {
                        continue;
                    }

                    let mut neighbor_neighbors = index.get_neighbors(neighbor_idx, level);
                    if !neighbor_neighbors.contains(&slot) {
                        neighbor_neighbors.push(slot);

                        if neighbor_neighbors.len() > m_max {
                            let mut with_dist: Vec<(f32, usize)> = neighbor_neighbors
                                .iter()
                                .map(|&idx| {
                                    let dist = if idx == slot {
                                        index.metric.distance(&vec, &index.get_vec(idx))
                                    } else {
                                        index.node_distance(neighbor_idx, idx)
                                    };
                                    (dist, idx)
                                })
                                .collect();
                            with_dist.sort_by(|a, b| {
                                a.0.partial_cmp(&b.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
                            });
                            neighbor_neighbors = with_dist.into_iter().take(m_max).map(|(_, idx)| idx).collect();
                        }

                        index.set_neighbors(neighbor_idx, level, neighbor_neighbors);
                    }
                }

                if let Some(&(_, best_idx)) = neighbors_found.first() {
                    curr_ep = best_idx;
                }
            }

            if node_level > ep_max_level {
                index.entry_point.store(num_id, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            }
        }

        // 重置删除计数
        index.deleted_count.store(0, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);

        // 如果重建后没有任何节点，恢复旧 entry_point
        let new_len = index.len();
        if new_len == 0 && old_ep != u64::MAX {
            index.entry_point.store(old_ep, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        }
    }

    /// 返回内部 幽影星图 的 snapshot
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> HnswSnapshot {
        self.inner.read().snapshot()
    }

    /// 返回索引的维度
    pub fn dimension(&self) -> usize {
        self.inner.read().dimension()
    }

    /// 返回最大容量
    pub fn max_elements(&self) -> usize {
        self.inner.read().max_elements()
    }

    /// 计算两个原始向量的距离
    pub fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        self.inner.read().distance(a, b)
    }

    /// 设置 ef_search 参数
    pub fn set_ef_search(&self, ef: usize) {
        self.inner.write().ef_search = ef;
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 统一导出与便利函数
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// 创建默认配置的 ThreadSafe幽影星图（Euclidean 距离）
pub fn create_default_hnsw(max_elements: usize, dimension: usize) -> ThreadSafe幽影星图 {
    ThreadSafe幽影星图::new(max_elements, 16, 200, 64, dimension, MetricType::Euclidean, false)
}

/// 创建归一化 Cosine 相似度配置的 ThreadSafe幽影星图
pub fn create_cosine_hnsw(max_elements: usize, dimension: usize) -> ThreadSafe幽影星图 {
    ThreadSafe幽影星图::new(max_elements, 16, 200, 64, dimension, MetricType::Cosine, true)
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 测试模块
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════



// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ── 心象迷宫与认知引擎 ──
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §35 AFFECT MODEL — 奇迹再现-7 Weighted Emotion
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// A 7-weight affective-computing model that drives valence, arousal,
/// dominance, trust, curiosity, surprise and confirmation from a raw
/// input vector.
pub struct 心象模型 {
    pub valence_weights: [f64; 3],
    pub arousal_weights: [f64; 2],
    pub dominance_weights: [f64; 2],
    current: PLMutex<Affect>,
    history: PLMutex<VecDeque<(TimestampMs, Affect)>>,
    history_capacity: usize,
}

impl 心象模型 {
    /// Build a new model from 7 scalar weights.
    ///   weights[0..3] drive valence
    ///   weights[3..5] drive arousal
    ///   weights[5..7] drive dominance
    pub fn new(weights: [f64; 7]) -> Self {
        let vw = [weights[0], weights[1], weights[2]];
        let aw = [weights[3], weights[4]];
        let dw = [weights[5], weights[6]];
        心象模型 {
            valence_weights: vw,
            arousal_weights: aw,
            dominance_weights: dw,
            current: PLMutex::new(Affect::default()),
            history: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            history_capacity: 1000,
        }
    }

    /// Default model using `DEFAULT_AFFECT_WEIGHTS`.
