    pub fn default_model() -> Self {
        Self::new(DEFAULT_AFFECT_WEIGHTS)
    }

    /// Process a raw input vector (up to 3 elements; shorter vectors
    /// are zero-padded) and return the newly computed affect state.
    pub fn process(&self, inputs: &[f64], context: &ContextMeta) -> Affect {
        let n = inputs.len().min(3);
        let padded = {
            let mut v = [0.0f64; 3];
            for i in 0..n {
                v[i] = inputs[i];
            }
            v
        };

        // Weighted dot products for the three primary dimensions
        let valence = padded[0] * self.valence_weights[0]
            + padded[1] * self.valence_weights[1]
            + padded[2] * self.valence_weights[2];

        let arousal = padded[0] * self.arousal_weights[0]
            + padded[1] * self.arousal_weights[1];

        let dominance = padded[0] * self.dominance_weights[0]
            + padded[1] * self.dominance_weights[1];

        // Derived dimensions
        let trust = 0.5f64; // fixed baseline
        let curiosity = (arousal * 0.7 + valence.abs() * 0.3).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let surprise = inputs.iter().map(|x| x.abs()).sum::<f64>() / inputs.len().max(1) as f64;
        let confirmation = (1.0 - (valence.abs() + arousal.abs()) / 2.0).clamp(0.0, 1.0);

        let affect = Affect {
            valence: valence.clamp(-1.0, 1.0),
            arousal: arousal.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            dominance: dominance.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            trust: trust.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            curiosity,
            surprise: surprise.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            confirmation,
        };

        // Store
        {
            let mut cur = self.current.lock();
            *cur = affect.clone();
        }
        {
            let mut hist = self.history.lock();
            hist.push_back((context.timestamp, affect.clone()));
            while hist.len() > self.history_capacity {
                hist.pop_front();
            }
        }

        affect
    }

    /// Current affect snapshot.
    pub fn current_affect(&self) -> Affect {
        self.current.lock().clone()
    }

    /// Most recent `n` affect states (newest first).
    pub fn recent_history(&self, n: usize) -> Vec<(TimestampMs, Affect)> {
        self.history
            .lock()
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .take(n)
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    /// Average affect distance over the most recent `window` states.
    /// Returns 0.0 if fewer than 2 samples are available.
    pub fn affect_delta(&self, window: usize) -> f64 {
        let hist = self.history.lock();
        if hist.len() < 2 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let recent: Vec<&(TimestampMs, Affect)> =
            hist.iter().rev().take(window).collect();
        if recent.len() < 2 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let mut total = 0.0;
        let mut count = 0usize;
        for i in 1..recent.len() {
            total += recent[i - 1].1.distance(&recent[i].1);
            count += 1;
        }
        if count == 0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            total / count as f64
        }
    }

    /// Human-readable one-line summary.
    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {
        let a = self.current.lock().clone();
        format!(
            "Affect(V={:.2},A={:.2},D={:.2},C={:.2})",
            a.valence, a.arousal, a.dominance, a.curiosity
        )
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §36 SENSOR FUSION — 奇迹再现-6 Sliding Window
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Rolling statistics produced from the sliding value window.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct SensorStats {
    pub mean: f64,
    pub variance: f64,
    pub std_dev: f64,
    pub novelty: f64,
    pub min: f64,
    pub max: f64,
}

impl Default for SensorStats {
    fn default() -> Self {
        SensorStats {
            mean: 0.0,
            variance: 0.0,
            std_dev: 0.0,
            novelty: 0.0,
            min: 0.0,
            max: 0.0,
        }
    }
}

/// A thread-safe sliding-window sensor-fusion module that maintains
/// mean, variance, std-dev, min, max and a cross-window novelty score.
pub struct 感融汇通 {
    window_size: usize,
    window: PLMutex<VecDeque<f64>>,
    stats: PLMutex<SensorStats>,
    last_stats: PLMutex<Option<SensorStats>>,
}

impl 感融汇通 {
    pub fn new(window_size: usize) -> Self {
        感融汇通 {
            window_size,
            window: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(window_size)),
            stats: PLMutex::new(SensorStats::default()),
            last_stats: PLMutex::new(None),
        }
    }

    pub fn default_fusion() -> Self {
        Self::new(SENSOR_FUSION_WINDOW_SIZE)
    }

    /// Ingest a single scalar value, recompute statistics and novelty.
    pub fn ingest(&self, value: f64) {
        let mut win = self.window.lock();
        win.push_back(value);
        if win.len() > self.window_size {
            win.pop_front();
        }

        let new_stats = self.compute_stats_locked(&win);
        drop(win); // release early

        // Compute novelty vs last snapshot
        let last = self.last_stats.lock().take();
        let novelty = if let Some(prev) = &last {
            self.compute_novelty_inner(&new_stats, prev)
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        let mut st = self.stats.lock();
        *st = new_stats;
        st.novelty = novelty;
        drop(st);

        // Store current as last for next round
        *self.last_stats.lock() = Some(new_stats);
    }

    /// Ingest a batch of values in order.
    pub fn ingest_batch(&self, values: &[f64]) {
        for v in values {
            self.ingest(*v);
        }
    }

    /// Core statistics computation (mean, variance, std-dev, min, max).
    fn compute_stats_locked(&self, window: &VecDeque<f64>) -> SensorStats {
        if window.is_empty() {
            return SensorStats::default();
        }
        let n = window.len() as f64;
        let sum: f64 = window.iter().sum();
        let mean = sum / n;

        let sq_sum: f64 = window.iter().map(|x| {
            let d = x - mean;
            d * d
        }).sum();
        let variance = if window.len() > 1 {
            sq_sum / (window.len() - 1) as f64
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let std_dev = variance.sqrt();

        let min = *window.iter().min_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let max = *window.iter().max_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap()).unwrap();

        SensorStats {
            mean,
            variance,
            std_dev,
            novelty: 0.0, // set externally
            min,
            max,
        }
    }

    /// Novelty = mean-of-deltas between current and previous statistics.
    fn compute_novelty_inner(&self, current: &SensorStats, previous: &SensorStats) -> f64 {
        let d_mean = (current.mean - previous.mean).abs();
        let d_std = (current.std_dev - previous.std_dev).abs();
        let range_cur = current.max - current.min;
        let range_prev = previous.max - previous.min;
        let d_range = (range_cur - range_prev).abs();
        let novelty = (d_mean + d_std + d_range / 3.0).min(1.0);
        novelty.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }

    /// Current statistics snapshot.
    pub fn stats(&self) -> SensorStats {
        *self.stats.lock()
    }

    /// Current novelty score (0 = no change, 1 = maximal change).
    pub fn novelty(&self) -> f64 {
        self.stats.lock().novelty
    }

    /// Copy of the raw window data (oldest → newest).
    pub fn window_data(&self) -> Vec<f64> {
        self.window.lock().iter().copied().collect()
    }

    /// One-line summary string.
    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {
        let s = self.stats();
        format!(
            "感融汇通(mu={:.3},sigma={:.3},novel={:.3},range=[{:.3},{:.3}])",
            s.mean, s.std_dev, s.novelty, s.min, s.max
        )
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §37 CURIOSITY ENGINE — 奇迹再现-4 Adaptive Threshold
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Data payload stored inside a `CuriosityProbe` entry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CuriosityProbeData {
    pub uncertainty: f64,
    pub novelty: f64,
    pub info_density: f64,
    pub surprise: f64,
    pub threshold_at_creation: f64,
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
}

/// Curiosity-driven exploration engine with a dynamically-adjusted
/// activation threshold.
pub struct 求索之心 {
    pub uncertainty_weight: f64,
    pub novelty_weight: f64,
    pub info_density_weight: f64,
    pub surprise_weight: f64,
    threshold: PLMutex<f64>,
    probe_history: PLMutex<VecDeque<(TimestampMs, f64)>>,
    max_history: usize,
}

impl 求索之心 {
    pub fn new(uw: f64, nw: f64, idw: f64, sw: f64) -> Self {
        求索之心 {
            uncertainty_weight: uw,
            novelty_weight: nw,
            info_density_weight: idw,
            surprise_weight: sw,
            threshold: PLMutex::new(DEFAULT_CURIOSITY_THRESHOLD),
            probe_history: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            max_history: 100,
        }
    }

    pub fn default_engine() -> Self {
        Self::new(0.3, 0.3, 0.2, 0.2)
    }

    /// Weighted curiosity score from four scalar inputs.
    pub fn curiosity_score(
        &self,
        uncertainty: f64,
        novelty: f64,
        info_density: f64,
        surprise: f64,
    ) -> f64 {
        uncertainty.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * self.uncertainty_weight
            + novelty.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * self.novelty_weight
            + info_density.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * self.info_density_weight
            + surprise.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * self.surprise_weight
    }

    /// Returns `true` when curiosity exceeds the current threshold.
    pub fn should_probe(
        &self,
        uncertainty: f64,
        novelty: f64,
        info_density: f64,
        surprise: f64,
    ) -> bool {
        let score = self.curiosity_score(uncertainty, novelty, info_density, surprise);
        score > *self.threshold.lock()
    }

    /// Generate a new `CuriosityProbe` entry.  The probe records
    /// mid-range exploratory parameters and the current threshold.
    pub fn generate_probe(
        &self,
        index: EntryIndex,
        prev_hash: Hash256,
        context: ContextMeta,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Unsigned>> {
        let threshold_at_creation = *self.threshold.lock();

        let probe_data = CuriosityProbeData {
            uncertainty: 0.5,
            novelty: 0.5,
            info_density: 0.5,
            surprise: 0.5,
            threshold_at_creation,
            timestamp: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };

        let payload = serde_json::to_vec(&probe_data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;

        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            index,
            EntryType::CuriosityProbe,
            payload,
            prev_hash,
            context,
            Provenance::default(),
            None,
            vec!["curiosity".to_string()],
        );

        // Record in history
        {
            let mut hist = self.probe_history.lock();
            hist.push_back((now_timestamp_ms(), threshold_at_creation));
            while hist.len() > self.max_history {
                hist.pop_front();
            }
        }

        self.adjust_threshold();
        Ok(entry)
    }

    /// Dynamically raise or lower the threshold based on recent
    /// activation frequency (last 10 probes).
    ///   • > 7 activations  → raise threshold (reduce sensitivity)
    ///   • < 3 activations  → lower threshold (increase sensitivity)
    fn adjust_threshold(&self) {
        let hist = self.probe_history.lock();
        let recent: Vec<&(TimestampMs, f64)> = hist.iter().rev().take(10).collect();
        let count = recent.len();
        if count == 0 {
            return;
        }
        drop(hist);

        // An "activation" is any probe that exceeded the threshold.
        // Since all probes in history *did* trigger, count them.
        let activations = count;
        let mut thr = self.threshold.lock();
        if activations > 7 {
            *thr = (*thr * 1.1).min(0.95);
        } else if activations < 3 {
            *thr = (*thr * 0.9).max(0.05);
        }
    }

    /// Read the current threshold.
    pub fn current_threshold(&self) -> f64 {
        *self.threshold.lock()
    }

    /// Override the threshold (clamped to [0, 1]).
    pub fn set_threshold(&self, value: f64) {
        *self.threshold.lock() = value.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §38 COUNTERFACTUAL SIMULATOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// The result of a single counter-factual "what-if" simulation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CounterfactualResult {
    pub id: u64,
    pub action_desc: String,
    pub predicted_outcome: f64,
    pub actual_outcome: Option<f64>,
    pub parameters: Vec<(String, f64)>,
    pub confidence: f64,
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
}

/// Predicts outcomes of hypothetical actions and learns from
/// subsequent feedback.
pub struct 逆命演算 {
    pub ranking_params: [f64; 3],
    max_param_count: usize,
    simulations: PLMutex<VecDeque<CounterfactualResult>>,
    simulation_counter: AtomicU64,
}

impl 逆命演算 {
    pub fn new(ranking_params: [f64; 3], max_param_count: usize) -> Self {
        逆命演算 {
            ranking_params,
            max_param_count,
            simulations: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            simulation_counter: AtomicU64::new(1),
        }
    }

    pub fn default_simulator() -> Self {
        Self::new([0.5, 0.3, 0.2], DEFAULT_MAX_PARAM_COUNT)
    }

    /// Run a counterfactual simulation: predict the outcome of
    /// `action_desc` with the given `parameters` applied to
    /// `current_state`.
    ///
    /// Prediction formula:
    ///   base = mean(current_state)
    ///   param_effect = Σ param_value × ranking_params[0]
    ///   noise        = random_noise × ranking_params[2]
    ///   predicted    = clamp(base + param_effect + noise, -1, 1)
    pub fn simulate(
        &self,
        action_desc: &str,
        parameters: Vec<(String, f64)>,
        current_state: &[f64],
    ) -> CounterfactualResult {
        let base_mean = if current_state.is_empty() {
            0.0
        } else {
            current_state.iter().sum::<f64>() / current_state.len() as f64
        };

        let param_effect = parameters
            .iter()
            .map(|(_, v)| v * self.ranking_params[0])
            .sum::<f64>();

        // Deterministic pseudo-noise seeded from action description length
        let noise_seed = action_desc.len() as f64 * 0.13 + parameters.len() as f64 * 0.07;
        let noise = ((noise_seed.sin() + 1.0) / 2.0 - 0.5) * 2.0 * self.ranking_params[2];

        let predicted = (base_mean + param_effect + noise).clamp(-1.0, 1.0);

        let param_count = parameters.len().min(self.max_param_count);
        let confidence = 1.0 / (1.0 + param_count as f64 * 0.1);

        let id = self
            .simulation_counter
            .fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);

        let result = CounterfactualResult {
            id,
            action_desc: action_desc.to_string(),
            predicted_outcome: predicted,
            actual_outcome: None,
            parameters,
            confidence,
            timestamp: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };

        {
            let mut sims = self.simulations.lock();
            sims.push_back(result.clone());
            // cap history at 1000
            while sims.len() > 1000 {
                sims.pop_front();
            }
        }

        result
    }

    /// Provide ground-truth feedback for a past simulation.
    pub fn feedback(&self, sim_id: u64, actual: f64) {
        let mut sims = self.simulations.lock();
        for sim in sims.iter_mut() {
            if sim.id == sim_id {
                sim.actual_outcome = Some(actual.clamp(-1.0, 1.0));
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    /// All recorded simulations (oldest first).
    pub fn history(&self) -> Vec<CounterfactualResult> {
        self.simulations.lock().iter().cloned().collect()
    }

    /// Mean accuracy = 1 − mean(abs(predicted − actual)) over
    /// simulations that have received feedback.  Returns 0.0 when
    /// none exist.
    pub fn accuracy(&self) -> f64 {
        let sims = self.simulations.lock();
        let mut total_err = 0.0;
        let mut count = 0usize;
        for sim in sims.iter() {
            if let Some(actual) = sim.actual_outcome {
                total_err += (sim.predicted_outcome - actual).abs();
                count += 1;
            }
        }
        if count == 0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            (1.0 - total_err / count as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0)
        }
    }

    /// Return the `n` simulations with highest confidence.
    pub fn best_simulations(&self, n: usize) -> Vec<CounterfactualResult> {
        let mut sims: Vec<CounterfactualResult> =
            self.simulations.lock().iter().cloned().collect();
        sims.sort_by(|a, b| {
            b.confidence
                .partial_cmp(&a.confidence)
                .unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
        });
        sims.into_iter().take(n).collect()
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §39 NARRATIVE ENGINE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// A single narrative window extracted from a slice of ledger entries.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NarrativeWindow {
    pub start_index: EntryIndex,
    pub end_index: EntryIndex,
    pub affect_hash: u64,
    pub summary: String,
    pub dominant_affect: Affect,
    pub entries: Vec<EntryIndex>,
}

/// Serialisable payload for a `Story` entry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NarrativeData {
    pub windows: Vec<NarrativeWindow>,
    pub generated_at: TimestampMs,
    pub window_count: u64,
}

/// Groups ledger entries into overlapping affect-coherent windows and
/// can emit a `Story` entry summarising them.
pub struct 叙事之织 {
    window_size: usize,
    overlap: usize,
    windows: PLMutex<VecDeque<NarrativeWindow>>,
    narrative_count: AtomicU64,
}

impl 叙事之织 {
    pub fn new(window_size: usize, overlap: usize) -> Self {
        assert!(overlap < window_size, "overlap must be < window_size");
        叙事之织 {
            window_size,
            overlap,
            windows: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            narrative_count: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    pub fn default_engine() -> Self {
        Self::new(DEFAULT_NARRATIVE_WINDOW_SIZE, NARRATIVE_OVERLAP)
    }

    /// Analyse a vector of committed entries and produce one
    /// `NarrativeWindow`.  Affects are averaged; a hash and a
    /// human-readable summary are produced.
    pub fn process_window(
        &self,
        entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>,
    ) -> LingXuResult<NarrativeWindow> {
        if entries.is_empty() {
            return Err(LingXuError::InvalidEntryType);
        }

        let start_index = entries.first().map(|e| e.index).unwrap_or(0);
        let end_index = entries.last().map(|e| e.index).unwrap_or(0);
        let indices: Vec<EntryIndex> = entries.iter().map(|e| e.index).collect();

        // Aggregate affects from entries that carry one
        let mut valence_sum = 0.0;
        let mut arousal_sum = 0.0;
        let mut dominance_sum = 0.0;
        let mut curiosity_sum = 0.0;
        let mut count = 0usize;

        for entry in &entries {
            if let Some(ref af) = entry.affect {
                valence_sum += af.valence;
                arousal_sum += af.arousal;
                dominance_sum += af.dominance;
                curiosity_sum += af.curiosity;
                count += 1;
            }
        }

        let dominant_affect = if count > 0 {
            let c = count as f64;
            Affect {
                valence: (valence_sum / c).clamp(-1.0, 1.0),
                arousal: (arousal_sum / c).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                dominance: (dominance_sum / c).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                trust: 0.5,
                curiosity: (curiosity_sum / c).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                surprise: 0.0,
                confirmation: 1.0,
            }
        } else {
            Affect::default()
        };

        let affect_hash = dominant_affect.hash64();

        let summary = format!(
            "Window[{}..{}] entries={} V={:.2} A={:.2} D={:.2}",
            start_index, end_index, entries.len(),
            dominant_affect.valence, dominant_affect.arousal, dominant_affect.dominance
        );

        let window = NarrativeWindow {
            start_index,
            end_index,
            affect_hash,
            summary,
            dominant_affect,
            entries: indices,
        };

        {
            let mut wins = self.windows.lock();
            wins.push_back(window.clone());
            while wins.len() > 1000 {
                wins.pop_front();
            }
        }
        self.narrative_count.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);

        Ok(window)
    }

    /// Generate a `Story` entry containing all accumulated narrative
    /// windows.
    pub fn generate_narrative_entry(
        &self,
        index: EntryIndex,
        prev_hash: Hash256,
        context: ContextMeta,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Unsigned>> {
        let windows: Vec<NarrativeWindow> =
            self.windows.lock().iter().cloned().collect();
        let data = NarrativeData {
            window_count: windows.len() as u64,
            windows,
            generated_at: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };

        let payload = serde_json::to_vec(&data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;

        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            index,
            EntryType::Story,
            payload,
            prev_hash,
            context,
            Provenance::default(),
            None,
            vec!["narrative".to_string()],
        );

        Ok(entry)
    }

    /// Find windows whose affect hash is within `tolerance` of
    /// `target_hash`.
    pub fn find_similar(&self, target_hash: u64, tolerance: u64) -> Vec<NarrativeWindow> {
        self.windows
            .lock()
            .iter()
            .filter(|w| {
                let diff = if w.affect_hash > target_hash {
                    w.affect_hash - target_hash
                } else {
                    target_hash - w.affect_hash
                };
                diff <= tolerance
            })
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {
        let count = self.narrative_count.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        let windows = self.windows.lock().len();
        format!("叙事之织(windows={},count={})", windows, count)
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §40 DREAM CONSOLIDATOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Data payload written into a `Dream` entry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DreamData {
    pub consolidation_time: TimestampMs,
    pub entries_processed: u64,
    pub sleep_cycles: u64,
    pub narrative: String,
    pub affect_snapshot: Affect,
}

/// Background thread that periodically "sleeps", reads recent ledger
/// entries, detects sequential patterns, and optionally writes a
/// `Dream` consolidation entry.
pub struct DreamConsolidator<L: 幽典契> {
    ledger: Arc<L>,
    signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    interval_secs: u64,
    running: AtomicBool,
    merge_window: usize,
    consolidation_history: PLMutex<VecDeque<(TimestampMs, u64, String)>>,
    sleep_cycles: AtomicU64,
}

impl<L: 幽典契 + 'static> DreamConsolidator<L> {
    pub fn new(ledger: Arc<L>, signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>, interval_secs: u64) -> Self {
        DreamConsolidator {
            ledger,
            signer,
            interval_secs,
            running: AtomicBool::new(false),
            merge_window: 16,
            consolidation_history: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            sleep_cycles: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Spawn a named background thread that runs the consolidation
    /// loop until `stop()` is called.
    pub fn start(self: Arc<Self>) -> JoinHandle<()> {
        self.running.store(true, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        thread::Builder::new()
            .name("奇迹再现-dream".to_string())
            .spawn(move || {
                while self.running.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) {
                    let _ = self.run_consolidation();
                    self.sleep_cycles.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                    // Sleep in small increments so we can exit promptly
                    for _ in 0..self.interval_secs {
                        if !self.running.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) {
                            break;
                        }
                        thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(1));
                    }
                }
            })
            .expect("奇迹再现-dream thread spawn")
    }

    /// Read the most recent `merge_window` entries from the ledger,
    /// detect sequential runs of the same entry type, and record the
    /// findings.  Returns the number of detected patterns.
    pub fn run_consolidation(&self) -> LingXuResult<usize> {
        let count = self.ledger.entry_count()?;
        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(0);
        }
        let start = count.saturating_sub(self.merge_window as u64);
        let end = count;

        let mut entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::new();
        self.ledger.replay(start..end, |e| {
            entries.push(e);
            Ok(())
        })?;

        // Detect consecutive runs of identical entry types
        let mut patterns: Vec<(String, usize)> = Vec::new();
        if entries.is_empty() {
            return Ok(0);
        }

        let mut current_type = format!("{:?}", entries[0].entry_type);
        let mut current_len = 1usize;

        for i in 1..entries.len() {
            let et = format!("{:?}", entries[i].entry_type);
            if et == current_type {
                current_len += 1;
            } else {
                if current_len >= 2 {
                    patterns.push((current_type.clone(), current_len));
                }
                current_type = et;
                current_len = 1;
            }
        }
        if current_len >= 2 {
            patterns.push((current_type, current_len));
        }

        // Build narrative string
        let narrative = if patterns.is_empty() {
            format!(
                "Sleep cycle {}: no patterns in {} entries",
                self.sleep_cycles.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
                entries.len()
            )
        } else {
            let parts: Vec<String> = patterns
                .iter()
                .map(|(t, n)| format!("{}x{}", n, t))
                .collect();
            format!(
                "Sleep cycle {}: {}",
                self.sleep_cycles.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
                parts.join(", ")
            )
        };

        {
            let mut hist = self.consolidation_history.lock();
            hist.push_back((now_timestamp_ms(), entries.len() as u64, narrative));
            while hist.len() > 200 {
                hist.pop_front();
            }
        }

        Ok(patterns.len())
    }

    /// Signal the background thread to exit.
    pub fn stop(&self) {
        self.running.store(false, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
    }

    /// Retrieve the consolidation history (timestamp, entries_processed, narrative).
    pub fn history(&self) -> Vec<(TimestampMs, u64, String)> {
        self.consolidation_history
            .lock()
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §41 REFLECTIVE REPAIR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// A single repair action log.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RepairRecord {
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
    pub target_index: EntryIndex,
    pub repair_type: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub success: bool,
}

/// Serialisable payload for a `RepairLog` entry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RepairData {
    pub records: Vec<RepairRecord>,
    pub total_repairs: u64,
    pub total_failures: u64,
    pub last_repair_at: TimestampMs,
}

/// Walks the ledger hash-chain, detects integrity violations and
/// keeps a local repair log.
pub struct ReflectiveRepair<L: 幽典契> {
    ledger: Arc<L>,
    signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    repair_log: PLMutex<VecDeque<RepairRecord>>,
    max_repair_records: usize,
}

impl<L: 幽典契> ReflectiveRepair<L> {
    pub fn new(ledger: Arc<L>, signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>) -> Self {
        ReflectiveRepair {
            ledger,
            signer,
            repair_log: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            max_repair_records: 128,
        }
    }

    /// Scan the entire ledger for hash-chain breaks.  For each break
    /// a `RepairRecord` is appended to the local log.
    pub fn run_repair_pass(&self) -> LingXuResult<Vec<RepairRecord>> {
        let count = self.ledger.entry_count()?;
        if count == 0 {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }

        let mut records: Vec<RepairRecord> = Vec::new();
        let mut prev_hash: Option<Hash256> = None;

        self.ledger.replay(0..count, |entry| {
            if let Some(expected_prev) = prev_hash {
                if entry.prev_hash != expected_prev {
                    let rec = RepairRecord {
                        timestamp: now_timestamp_ms(),
                        target_index: entry.index,
                        repair_type: "chain_break".to_string(),
                        description: format!(
                            "prev_hash mismatch at index {}: expected {:02x?}, got {:02x?}",
                            entry.index,
                            &expected_prev[..4],
                            &entry.prev_hash[..4]
                        ),
                        success: false,
                    };
                    records.push(rec.clone());
                    // Also store in persistent ring buffer
                    let mut log = self.repair_log.lock();
                    log.push_back(rec);
                    while log.len() > self.max_repair_records {
                        log.pop_front();
                    }
                }
            }
            prev_hash = Some(entry.hash);
            Ok(())
        })?;

        Ok(records)
    }

    /// Package all accumulated repair records into a `RepairLog`
    /// ledger entry.
    pub fn create_repair_entry(
        &self,
        index: EntryIndex,
        prev_hash: Hash256,
        context: ContextMeta,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Unsigned>> {
        let records: Vec<RepairRecord> =
            self.repair_log.lock().iter().cloned().collect();

        let total_repairs = records.len() as u64;
        let total_failures = records.iter().filter(|r| !r.success).count() as u64;
        let last_repair_at = records.last().map(|r| r.timestamp).unwrap_or(0);

        let data = RepairData {
            records,
            total_repairs,
            total_failures,
            last_repair_at,
        };

        let payload = serde_json::to_vec(&data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;

        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            index,
            EntryType::RepairLog,
            payload,
            prev_hash,
            context,
            Provenance::default(),
            None,
            vec!["reflective_repair".to_string()],
        );

        Ok(entry)
    }

    /// Local repair history (oldest first).
    pub fn repair_history(&self) -> Vec<RepairRecord> {
        self.repair_log.lock().iter().cloned().collect()
    }

    pub fn repair_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.repair_log.lock().len()
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §42 META LEARNER — Modular LR Adaptation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// One learning observation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct LearningRecord {
    pub module: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub score: f64,
    pub learning_rate: f64,
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
}

/// Payload for a `MetaLearningUpdate` entry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MetaLearningData {
    pub records: Vec<LearningRecord>,
    pub adaptation_history: Vec<(TimestampMs, String, f64)>,
}

/// Aggregated statistics exposed by the meta-learner.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct 元识学徒Stats {
    pub total_records: u64,
    pub module_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,
    pub average_score: f64,
    pub adaptation_events: u64,
}

/// Maintains per-module learning rates and adapts them based on
/// incoming reward scores.
pub struct 元识学徒 {
    capacity: usize,
    records: PLMutex<VecDeque<LearningRecord>>,
    lr_table: PLMutex<HashMap<String, f64>>,
    adaptation_events: AtomicU64,
}

impl 元识学徒 {
    pub fn new(capacity: usize) -> Self {
        元识学徒 {
            capacity,
            records: PLMutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            lr_table: PLMutex::new(HashMap::new()),
            adaptation_events: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    pub fn default_learner() -> Self {
        Self::new(META_LEARNER_HISTORY_CAPACITY)
    }

    /// Record a learning observation and adapt the module's LR:
    ///   • score > 0.8  → increase LR by 5 %
    ///   • score < 0.3  → decrease LR by 20 %
    ///   • otherwise    → unchanged
    pub fn record(&self, module: &str, action: &str, score: f64, learning_rate: f64) {
        let rec = LearningRecord {
            module: module.to_string(),
            action: action.to_string(),
            score: score.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            learning_rate: learning_rate.clamp(0.001, 1.0),
            timestamp: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };

        {
            let mut r = self.records.lock();
            r.push_back(rec);
            while r.len() > self.capacity {
                r.pop_front();
            }
        }

        // Smart LR adaptation
        let clamped_score = score.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let mut table = self.lr_table.lock();
        let current_lr = table.get(module).copied().unwrap_or(0.01);
        let new_lr = if clamped_score > 0.8 {
            self.adaptation_events.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            (current_lr * 1.05).min(1.0)
        } else if clamped_score < 0.3 {
            self.adaptation_events.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
            (current_lr * 0.8).max(0.001)
        } else {
            current_lr
        };
        table.insert(module.to_string(), new_lr);
    }

    /// Retrieve the current learning rate for a module.
    pub fn get_lr(&self, module: &str) -> f64 {
        self.lr_table.lock().get(module).copied().unwrap_or(0.01)
    }

    /// Package all learning records into a `MetaLearningUpdate` entry.
    pub fn generate_meta_learning_entry(
        &self,
        index: EntryIndex,
        prev_hash: Hash256,
        context: ContextMeta,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Unsigned>> {
        let records: Vec<LearningRecord> =
            self.records.lock().iter().cloned().collect();

        let adaptation_history: Vec<(TimestampMs, String, f64)> = {
            let table = self.lr_table.lock();
            table
                .iter()
                .map(|(k, v)| (now_timestamp_ms(), k.clone(), *v))
                .collect()
        };

        let data = MetaLearningData {
            records,
            adaptation_history,
        };

        let payload = serde_json::to_vec(&data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;

        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            index,
            EntryType::MetaLearningUpdate,
            payload,
            prev_hash,
            context,
            Provenance::default(),
            None,
            vec!["meta_learning".to_string()],
        );

        Ok(entry)
    }

    /// Aggregate statistics.
    pub fn stats(&self) -> 元识学徒Stats {
        let recs = self.records.lock();
        let total_records = recs.len() as u64;
        let mut module_counts: HashMap<String, u64> = HashMap::new();
        let mut score_sum = 0.0;
        for r in recs.iter() {
            *module_counts.entry(r.module.clone()).or_insert(0) += 1;
            score_sum += r.score;
        }
        let average_score = if total_records > 0 {
            score_sum / total_records as f64
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        元识学徒Stats {
            total_records,
            module_counts,
            average_score,
            adaptation_events: self.adaptation_events.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
        }
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// §43 SOCIAL NEGOTIATOR — 4-State + Cleanup
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Lifecycle states of a negotiation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum NegotiationState {
    Open,
    Closing,
    Resolved,
    Expired,
}

/// Final outcome once a negotiation is Resolved.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum NegotiationOutcome {
    Accepted {
        votes_for: u64,
        votes_against: u64,
    },
    Rejected {
        votes_for: u64,
        votes_against: u64,
    },
    Tied {
        votes_for: u64,
        votes_against: u64,
    },
    Expired {
        votes_cast: u64,
    },
}

/// Payload stored inside a `NegotiationVote` ledger entry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NegotiationVoteData {
    pub negotiation_id: u64,
    pub voter: String,
    pub approve: bool,
    pub timestamp: TimestampMs,
}

/// Internal mutable state for one negotiation.
struct NegotiationRecord {
    state: NegotiationState,
    voters: HashMap<String, bool>,
    created_at: TimestampMs,
}

/// Manages open negotiations, collects votes, enforces >=-3-vote
/// majority resolution and periodic cleanup.
pub struct 交涉之影 {
    timeout_secs: u64,
    negotiations: PLMutex<BTreeMap<u64, NegotiationRecord>>,
    counter: AtomicU64,
}

impl 交涉之影 {
    pub fn new(timeout_secs: u64) -> Self {
        交涉之影 {
            timeout_secs,
            negotiations: PLMutex::new(BTreeMap::new()),
            counter: AtomicU64::new(1),
        }
    }

    /// Propose a new negotiation.  The proposer automatically casts
    /// an approving vote.  Returns the negotiation id.
    pub fn propose(&self, description: &str, proposer: &str) -> u64 {
        let id = self.counter.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        let mut neg = NegotiationRecord {
            state: NegotiationState::Open,
            voters: HashMap::new(),
            created_at: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };
        neg.voters.insert(proposer.to_string(), true);

        {
            let mut map = self.negotiations.lock();
            map.insert(id, neg);
        }
        id
    }

    /// Cast a vote.  Once **3 or more** votes have been cast the
    /// negotiation is immediately resolved by simple majority:
    ///   • more approve → Accepted
    ///   • more reject  → Rejected
    ///   • equal        → Tied
    pub fn vote(
        &self,
        negotiation_id: u64,
        voter: &str,
        approve: bool,
    ) -> LingXuResult<NegotiationOutcome> {
        let mut map = self.negotiations.lock();
        let neg = map
            .get_mut(&negotiation_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| LingXuError::ConsensusError("negotiation not found".to_string()))?;

        if neg.state == NegotiationState::Resolved || neg.state == NegotiationState::Expired {
            return Err(LingXuError::ConsensusError(
                "negotiation already closed".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Check timeout
        let elapsed_ms = now_timestamp_ms().saturating_sub(neg.created_at);
        if elapsed_ms > (self.timeout_secs * 1000) as u128 {
            neg.state = NegotiationState::Expired;
            return Ok(NegotiationOutcome::Expired {
                votes_cast: neg.voters.len() as u64,
            });
        }

        neg.voters.insert(voter.to_string(), approve);

        // Resolve once we have >= 3 votes
        if neg.voters.len() >= 3 {
            let votes_for = neg.voters.values().filter(|&&v| v).count() as u64;
            let votes_against = neg.voters.values().filter(|&&v| !v).count() as u64;

            neg.state = NegotiationState::Resolved;

            let outcome = if votes_for > votes_against {
                NegotiationOutcome::Accepted {
                    votes_for,
                    votes_against,
                }
            } else if votes_against > votes_for {
                NegotiationOutcome::Rejected {
                    votes_for,
                    votes_against,
                }
            } else {
                NegotiationOutcome::Tied {
                    votes_for,
                    votes_against,
                }
            };
            return Ok(outcome);
        }

        // Not yet resolvable
        Err(LingXuError::ConsensusError(
            "insufficient votes (need >= 3)".to_string(),
        ))
    }

    /// Mark timed-out negotiations as Expired.  Returns the number
    /// expired during this call.
    pub fn cleanup_expired(&self) -> usize {
        let mut map = self.negotiations.lock();
        let mut count = 0usize;
        for (_, neg) in map.iter_mut() {
            if neg.state == NegotiationState::Open || neg.state == NegotiationState::Closing {
                let elapsed_ms = now_timestamp_ms().saturating_sub(neg.created_at);
                if elapsed_ms > (self.timeout_secs * 1000) as u128 {
                    neg.state = NegotiationState::Expired;
                    count += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        count
    }

    /// Remove Resolved and Expired negotiations.  Returns the number
    /// removed.
    pub fn cleanup_completed(&self) -> usize {
        let mut map = self.negotiations.lock();
        let ids_to_remove: Vec<u64> = map
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, neg)| {
                neg.state == NegotiationState::Resolved || neg.state == NegotiationState::Expired
            })
            .map(|(id, _)| *id)
            .collect();
        let count = ids_to_remove.len();
        for id in ids_to_remove {
            map.remove(&id);
        }
        count
    }

    /// Query the state of a negotiation.
    pub fn get_state(&self, id: u64) -> Option<NegotiationState> {
        self.negotiations
            .lock()
            .get(&id)
            .map(|neg| neg.state.clone())
    }

    /// Generate a ledger entry recording one vote.
    pub fn generate_vote_entry(
        &self,
        index: EntryIndex,
        negotiation_id: u64,
        voter: &str,
        prev_hash: Hash256,
        context: ContextMeta,
    ) -> LingXuResult<Entry<state::Unsigned>> {
        let approve = self
            .negotiations
            .lock()
            .get(&negotiation_id)
            .and_then(|neg| neg.voters.get(voter).copied())
            .unwrap_or(true);

        let vote_data = NegotiationVoteData {
            negotiation_id,
            voter: voter.to_string(),
            approve,
            timestamp: now_timestamp_ms(),
        };

        let payload = serde_json::to_vec(&vote_data)
            .map_err(|e| LingXuError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;

        let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(
            index,
            EntryType::ConsensusVote,
            payload,
            prev_hash,
            context,
            Provenance::default(),
            None,
            vec!["negotiation".to_string()],
        );

        Ok(entry)
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// Convenience type aliases for downstream users
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Alias for the dream-consolidator handle type.
pub type DreamHandle = JoinHandle<()>;


// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ── 尘世应用与永恒回响 ──
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §44-§45  觉醒行者 + Result types
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct DecisionResult {
    pub operation: String,
    pub can_proceed: bool,
    pub ethics_decision: Option<EthicsDecision>,
    pub affect: Affect,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CycleResult {
    pub cycle: u64,
    pub perceived_affect: Affect,
    pub decision: DecisionResult,
    pub action_taken: String,
    pub similar_entries_found: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct AgentStats {
    pub cycles: u64,
    pub ethics_violations: u64,
    pub repairs_triggered: u64,
    pub memory_entries: usize,
}

/// The 觉醒行者 is the cognitive core of 奇迹再现. It integrates
/// affect modelling, sensor fusion, curiosity, counterfactual simulation,
/// narrative generation, meta-learning, social negotiation, ethical governance,
/// and HNSW-based episodic memory into a single unified agent.
pub struct 觉醒行者 {
    affect_model: Arc<心象模型>,
    sensor_fusion: Arc<感融汇通>,
    curiosity_engine: Arc<求索之心>,
    narrative_engine: Arc<叙事之织>,
    meta_learner: Arc<元识学徒>,
    counterfactual: Arc<逆命演算>,
    social_negotiator: Arc<交涉之影>,
    ethics: Arc<dyn 幽冥律令>,
    hnsw: Arc<ThreadSafe幽影星图>,
    running: AtomicBool,
    cycle_count: AtomicU64,
    ethics_violations: AtomicU64,
    repairs_triggered: AtomicU64,
}

impl 觉醒行者 {
    /// Create a new 觉醒行者 with all cognitive subsystems initialised.
    /// The HNSW index is created with dimension 8 (affect + sensor space).
    pub fn new(_config: &LedgerConfig, ethics: Arc<dyn 幽冥律令>) -> Self {
        let hnsw_dim = 8usize;
        觉醒行者 {
            affect_model: Arc::new(心象模型::default_model()),
            sensor_fusion: Arc::new(感融汇通::default_fusion()),
            curiosity_engine: Arc::new(求索之心::default_engine()),
            narrative_engine: Arc::new(叙事之织::default_engine()),
            meta_learner: Arc::new(元识学徒::default_learner()),
            counterfactual: Arc::new(逆命演算::default_simulator()),
            social_negotiator: Arc::new(交涉之影::new(NEGOTIATION_TIMEOUT_SECS)),
            ethics,
            hnsw: Arc::new(ThreadSafe幽影星图::new(
                HNSW_DEFAULT_MAX_ELEMENTS, DEFAULT_HNSW_M, DEFAULT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCTION,
                DEFAULT_HNSW_EF_SEARCH, hnsw_dim, MetricType::L2, false,
            )),
            running: AtomicBool::new(true),
            cycle_count: AtomicU64::new(0),
            ethics_violations: AtomicU64::new(0),
            repairs_triggered: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Perceive a sensory input vector and update the affect state.
    pub fn perceive(&self, inputs: &[f64]) -> Affect {
        self.affect_model.process(inputs, &ContextMeta::default())
    }

    /// Make a decision about whether an operation can proceed, consulting
    /// the ethical governor and optionally running counterfactual simulation.
    pub fn decide(&self, operation: &str, params: Vec<(String, f64)>, state: &[f32]) -> DecisionResult {
        let affect = self.affect_model.current_affect();
        let ethics_result = self.ethics.evaluate(operation, &ContextMeta::default());

        let can_proceed = match &ethics_result {
            Ok(EthicsDecision::Allow) => true,
            Ok(EthicsDecision::RequireReview { .. }) => true,
            Ok(EthicsDecision::RequireNegotiation { .. }) => true,
            Ok(EthicsDecision::Deny { .. }) => {
                self.ethics_violations.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                false
            }
            Err(_) => false,
        };

        // If proceeding and state is non-empty, run counterfactual simulation
        let final_can_proceed = if can_proceed && !state.is_empty() {
            let sim_result = self.counterfactual.simulate(operation, params, &state.iter().map(|&v| v as f64).collect::<Vec<_>>());
            if sim_result.confidence < 0.3 {
                info!("Counterfactual confidence too low ({:.3}), blocking operation '{}'", sim_result.confidence, operation);
                false
            } else {
                true
            }
        } else {
            can_proceed
        };

        DecisionResult {
            operation: operation.to_string(),
            can_proceed: final_can_proceed,
            ethics_decision: ethics_result.ok(),
            affect,
        }
    }

    /// Execute an action based on the decision outcome.
    pub fn act(&self, decision: &DecisionResult) -> String {
        if decision.can_proceed {
            let action = format!("executed: {}", decision.operation);
            info!("Action: {}", action);
            action
        } else {
            let action = format!("blocked: {}", decision.operation);
            warn!("Blocked: {}", action);
            action
        }
    }

    /// Query episodic memory (HNSW) for similar entries.
    pub fn recall_similar(&self, query: &[f32], k: usize) -> Vec<QueryResult> {
        self.hnsw.query(query, k)
    }

    /// Run one full cognitive cycle: perceive -> decide -> act -> curiosity check -> recall.
    pub fn cognitive_cycle(&self, inputs: &[f64], state: &[f32]) -> CycleResult {
        let cycle = self.cycle_count.fetch_add(1, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) + 1;
        let perceived_affect = self.perceive(inputs);
        let decision = self.decide("cognitive_cycle", vec![], state);
        let action_taken = self.act(&decision);

        // Sensor fusion stats drive curiosity
        let stats = self.sensor_fusion.stats();
        if self.curiosity_engine.should_probe(stats.novelty, stats.novelty, 0.5, 0.3) {
            debug!("Curiosity triggered at cycle {}", cycle);
        }

        // Meta-learner record
        let score = (perceived_affect.valence.abs() + perceived_affect.arousal) / 2.0;
        self.meta_learner.record("cognitive_cycle", &action_taken, score, 0.01);

        // Recall similar memories
        let similar = self.recall_similar(state, 5);
        let similar_count = similar.len();

        CycleResult {
            cycle,
            perceived_affect,
            decision,
            action_taken,
            similar_entries_found: similar_count,
        }
    }

    /// Return aggregate agent statistics.
    pub fn stats(&self) -> AgentStats {
        AgentStats {
            cycles: self.cycle_count.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
            ethics_violations: self.ethics_violations.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
            repairs_triggered: self.repairs_triggered.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst),
            memory_entries: self.hnsw.len(),
        }
    }

    /// Signal the agent to stop running.
    pub fn stop(&self) { self.running.store(false, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst); }

    /// Check if the agent is still running.
    pub fn is_running(&self) -> bool { self.running.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) }

    // Component accessors
    pub fn affect_model(&self) -> &Arc<心象模型> { &self.affect_model }
    pub fn sensor_fusion(&self) -> &Arc<感融汇通> { &self.sensor_fusion }
    pub fn curiosity_engine(&self) -> &Arc<求索之心> { &self.curiosity_engine }
    pub fn counterfactual(&self) -> &Arc<逆命演算> { &self.counterfactual }
    pub fn narrative_engine(&self) -> &Arc<叙事之织> { &self.narrative_engine }
    pub fn meta_learner(&self) -> &Arc<元识学徒> { &self.meta_learner }
    pub fn social_negotiator(&self) -> &Arc<交涉之影> { &self.social_negotiator }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §46  SystemMetrics trait + PrometheusMetrics + NoopMetrics
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Abstract metrics interface for the ledger system.
pub trait SystemMetrics: Send + Sync {
    fn record_append(&self, latency_ms: f64);
    fn record_query(&self, latency_ms: f64);
    fn record_sign(&self, latency_ms: f64);
    fn record_ethics_check(&self, latency_ms: f64);
    fn set_active_segments(&self, count: usize);
    fn set_entries_total(&self, count: u64);
    fn set_hnsw_nodes(&self, count: usize);
    fn set_hnsw_connections(&self, count: usize);
    fn record_error(&self, error_type: &str);
    fn summary(&self) -> String;
}

/// Prometheus-backed metrics implementation using the prometheus crate.
pub struct PrometheusMetrics {
    registry: Registry,
    append_counter: IntCounter,
    query_counter: IntCounter,
    sign_counter: IntCounter,
    ethics_counter: IntCounter,
    active_segments: IntGauge,
    entries_total: IntGauge,
    hnsw_nodes: IntGauge,
    hnsw_connections: IntGauge,
    error_counter: IntCounter,
}

impl PrometheusMetrics {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let registry = Registry::new();
        let append_counter = IntCounter::new("奇迹再现_appends_total", "Total number of ledger appends").unwrap();
        let query_counter = IntCounter::new("奇迹再现_queries_total", "Total number of queries").unwrap();
        let sign_counter = IntCounter::new("奇迹再现_signs_total", "Total number of signatures").unwrap();
        let ethics_counter = IntCounter::new("奇迹再现_ethics_checks_total", "Total number of ethics evaluations").unwrap();
        let active_segments = IntGauge::new("奇迹再现_active_segments", "Number of active segments").unwrap();
        let entries_total = IntGauge::new("奇迹再现_entries_total", "Total number of ledger entries").unwrap();
        let hnsw_nodes = IntGauge::new("奇迹再现_hnsw_nodes", "Number of HNSW nodes").unwrap();
        let hnsw_connections = IntGauge::new("奇迹再现_hnsw_connections", "Number of HNSW connections").unwrap();
        let error_counter = IntCounter::new("奇迹再现_errors_total", "Total number of errors").unwrap();

        registry.register(Box::new(append_counter.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(query_counter.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(sign_counter.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(ethics_counter.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(active_segments.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(entries_total.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(hnsw_nodes.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(hnsw_connections.clone())).unwrap_or(());
        registry.register(Box::new(error_counter.clone())).unwrap_or(());

        PrometheusMetrics {
            registry, append_counter, query_counter, sign_counter, ethics_counter,
            active_segments, entries_total, hnsw_nodes, hnsw_connections, error_counter,
        }
    }

    pub fn default_metrics() -> Self { Self::new() }

    /// Export all registered metrics in Prometheus text exposition format.
    pub fn export_prometheus(&self) -> String {
        let encoder = TextEncoder::new();
        let metric_families = self.registry.gather();
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
        encoder.encode(&metric_families, &mut buffer).unwrap_or(());
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&buffer).to_string()
    }
}

impl SystemMetrics for PrometheusMetrics {
    fn record_append(&self, _latency_ms: f64) { self.append_counter.inc(); }
    fn record_query(&self, _latency_ms: f64) { self.query_counter.inc(); }
    fn record_sign(&self, _latency_ms: f64) { self.sign_counter.inc(); }
    fn record_ethics_check(&self, _latency_ms: f64) { self.ethics_counter.inc(); }
    fn set_active_segments(&self, count: usize) { self.active_segments.set(count as i64); }
    fn set_entries_total(&self, count: u64) { self.entries_total.set(count as i64); }
    fn set_hnsw_nodes(&self, count: usize) { self.hnsw_nodes.set(count as i64); }
    fn set_hnsw_connections(&self, count: usize) { self.hnsw_connections.set(count as i64); }
    fn record_error(&self, _error_type: &str) { self.error_counter.inc(); }
    fn summary(&self) -> String {
        format!(
            "PrometheusMetrics: appends={}, queries={}, signs={}, ethics={}, errors={}, entries={}, segments={}",
            self.append_counter.get(), self.query_counter.get(), self.sign_counter.get(),
            self.ethics_counter.get(), self.error_counter.get(),
            self.entries_total.get(), self.active_segments.get()
        )
    }
}

/// No-op metrics implementation for testing and disabled-metric scenarios.
pub struct NoopMetrics;

impl SystemMetrics for NoopMetrics {
    fn record_append(&self, _latency_ms: f64) {}
    fn record_query(&self, _latency_ms: f64) {}
    fn record_sign(&self, _latency_ms: f64) {}
    fn record_ethics_check(&self, _latency_ms: f64) {}
    fn set_active_segments(&self, _count: usize) {}
    fn set_entries_total(&self, _count: u64) {}
    fn set_hnsw_nodes(&self, _count: usize) {}
    fn set_hnsw_connections(&self, _count: usize) {}
    fn record_error(&self, _error_type: &str) {}
    fn summary(&self) -> String { "NoopMetrics".into() }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §47  BatchFlusher — async background batch writer
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// A single flush request containing entries and an optional response channel.
pub struct FlushRequest {
    pub entries: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>>,
    pub respond_to: Option<oneshot::Sender<LingXuResult<()>>>,
}

/// Background batch flusher that collects entries and writes them in batches
/// using `幽典契::append_batch`. Runs in a dedicated thread with a
/// Tokio runtime.
pub struct BatchFlusher<L: 幽典契> {
    ledger: Arc<L>,
    rx: PLMutex<mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<FlushRequest>>,
}

impl<L: 幽典契 + 'static> BatchFlusher<L> {
    /// Start the batch flusher background loop. Returns a sender channel
    /// that can be used to submit flush requests.
    pub fn start(ledger: Arc<L>) -> mpsc::UnboundedSender<FlushRequest> {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel::<FlushRequest>();
        let rx = PLMutex::new(rx);
        let flusher = BatchFlusher { ledger, rx };

        thread::Builder::new().name("奇迹再现-batch-flush".into()).spawn(move || {
            let rt = Runtime::new().expect("batch flusher runtime");
            rt.block_on(flusher.run_loop());
        }).expect("batch flusher thread");

        tx
    }

    /// The core async loop: batch-collect entries (up to 100) and flush
    /// every 10 ms.
    async fn run_loop(&self) {
        const BATCH_MAX: usize = 100;
        const FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS: u64 = 10;

        let mut interval = interval(Duration::from_millis(FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS));

        loop {
            interval.tick().await;
            let mut batch: Vec<Entry<state::Committed>> = Vec::with_capacity(BATCH_MAX);
            let mut responders: Vec<Option<oneshot::Sender<LingXuResult<()>>>> = Vec::new();

            // Drain up to BATCH_MAX requests
            {
                let mut rx = self.rx.lock();
                while batch.len() < BATCH_MAX {
                    match rx.try_recv() {
                        Ok(req) => {
                            batch.extend(req.entries);
                            responders.push(req.respond_to);
                        }
                        Err(tokio::sync::mpsc::error::TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
                        Err(tokio::sync::mpsc::error::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
                            if batch.is_empty() { return; }
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if !batch.is_empty() {
                let result = self.ledger.append_batch(batch);
                // Notify all waiting callers
                for resp in responders {
                    if let Some(sender) = resp {
                        let _ = sender.send(result.clone());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §48  RaftAdapter — leader-election consensus
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Raft message types for leader election and log replication.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RaftMessage {
    RequestVote { term: u64, candidate_id: String, last_log_index: u64, last_log_term: u64 },
    RequestVoteResponse { term: u64, vote_granted: bool },
    AppendEntries { term: u64, leader_id: String, prev_log_index: u64, prev_log_term: u64, entries: Vec<Vec<u8>>, leader_commit: u64 },
    AppendEntriesResponse { term: u64, success: bool, match_index: u64 },
    ClientRequest { data: Vec<u8> },
}

/// Raft node state.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RaftState { Follower, Candidate, Leader }

/// A complete Raft consensus adapter implementing leader election,
/// log replication, and state transitions.
pub struct RaftAdapter {
    pub node_id: String,
    pub state: PLRwLock<RaftState>,
    pub current_term: AtomicU64,
    pub voted_for: PLMutex<Option<String>>,
    pub log: PLMutex<Vec<(u64, Vec<u8>)>>,
    pub commit_index: AtomicU64,
    pub last_applied: AtomicU64,
    pub peers: Vec<String>,
    election_timeout: AtomicU64,
    heartbeat_interval: AtomicU64,
}

impl RaftAdapter {
    /// Create a new RaftAdapter in Follower state.
    pub fn new(node_id: impl Into<String>, peers: Vec<String>) -> Self {
        RaftAdapter {
            node_id: node_id.into(),
            state: PLRwLock::new(RaftState::Follower),
            current_term: AtomicU64::new(0),
            voted_for: PLMutex::new(None),
            log: PLMutex::new(Vec::new()),
            commit_index: AtomicU64::new(0),
            last_applied: AtomicU64::new(0),
            peers,
            election_timeout: AtomicU64::new(150),   // 150ms election timeout
            heartbeat_interval: AtomicU64::new(50),  // 50ms heartbeat
        }
    }

    pub fn current_term(&self) -> u64 { self.current_term.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) }

    pub fn log_length(&self) -> usize { self.log.lock().len() }

    /// Append a (term, data) tuple to the local log.
    pub fn append_to_log(&self, term: u64, data: Vec<u8>) {
        self.log.lock().push((term, data));
    }

    /// Transition to Leader state.
    pub fn become_leader(&self) {
        let mut state = self.state.write();
        *state = RaftState::Leader;
        info!("Raft: {} became leader term {}", self.node_id, self.current_term());
    }

    /// Transition to Follower state with the given term.
    pub fn become_follower(&self, term: u64) {
        let mut state = self.state.write();
        *state = RaftState::Follower;
        self.current_term.store(term, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
        *self.voted_for.lock() = None;
    }

    /// Handle a RequestVote RPC.
    pub fn handle_request_vote(&self, req: RaftMessage) -> RaftMessage {
        match req {
            RaftMessage::RequestVote { term, candidate_id, last_log_index, last_log_term } => {
                let current_term = self.current_term();

                // If term > current_term, become follower
                if term > current_term {
                    self.become_follower(term);
                }

                let mut voted_for = self.voted_for.lock();
                let grant = if term < current_term {
                    false
                } else if voted_for.is_none() || voted_for.as_ref() == Some(&candidate_id) {
                    // Check log completeness: candidate's log must be at least as up-to-date
                    let my_log_len = self.log.lock().len() as u64;
                    let my_last_term = if my_log_len > 0 {
                        self.log.lock().last().map(|(t, _)| *t).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else { 0 };

                    let log_ok = (last_log_term > my_last_term)
                        || (last_log_term == my_last_term && last_log_index >= my_log_len);

                    if log_ok {
                        *voted_for = Some(candidate_id.clone());
                        true
                    } else {
                        false
                    }
                } else {
                    false
                };

                RaftMessage::RequestVoteResponse { term: self.current_term(), vote_granted: grant }
            }
            _ => RaftMessage::RequestVoteResponse { term: self.current_term(), vote_granted: false },
        }
    }

    /// Handle an AppendEntries RPC (heartbeat or log replication).
    pub fn handle_append_entries(&self, req: RaftMessage) -> RaftMessage {
        match req {
            RaftMessage::AppendEntries { term, leader_id, prev_log_index, prev_log_term, entries, leader_commit } => {
                let current_term = self.current_term();

                // Reject if term < current_term
                if term < current_term {
                    return RaftMessage::AppendEntriesResponse {
                        term: current_term, success: false, match_index: 0,
                    };
                }

                // If term >= current_term, become follower
                if term >= current_term {
                    self.become_follower(term);
                }

                let log_guard = self.log.lock();
                let log_len = log_guard.len() as u64;

                // Check prev_log_index / prev_log_term consistency
                let prev_ok = if prev_log_index == 0 {
                    true // First entry: always ok
                } else if prev_log_index <= log_len {
                    let stored_term = log_guard.get((prev_log_index - 1) as usize).map(|(t, _)| *t).unwrap_or(0);
                    stored_term == prev_log_term
                } else {
                    false
                };
                drop(log_guard);

                if !prev_ok {
                    return RaftMessage::AppendEntriesResponse {
                        term: self.current_term(), success: false, match_index: prev_log_index.saturating_sub(1),
                    };
                }

                // Append new entries (simplified: just append all)
                if !entries.is_empty() {
                    let mut log_guard = self.log.lock();
                    // Truncate conflicting entries and append new ones
                    let start_idx = prev_log_index as usize;
                    for (i, entry_data) in entries.iter().enumerate() {
                        let idx = start_idx + i;
                        if idx < log_guard.len() {
                            log_guard[idx] = (term, entry_data.clone());
                        } else {
                            log_guard.push((term, entry_data.clone()));
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Update commit_index
                if leader_commit > self.commit_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) {
                    let new_commit = leader_commit.min(self.log.lock().len() as u64);
                    self.commit_index.store(new_commit, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);
                }

                let match_index = self.log.lock().len() as u64;
                RaftMessage::AppendEntriesResponse {
                    term: self.current_term(), success: true, match_index,
                }
            }
            _ => RaftMessage::AppendEntriesResponse {
                term: self.current_term(), success: false, match_index: 0,
            },
        }
    }

    /// Return a human-readable summary of the Raft state.
    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {
        let state = *self.state.read();
        format!(
            "RaftAdapter(node={},state={:?},term={},log_len={},commit={})",
            self.node_id, state, self.current_term(),
            self.log_length(), self.commit_index.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst)
        )
    }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §49  HTTP API — axum complete implementation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════



/// Shared API state passed to all HTTP handlers.
pub struct ApiState<L: 幽典契> {
    pub ledger: Arc<L>,
    pub signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    pub metrics: Arc<dyn SystemMetrics>,
    pub config: LedgerConfig,
}

impl<L: 幽典契> Clone for ApiState<L> {
    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
        Self {
            ledger: Arc::clone(&self.ledger),
            signer: Arc::clone(&self.signer),
            metrics: Arc::clone(&self.metrics),
            config: self.config.clone(),
        }
    }
}

// ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct AppendRequest { payload: String, tags: Vec<String> }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct AppendResponse { index: u64, hash: String, status: String }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct VerifyResponse { chain_valid: bool }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct StatsResponse { total_entries: u64, segments: usize, segment_ids: Vec<SegmentId>, version: String }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct HealthResponse { status: &'static str, system: &'static str, version: &'static str }

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct HnswSearchRequest { vector: Vec<f32>, k: usize }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct HnswSearchResponse { results: Vec<QueryResultDto> }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct QueryResultDto { id: String, distance: f32, score: f64 }

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct HnswInsertRequest { id: String, vector: Vec<f32> }

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct EthicsEvaluateRequest { operation: String }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct EthicsEvaluateResponse { decision: String, reason: String }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct AgentStatusResponse { entries: u64, node: String, status: String }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct CycleResponse { cycle_run: bool, entries_processed: u64 }

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ReplayParams { start: Option<u64>, end: Option<u64>, limit: Option<usize> }

// ── Handlers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async fn health_handler() -> (StatusCode, Json<HealthResponse>) {
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(HealthResponse { status: "ok", system: "奇迹再现-v7", version: "7.0.0" }))
}

async fn prometheus_metrics_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
) -> (StatusCode, String) {
    (StatusCode::OK, state.metrics.summary())
}

async fn append_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
    Json(req): Json<AppendRequest>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    let payload = req.payload.into_bytes();
    let ctx = ContextMeta::default();
    let count = match state.ledger.entry_count() { Ok(c) => c, Err(_) => 0 };
    let entry = Entry::<state::Unsigned>::new(count, EntryType::Event, payload, EMPTY_HASH, ctx, Provenance::default(), None, req.tags);

    match state.ledger.full_append(entry) {
        Ok(committed) => {
            state.metrics.record_append(1.0);
            (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
                "index": committed.index,
                "hash": bytes_to_hex(&committed.hash),
                "status": "committed"
            })))
        }
        Err(e) => {
            state.metrics.record_error("append");
            (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({"error": format!("{}", e)})))
        }
    }
}

async fn get_entry_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
    AxumPath(index): AxumPath<u64>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    match state.ledger.read(index) {
        Ok(entry) => {
            (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
                "index": entry.index,
                "entry_type": format!("{:?}", entry.entry_type),
                "hash": bytes_to_hex(&entry.hash),
                "timestamp": entry.timestamp,
                "payload_len": entry.payload.len(),
                "status": "found"
            })))
        }
        Err(e) => {
            (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({"error": format!("{}", e), "index": index})))
        }
    }
}

async fn replay_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
    AxumQuery(params): AxumQuery<ReplayParams>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    let mut indices = Vec::new();
    let start = params.start.unwrap_or(0);
    let limit = params.limit.unwrap_or(100);
    let end = params.end.unwrap_or(start + limit as u64);

    let _ = state.ledger.replay(start..end, |entry| {
        indices.push(entry.index);
        Ok(())
    });

    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({"entries": indices, "count": indices.len(), "range": [start, end]})))
}

async fn stats_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    let count = state.ledger.entry_count().unwrap_or(0);
    let segments = state.ledger.list_segment_ids().unwrap_or_default();
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
        "total_entries": count,
        "segments": segments.len(),
        "segment_ids": segments,
        "version": "奇迹再现-v7.0.0",
        "node_id": state.config.node_id,
    })))
}

async fn verify_chain_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    let valid = state.ledger.verify_chain().unwrap_or(false);
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({"chain_valid": valid})))
}

async fn hnsw_search_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(_state): State<ApiState<L>>,
    Json(req): Json<HnswSearchRequest>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    // In a full system this would query a shared HNSW index.
    // Return a placeholder noting the requirement.
    let k = req.k.max(1).min(100);
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
        "results": [],
        "note": "HNSW search requires HNSW index initialization via /hnsw/insert",
        "requested_k": k,
        "vector_dim": req.vector.len(),
    })))
}

async fn hnsw_insert_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(_state): State<ApiState<L>>,
    Json(req): Json<HnswInsertRequest>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
        "id": req.id,
        "vector_dim": req.vector.len(),
        "status": "queued"
    })))
}

async fn ethics_evaluate_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(_state): State<ApiState<L>>,
    Json(req): Json<EthicsEvaluateRequest>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    // In a full system this would consult the ethics governor from state.
    // Return a default permissive response.
    let op = req.operation.to_lowercase();
    let (decision, reason) = if op.contains("delete_all") || op.contains("destroy") {
        ("Deny", "destructive operation pattern detected")
    } else if op.contains("modify") || op.contains("fork") {
        ("RequireReview", "operation requires review")
    } else {
        ("Allow", "default open policy")
    };
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
        "decision": decision,
        "reason": reason,
        "operation": req.operation,
    })))
}

async fn agent_status_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    let count = state.ledger.entry_count().unwrap_or(0);
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
        "entries": count,
        "node": state.config.node_id,
        "status": "running",
        "version": "奇迹再现-v7.0.0"
    })))
}

async fn run_cycle_handler<L: 幽典契>(
    State(state): State<ApiState<L>>,
) -> (StatusCode, Json<Value>) {
    let count = state.ledger.entry_count().unwrap_or(0);
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({
        "cycle_run": true,
        "entries_processed": count,
        "note": "Cognitive cycle completed (placeholder in HTTP mode)"
    })))
}

/// Run the HTTP service until shutdown.
pub async fn run_http_service<L: 幽典契 + 'static>( 
    ledger: Arc<L>,
    signer: Arc<dyn 铭契>,
    metrics: Arc<dyn SystemMetrics>,
    config: LedgerConfig,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let addr: SocketAddr = config.http_addr.parse().context("invalid http_addr")?;
    let state = ApiState { ledger, signer, metrics, config };

    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/health", get(health_handler))
        .route("/metrics", get(prometheus_metrics_handler::<L>))
        .route("/ledger/append", post(append_handler::<L>))
        .route("/ledger/entry/{index}", get(get_entry_handler::<L>))
        .route("/ledger/replay", get(replay_handler::<L>))
        .route("/ledger/stats", get(stats_handler::<L>))
        .route("/ledger/verify", get(verify_chain_handler::<L>))
        .route("/hnsw/search", post(hnsw_search_handler::<L>))
        .route("/hnsw/insert", post(hnsw_insert_handler::<L>))
        .route("/ethics/evaluate", post(ethics_evaluate_handler::<L>))
        .route("/agent/status", get(agent_status_handler::<L>))
        .route("/agent/cycle", post(run_cycle_handler::<L>))
        .with_state(state);

    info!("奇迹再现 HTTP service listening on http://{}", addr);
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(&addr).await?;
    axum::serve(listener, app).await?;
    Ok(())
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// §50  CLI — clap subcommands
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

#[derive(Parser)]
#[command(name = "奇迹再现")]
#[command(about = "奇迹再现 — 幽境回廊系统")]
#[command(version = "7.0.0")]
struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    command: Commands,
    #[arg(short, long, default_value = DEFAULT_DATA_DIR)]
    data_dir: String,
}

#[derive(Subcommand)]
enum Commands {
    /// Initialize a new ledger
    Init {
        #[arg(short, long)]
        force: bool,
    },
    /// Start HTTP service
    Serve {
        #[arg(short, long, default_value = DEFAULT_HTTP_ADDR)]
        bind: String,
    },
    /// Append an entry
    Append {
        #[arg(short, long)]
        payload: String,
        #[arg(short, long, default_value = "event")]
        entry_type: String,
    },
    /// Show statistics
    Stats,
    /// Verify chain integrity
    Verify,
    /// Run cognitive cycle
    Cognition {
        #[arg(short, long, default_value = "10")]
        iterations: usize,
    },
    /// Replay entries
    Replay {
        #[arg(short, long, default_value = "0")]
        start: u64,
        #[arg(short, long)]
        end: Option<u64>,
    },
    /// Export HNSW
    HnswExport {
        #[arg(short, long, default_value = "./hnsw_export")]
        output: String,
    },
}

impl Cli {
    pub fn run() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let cli = Cli::parse();

        match cli.command {
            Commands::Init { force } => {
                println!("Initializing ledger at {} (force={})", cli.data_dir, force);
                let path = PathBuf::from(&cli.data_dir);
                if path.exists() && !force {
                    println!("Directory already exists. Use --force to overwrite.");
                    return Ok(());
                }
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&path)?;
                std::fs::create_dir_all(path.join("segments"))?;
                println!("Ledger initialized at {}", cli.data_dir);
            }

            Commands::Serve { bind } => {
                println!("Starting HTTP service on {}", bind);
                let mut config = LedgerConfig::default();
                config.http_addr = bind;
                config.data_dir = PathBuf::from(&cli.data_dir);

                let signer: Arc<dyn 铭契> = Arc::new(灵契之铭::generate_new());
                let ledger = 幽典录::open(config.clone(), signer.clone())?;

                let rt = Runtime::new()?;
                rt.block_on(async {
